[07/12 07:50] Sadidin Baba: 🌐 _*🇭ιĸιмaн 🇼rιтer'ѕ 🇦ѕѕocιaтιon*_
   🌐( _*Kυngιya Daya Taмĸar Dυвυ*_)
Hikmahwritersassociation2019@gmail.com

                           

loadιng...........✍🏻

 
  🌐 IZZAR MULKI
           💓💓💓💓

Story 
        &
          Writing by
UMMU MUFEED
      (Mrs Aliyu)




Alhamdulillah Ina godiya ga Allah me kowa me komai da yasa na Kammala littafin *INA DANGINA* Lafiya Ubangiji nake roko yasa a tsinci darasin da ke ciki, saboda ba wai muna son ku karanta Labarin mu bane kuji dadi da nishadi, muna son a hada da darasin dake ciki,  sannan kuma gashi zan fara *IZZAR MULKI* Ina rokon sa yadda zan fara shi yanzu, Allah yasa mu gama lafiya, Ubangijin nake sake roko yasa yasa a amfana lafiya,




*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 1⃣


"Nasrin! Nasrin!! Nasrin!!! Zoki sayo min siga in sha kunun nan"


Yarinya ce da bata wuce shekara goma ba ta tako a hankali daga ganin fuskarta zaka gane miskilanci, gidan da suke ginin ma ya rushe amma yadda yarinyar take taku kai kace 'yar gidan wani attajirin ne. Tana zuwa tace a hankali "Umma Kina ta kirana ina amsa wa amma sai sake kira kike"


Umman tace "To Nasrin irin amsawarki ce ba lalle in jiki ba"

Tace "To bani kudin sigarin in siyo miki ni bana son kina daga muryan ki sosaine"


Ta mika mata Nasrin ta fita cikin takunta na kasaita


Tana fita wata yarinya ta taho da gudu ta take mata fada, amma yarinyar ko juyowa batayi ba, balle ta bata hakuri, Nasrin ta kulu iya kuluwa sai tabi yarinyar har zata shiga gida Nasrin ta kamota


Huci take tana kallon ta ta nuna ta da yatsa tace "Ke kinsan ni wacece ni zaki taka kuma na miki magana ko ki bani hakuri "


Yarinyar tana huci tace cikin in'ina "Yaki ake yi yan garin gogar sun zo zasu yaki Sarkin garin mu"



Da Sauri Nasrin ta sake ta ta nufi gidansu da gudunta saidai kafin kace me gari ya turnike da kura da kyar Nasrin ta shiga gida ta samu Ummanta na kokarin fitowa ta rumgume tace "Allah na gode maka da ka kawo min 'yata gida Saura Malam"


Bata rufe baki ba saigashi ya shigo yace "Hauwa garin nan fa yau sai dai Addu'a ku shiga daki "

Suna shiga ya fita, 


Yakin Bazata aka shigo Babban birnin Samina, wanda irin wannan yakin ne yake sa aci gari, 

Haka ta kasance da garin aka cisu da yaki inda aka tattara musu ganimar dukiya sannan suka maida talakawan garin bayinsu suna tattarasu suna tafiya dasu,


Nasrin da Ummanta suna daki Malam Iliya yazo yace "Hauwa sun ci mu a yaki ga sarkin mu can yana kuka, yaga ana tafiya da muta..."



Ji yayi an turo kofar dukansu suka mike, wasu mutane da kayan yakin a jikin su, Malam suka fara dauka suka daure masa hannu suka hada da wani icen. 


Ran Nasrin ya baci ganin abin da aka yiwa Malam da sauri ta matso kusa dasu tace "Kai waye da zaka yiwa Mahaifina haka kuma uban waye ya turo ka"


Da sauri ya wanka mata mari saida ta fadi kasa. Zafin marin be hana ta tashi ba tana mikewa tana huci zata kara matsawa Umma ta riko ta, mutumin yace "badan ke yarinya ce ba dana kashe ki, amma kash sarki bai bamu daman kashe yara ba "



Nasrin tace "Ni ay akan wannan abin da kayi gwara ka kashe ni"



Ya fara takowa inda take da sauri Umma ta rumgume ta tace "yi hakuri yarinyace ba hankali"


Cikin daga murya tace "Ni da hankalina "


Mutumin be ce komaiba ya kama hannun Umma itama ya hada da katon icen ya daure, ya kama na Nasrin ma ta gantsara masa cizo, ya sake bata wani marin ta nufi icen da suke girki dashi ta dauko ta nufe shi dashi Ummanta ta daka mata tsawa tace "Keeee kiyi abin dayace ba yadda da muka iya tunda anci Sarkin mu da yaki "



Yarda icen tayi dan bata son fushin mahaifiyarta ta matso ta mika masa hannun ya daure da icen, ya zama Malam a gaba Ummanta a tsakiya sai ita a baya. Hawaye yake zuba mata wanda da gani kasan na bacin raine


Fito dasu yayi lokacin har an fito da mutanen garin wanda Sarki kuwa saboda kuka har saida Hawan jininsa ya tashi,

Duk talakawan da aka fito dasu mazansu da matansu kuka suke amma banda Nasrin wanda Hawayene ke zuba cikin bacin rai, Wajen sarkin su da yake kan dokinsa aka yi da su, saida ya kalle su yace "Ku tafi dasu ni yanzu zanyi gaba sai kun karaso"


Yana gama fada ya tafi da mutanen sa.


Su Nasrin kuwa Daji akayi dasu ana tafiya har dare yayi ganin duhu ya shiga Malam yace "Bayin Allah ku tsaya muyi Sallah,"

Wani ne ya daka masa tsawa "kai Malam kowa yayi shiru kaine me bakin magana, To Idan ka sake magana zan kashe ka"


Da sauri Nasrin ta dago jajayen idonta ta kalleshi cikin duhu take ranta ya kara baci.


Saida dare ya raba suka tsaya suka kunce su daga jikin icen amma hannayensu yana daure, Malam yace "Dan Allah ku kunce mu za muyi Sallah "


Wani daga cikin su ne ya zuba masa doruna a gadon bayansa suka kama tasu Sallar wanda dungure ce kawai ba Sallah ba,

 da yaga haka sai yayi dabara a haka yayi taimama yayi Sallah sauran ma sukayi yadda yayi

Haka kowa yayi barci amma banda Nasrin da Ummanta da Malam, Nasrin tace "Umma Ina zasu kaimu"


Tace "ban sani ba amma abin dana sani mun zama bayin su"


Nasrin tace "Mu bamu da bakin magana kenan"



Umma tace "sun Riga sunci garin mu da yaki bamu da karfi yanzu"


Nasrin zatayi magana ta daga mata hannu tace "ya isah ki kwanta ki barci"

Ba musu ta kwanta haka sukayi barci cikin yunwa



Da asuba Malam ne ya fara tashi, ko da yaga Alfijir ya fito ya tashi Nasrin da Ummanta yace "Suyi Sallah "


Sauram ma suka tashi suka yi, bayan su idar ne suka ci gama da tafiya. Yunwa ta galabaita su ga kishir ruwa, duk bakin su yayi fari su kuwa suna tafiya suna cin yayan itace, Malam ya kalli Nasrin wani tausayinta ne ya kama shi ya kalli daya daga cikin su yace "Dan Allah ka taimaka ka sam wa yaran cikin mu abin da zasu ci ko ka since mu mu samo masa "wai wawan naushi yakaiwa Malam a kirjinsa kafin kace me malam ya fadi suma Nasrin da Ummanta faduwa sukayi saboda rinjayarsu da yayi. 


Ci gaba yayi da dukansa da igiyar daurin shanu yana cewa "ba nace ka daina magana ba"

Malam kuwa Kalmar Shahada ya furta a bakinsa daga nan itace kalmar karshe rai yayi halinsa, 


Shiko me dukan yana ganin haka yace "Ka mutu a banza a wofi"

Nasrin da Ummanta kuka suka fasa suna so su fada kansa amma sun kasa saboda an daure hannunsu sosai, 

Wani ne yace "zamu kunce ku kuyi masa sitira anan ku binne shi, tunda ga kauye kusa damu zanje in samo muku likkafani



Koda aka kunce su Umma ce ta masa wanka tana kuka, ta nade shi da likkafanin da aka kawo musu suka masa sallah a gurin aka binne shi, 

Saida suka basu danyen mangoro da ruwan rafi sannan suka ci gaba da tafiya, wanda Nasrin da Ummanta kan basu iya ci ba,

Tafiya suke ba ci ba tsayawa ba hutu har yamma tayi, Sun iso wani lambu suka samu wata gimbiya a bakin lambun, tana hango Nasrin tace "Ku tsaya"


Suka tsaya tace "Ni sunana Gimbiya Sumayya yar Sarki Abdulbasid, Ina son waccer baiwar nawa zaku siyar min"

Tana magana tana nuna Nasrin


Daya daga cikin su yace "Ki bada sile biyar"


Tace "Wannan kyakkyawar yarinyar a sile biyar, na kara sile biyar, zan bada goma kenan"


A tare suka hada baki suka ce "mun amince ran Gimbiya ya dade"


Ta biya su, suna kokarin kunce ta tace "To Ummana fa"


Gimbiya tace "Ke kadai nake so yan mata"


Nasrin tayi mata mungun kallo 

Gimbiya kuma ta sake mata murmushi, daidai lokacin aka kawo ta gabanta, Nasrin da Ummanta suka fasa kuka a tare, Ummanta da taga da gaske suke zasu rabata da diyar ta tace "Nasrin ki rike mutumcin ki, ban yadda ki yada mutumcin ki ba duk halin da kika shiga"


Nasrin ta kuma fashewa da kuka ganin anci gaba da tafiya da Ummanta ita kuma an rike ta, tana ganin su suka kule,


Saida sukayi nisa Gimbiya Sumayya ta dokinta ta daurewa Nasrin hannu dokin na jan ita kuma tana kan dokin....

Page 3⃣



mika mata dorina irin wanda ake dukan doki yayi amma Nasrin ko a jikinta kuma cikin zuciyar ta ma bata tsoron, gani ba tsoro a fuskarta yasa ta ajiye dorunan tace "ya sunan ki"


Cikin isa Nasrin tace "Sunana Nasrin"


Gimbiya Sumayya tace "yar waye a garin Samina"


Tace "Ni yar malam ilyasu ne"


"Waye shi"


Nasrin tace "Malami"

Gimbiya Sumayya tace "To abin da nake son fada min nan da kike gani kinzo ne a matsayin baiwa kuma duk abin da nake so shi zaki min, kin jini ko?"

Shiru tayi tana kallon gefe


Gimbiya tace "baki jiba"


Tace "Naji mana "

Saida Gimbiya tabar wajen ta dawo tana mamakin kanta wai itace tayi haka, kuma a gimbiya,

Tana zaune Gimbiya ta aiko kiranta koda taje cewa tayi "zanyi barci kuma yau dake nake son nayi filo"


Nasrin tace "Baki da filo ne"


Gimbiya ta bata rai tace "ke idan ina magana fa ba'a amsa min"


Shiru tayi tana tsaye tace "Dalla zo ki kwanta anan ni na samu na kwanta barci zanyi"

Nasrin tace "To ni a ina zan kwanta"

Ta nuna mata da hannunta


Nasrin ta kwanta tayi yadda tace din

Gimbiya kuwa kanta ta dora a ruwan cikin ta, can Nasrin tace "Nifa kin min nawi"


Da sauri Gimbiya Sumayya ta mike yace "ke dan Ubanki ni kike cewa na miki nawi"

Nasrin tace "Nidai ba Ubana ba wlh "

Mari ta Gimbiya ta saukar mata tace "kaji min Shegiyar yarinya ni kike cewa ba ubanki ba"


Nasrin huci ta fara yi kamar zata rama sai kuma ta tuna a gidan su take kuma gidan sarauta ko ba komai ta girmeta, saidai bata san me yasa ita din ma take mata rashin kunya ba, yakamata ta mata biyayya tunda ita kuyanga ce

Cikin bacin rai ta koma ta kwanta Gimbiya ta dora kanta a ruwan cikinta, ba'a dade ba barci ya kwashe ta, ita ko bata iya yin barci ba tana tunanin Ummanta da Malam, Hawaye ne ya zubo mata tana karanto Addu'a da kyar barci ya kwashe ta,

Da asuba kuwa tana son ta tashi tayi Sallah amma tana jin tsoro kada tayi laifi, can kuma ta tuna to tsoron wa yakamata taji tsakanin Gimbiya da Allah, 

Cikin ranta tace "Allah"


Yunkurin tashi tayi Gimbiya ta bude Ido Nasrin tace "Gimbiya lokacin Sallah yayi shine zan tashe ki kiyi Sallah"


Gimbiya tace "Ke wa yace miki ana tashina "


Tace "To ni Sallah zanyi"


Gimbiya bata ce komaiba ita kuwa ta tashi tayi sallah a wajen ta koma ta kwanta 

Gimbiya kuwa bata tashi ba sai karfe goma lokacin yunwa ta damu Nasrin dan su saba karyawa karfe sassafe, tana tashi tayi mika ko salati babu tace "kije ki kira min kuyagin wanka sumin wanka"


Tashi Nasrin tayi ta fita tana fita taga Basma koda ta ganta da kayan Sarauta tasan 'yar gidan ce saboda haka bata mata magana ba, kada taje ita ma halinsu daya da Gimbiya



Itako Basma tana ganinta taji ta kwanta mata gashi shekarunsu zaizo daya da sauri taje ta tare tace "wa kike nima"


Nasrin tace "yawwa dama gimbiya ce tace In kira mata kuyangin wanka kuma bansan inda zan same suba"

Basma tace "muje in raka ki gunsu"


Suka jera suna tafiya Basma tace "Amma ke ba anan kike ba ko"

Nasrin tace "jiya Gimbiya ta siye ni"


Basma tace "kina nufin kice ke baiwa ce gidan nan "

Nasrin tayi irin Murmushinta na kasaita tace "eh ni baiwa ce "


Basma tace "Zansa Mai martaba ya yanta ki baki dace da baiwa ba"



Daidai lokacin suka iso gurin suna zuwa gun kowa ya durkusa ya fara gaida Basma ita ko cewa take "Dan Allah ku tashi"

Bayan sun tashi tace "Gimbiya tana niman kuyangin wanka kuje ku mata wanka"


Suka tafi Basma da Nasrin ma suka juya Basma tace "ki tafi gunta dan nasan Halin gimbiya idan bata ganki ba kin shiga uku Anjima zamu hadu"

Nasrin ta juya tana jin dadin haduwa da Basma


Kuyangin suna zuwa suka fara cire mata kaya Nasrin kan kauda kai tayi dan ganin yadda aka tubeta haihuwar uwarta a gabanta kuma,


Kama ta sukayi sukayi bayi da ita suka mata wanka bayan sun gama mata suka dauko ta aka kawota tsakiyar gado suka shafa mata mai suka yi mata kwalliya, suka mata shiri irin na alfarma,

Bayan sun gama aka kawo mata kayan karinta a kan gadon aka ajiye mata ta kira Nasrin tace "ki sunkuya"

Ta sunkuya, ita kuma ta dora kafarta a gadon bayanta tana cin abincinta hankalinta kwance,

Nasrin kuwa yunwa ta matsa mata ga bayanta ya gaji hawaye ne ya fara zuba nan da nan kuma ranta ya baci, zuciyarta ta fara kuna, yunkurawa tayi ta tashi har saida Abincin ya zube, Kallota Gimbiya tayi itama kallonta take cikin kunar rai


Gimbiya tace "Kinsha giyar wake ne"


Nasrin tace "ke zan tambaya ko kinsha giya ne, da zaki min wannan wulakanci kuma baki bani abinci naci ba , an gaya miki..."


Maganan ne ta makale sakamakon marin da Gimbiya ta mata ta jawota ta fito da ita saida ta kaita wajen sarkin duka wurgata tace "ka ladabtar min da ita kuma ka kira sarkin gishiri tare za kuyi aikin"

Tana gama fada tabar gurin, Sarkin duka kuwa dukan Nasrin yake iya karfinta tun tana kuka har ta kasa yin kuka, koda Sarkin gishiri yazo ya samu an fasa mata jiki da duka shi kuma ba imani ya debo gishirin ya zuba mata a gun daya fashen Nasrin ta sake wani Ihu me ban tausayi can kuma ta Suma, da Sarkin duka ya fahimci haka, ruwa ya debo ya watsa mata, tuni ta farfado ya ci gaba da dukanta Sarkin gishiri yana watsa gishiri.


Basma kuwa dama tayi Alkawari zata hadu da Nasrin koda taje Dakin Gimbiya ta tsuguna gefe tace "Yaya dama akwai wata kuyangarki ce da kika kawo jiya ita nake nima"


Tsawa ta daka mata tace "Idan na sake ganin ki dakina saina yanka ki shegiya me ruwan mayu"

Fita Basma tayi tana tambayan bayin gidan amma kowa yace "baima santa ba"

Zata wuce ta wajen hukuncine sai tajiyo ihu tace "To yau kuma wa yayi laifi"


Ta leka ay kuwa taga Nasrin ce da gudu ta fada gurin taje ta rungumota ta daka musu tsawa "wa yace ku mata haka"

Sarkin duka yace "Ranki ya dade Gimbiya ta samu"

Basma tace "Amma bakuda imani wlh "

Fita tayi taga wani bawa tace "Dan Allah zo ka dauketa ka kaimin ita wajen Ummina"

Yazo ya dauki Nasrin a kafada wacce nishi take tayi gama yana binta a baya har suka je part din Ummin nata, a falo ya ajiyeta, ita kuma ta shiga ta samu Ummi ta kishin gida tace "Ummi zo kiga Rashin imanin da ake a gidan nan "


Ummi ta fito taga Nasrin a yashe tace "Innalillahi Basma Ina kika samo wannan"


Tace "Ummi itace baiwar da nake fada miki fa"


"Waya mata haka"
Ummi ta tambaya 

Basma ta fada mata abin da ta sani Ummi daga ta tayi Tace "Ni dai bansan me yasa ba ina tausayawa bayi, nida Me martaba zaiji tawa daya yanta duk bayin gidan nan"


Bayi ta kaita ta sa ruwan dumi ta mata wanka ta shafa mata montulator ta bata tea me zafi tasha, bayan ta nutsu Ummi tace "Baiwar Allah ya sunan ki"

Cikin kasaita tace "Sunana Nasrin "

Ummi tace "Suna me dadi"


Bani labarinki

Cikin kasaita Nasrin ta fada musu komai 


Gimbiya Sumayya kuwa ta tashi dan ta bubo Nasrin dan yanzu tasan sun lisata tanazuwa ta samu ba ita koda ta tambaue suka fada mata komai, Da sauri ta yi part din Ummi Kuyangin ta na binta a baya ko Sallama babu ta shigo tana zuwa ta bawa Basma mari ta finciko hannun Nasrin, Ummi kuwa batayi mamaki ba dama saida tayi tunanin haka zata faru. Bata ce komai ba har ta fita da ita,


Basma kuwa fita tayi tayi part din me martaba da gudu bayanan dan haka tayi Fada, tana zuwa ta fada jikinsa tana kuka har yanzu hannunta na kuncinta, yan Fadan kuwa cewa suke "Allah ya huci zuciyar Gimbiya Basma"

Sarki ya shafi fuskarta yadda yaga yatsu ya fito yace "wani dan kasadan ne haka"


Basma ta fada masa komai ta kara dacewa "kuma Ina son ka 'yanta Nasrin yau"


Sarki yace "To jeki ciki idan na shigo mayi magana"

Page 2⃣


WACECE GIMBIYA SUMAYYA

Sarki Abdulbasid shine ke sarautan garin Gauda wanda shima ya gada ne a gurin Iyaye da Kakannu, Saida Yana da bukatar haihuwa Allah bai bashi ba Matansa Hudu yana da k'wark'warori guda biyu, cikin k'wark'warorin ne daya daga ciki ta haifi Sumayya tun tana jaririya Sumayya ta tashi a sangarce, Mahaifiyarta ta nuna mata, tayi abin da take so ita 'yar Sarkine, kuma itace Gimbiya. Shekaranta bakwai daya daga cikin matansa na Sunna ta samu ciki, murna gun sarki ba'a magana, saidai Uwar Sumayya da Sumayya suna bakin cikin haka, a haka ta haifi 'ya mace aka saka mata Basma. 

Basma ta taso cikin tarbiyya me kyau dan haka koda ta tashi taga abin da Sumayya take ba rashin Adalci bata jin dadi wata rana idan ta mata magana saita zageta wataran harda duka.


Cigaba...


Yunwa da kishi yasa Nasrin ta kasa tafiya tana nishi da kyar ta fadi kasa dokin ya ci gaba da janta a kasa, har Nasrin ta fita hayyacinta a hakan Gimbiya Sumayya ta isa da ita gidan



Tana isa taga Yarima shiko tana isowa yayi inda Nasrin take yana cewa "Subhanallah Gimbiya kina tsoron Allah kuwa, wacece wannan "


Ta tabe baki tace "Haba Yarima wannan fa baiwa ce siyanta nayi a hanya, amma har a kanta zaka ce min bana tsoron Allah, To waima da zaka zo nan ka rubuto min wasikar zaka zo ne"


Yace "Kiyi hakuri ran Gimbiya ya dade, ina bukatar ganin kyakyawan fuskarki ne"


Bata masa magaba ta wuce ciki Kuyangunta suka bita a baya



Da sauri ya je inda Nasrin take baiyi la'akari dashi waye ba ya tallafota yasa hannusa a kirjinta yaji zuciyar ta tana bugawa da sauri yace a dogarawan da suka rako shi "ku bani ruwa"


Ana kawo masa ya shafa mata a fuskarta cikin razana ta mike sai kuma luuuu ta kara zubewa a jikinsa, ya gano abin dake damunta. Ya juya ya hago wata baiwa yace "Ke samo min ruwan zafi"


Tace "An gama ranka ya dade"

Ba ta dade ba ta kawo masa ruwan zafi Zallah ya fifita sannan ya bata ta fara sha a hankali cikin jan aji da kasaita kamar ita yar wani ce, 

Saida ta gama sha yace "ke 'yan wani gari ce"


A hankali tace "Samina"

Yace "Tun Daga Samina Gimbiya ta dauko ki "


Tace "Aa


Ta kashe masa labarin komai ta bashi 

Ya tausaya mata matuka yace "Bari Gimbiya tazo kina son in siye ki in kaiki garin mu"


Da sauri tace "eh"


Sunahaka Gimbiya yazo tana ganin Nasrin tace a wata baiwa ku tafi da ita a bata abinci,


Baiwar suka tafi da Nasrin 


Yarima Ya kalleta yace "Gimbiya Ina son ki siyar min wannan baiwa taki"


Tace "Bazan iya siyar da ita ba saidai kayi hakuri"


Zai sake magana ta dakatar dashi tace "Bana son wannan magana muyi abin daya shafe mu"


Bai sake yin maganan ba har ya tafi garin su. 

Yarima Amar kenan 



WANENE YARIMA




Sarki Abdulhamid shine ke sarautar garin mula, sarkine me Adalci baya tsauwalama bayin sa kuma akwai biyyaya wa sarakuna na gaba dashi, Matansa biyu ne sai k'wark'warori, Sarauniya Sauda itace Matarsa ta farko, wanda farkon Auren sa ta haifa masa da namiji, aka sa masa suna marwan, Nanfa Sarauniya ta fara feleke har ta fara yiwa Abokiyar  zamanta Aisha  gori danta shi zai gadi saurautar Mula, ita kuwa Aisha Sarauta ma kwata-kwata be dame ta ba 


Marwan ya tashi a sangarce, gashi dan gidan sarauta ne amma karami dashi sai yana rashin kunya, har zagin Aisha yake itako batacewa komai, idan a gaban sarauniya ce kuwa sai tace "gwara ka fara tun yanzu saboda wata rana a karkashin ka zata zauna"

Shakaransa biyar Aisha ta samu ciki Murna Agun Sarki Abdulhamid ba a magana, Sarauniya Sauda kuma bakin ciki kamar ya kashe ta, cikin haka ne Marwan ya fara dauke dauke wa Sarki, sarki tun bai ganewa har ya gane, ya kudiri niyya a ransa bazau bawa Marwan Sarauta ba, saidai ya bawa dan da Aisha zata haifa, idan ta haifi mace kuma zai bawa mijin da zai aure ta dan yana son ko bayan ransa Masarautarsa ta kasance me Adalci.

Aisha kuwa da ta tashi nakuda ta haifi danta kyakkyawa namiji, Ansha bikin suna sosai inda Sarki ya daga Jariri a gaban taro yace wannan nan nawa na sanya masa suna Amar kuma shine Yarima, Shi nake son ya gajeni Idan Allah ya bashi tsawon rai. 


Nan fa aka fara kus-kus wasu cewa suke ay yakamata Marwan ne zai gaje shi tunda shine babba, wasu ko cewa suke, sun gode ma Allah dama daga ganin marwan din nan halin uwarsa zaiyi, dan uwarsa saida aka nadata sarauniya ta karo wasu wulakanci. Koda ya koma ciki Sarauniya ta sashi a gaba tana kuka wai ya nuna mata bambanci. Ya zaice jaririn da akai sunan sa yau shine zai gaji sarauta bayan danta shine babba. 


Sarki Yace "So nake ko bayan raina Masarautata ta kasance me Adalci amma idan na bawa Marwan ina ji a jikin bayan na gushe masarautata zata kasance me rashin Adalci 

Aisha tace "Shima fa yarinta ne zai daina"


Sarki ya daka musu tsawa dika yace "ku kyale ni nayi abin da nayi niyya game da 'ya'yana,"

Tun lokacin Sarauniya Sauda ta dauki tsana ta dora akan Aisha da danta


Marwan yana tashi amma rashin tarbiyarsa tana kara yawa ga sata yana dauka zai kawo mahaifiyarsa.


Sarkin kan cewa yayi "Nidai ban rage shi da komai ba amma me yasa yake haka, kuma abin da yake sata yafi karfin sa.


Amar kuwa ya tashi cikin tarbiya kowa girmamashi yake, daidai da bayin gidan yana girmama su


A haka suka girma kamanninsu daya saidai halaye da banbanci shi Marwan yanzu abu ya girma dan shaye-shaye yasa a gaba, dayin fyade wa bayi mata idan An masa magana yace "Ay su bayi ne zai iya yin komai dasu "


Amar kuwa yanzu yana zuwa gun malamin gida koyon karatu kuma tun yana yaronsa ya sauke yanzu saidai ya koyar da wani 

Da kansa yace wa Me bartaba yana son aure sai saiki yace Kaje ka nemi yar gidan Sarkin Gauda Sumayya, tun ranan da yaje yaji ya kamu da son Sumayya, itama tana sonsa, Saidai ya lura kamar bata da tarbiyya saidai bai damu ba kasancewarta jinin Sarauta.


Cigaba...


Nasrin kuwa suna shiga aka bata abinci cikin kasaita take cin abincin ta har ta Gimbiya Sumayya ta shigo bata gama ba, ta kalle ta tace "ke kiyi sauri kici in nuna miki aikin ki"

Nasrin ta kalle cikin kasaita tace a hankali "Ni ban iya cin abinci da sauri ba " tana magana tana yarfa hannu


Gimbiya tace zaki koya dan uban ki


Ran Nasrin ya baci tace a hankali "ki daina zagin Ubana babana ya rasu "


Mari Gimbiya ta bata tace "Ni kike fadawa Uban ki ya mutu in daina zagin sa"


Nasrin ta mike tana huci tace " Zan miki duk abin da kike so saidai abin da zan fada miki bana son duka da zagin ubana"


Gimbiya daga murya tace "A fada wa Sarkin Duka ya kawo min doruna da gishiri, yau sai naga abin da kike takama dashi "

Nasrin ta koma ta zauna ta dora kafa daya akan daya ita kanta bata san tayi hakan ba

Gimbiya kuwa kallon ta take cikin mamaki har Sarkin Duka yazo.

Page 4⃣

Ta tashi ta shiga ciki bata dade ba Jakadiya ta shigo saida ta risina tace "Ranki ya dade Sarki yana nemanku keda Gimbiya Basma"


Ummi tace "Ace wa Sarki gamunan zuwa"


Kusan tare Gimbiya Sumayya  suka shiga part din Sarki Sai hararansa take, Sarki yayi Gyaran murya yace "Sumayya me ua hada ki da 'yar uwarki har kika mare ta"


Gimbiya Sumayya tace "Wai jiya na siyo Baiwa shine Basma da Umminta suna zigata tana min rashin kunya,"



Ummi tace "Ni Sumayya kiji Tsoron Allah fa"

Sarki yace "Basma fadi ya akayi"


Basma ta kwashe masa yadda aka yi ta fada 


Sarki yace "To shikenan Ke Basma kiyi hakuri bazan 'yanta ta ba tunda ita ta siyo baiwar ta, da a cikin bayi na nagidan nan ne dana 'yanta miki ko nawa kike so, amma idan zaki mata kyauta saboda kaunar da kike nuna mata ki mata,  ke kuma Sumayya bana son ki sake dukan Basma, dan kema ba dukanki muke ba"


Dukansu suka gyada kai alamun gamsuwa


Yace "To kuje Na sallame ku"


Duka suka tashi suka tafi 


Sumayya tana zuwa ta samu Nasrin tace "Bake maras kunya ba har kin samu kawa ko to dake da kawar taki saina hallaka ku kuma in na sake ganinki da Basma saina sa an sake miki Azabar da aka miki har tafi wannan ma"

Hawaye ya tsiyayo daga idonta, ita kanta ba a son ranta take yin hakan ba zuciyar ne take Azalzalarta 



Bangaren Yarima Amar  kuwa sai bayan isha yana komawa gida part din Ammynsa ya wuce ya sameta tana kuka, ya karaso yace "Ammyna nasan wadannan mutanen ne Marwan da Ummansa ko kinki barina in dau mataki me yasa"



Ta share hawayenta tace "Marwan dai dan Uwanka ne bana son zumuncin ku ya lalace ta kaina, ka kyale su wata rana Sai labari, yanzu dai yaya kaje wajen Gimbiya,"


Yace "Yanzu ma daga can nake,"

Yace "To Allah ya maka Albarka ya gyara tsakanin ku da dan Uwanka"


Baice komaiba yayi shiru can yace "Ammy ki ayka min da kayan marmari zan tafi"


Ya tashi ya nufi part dinsa cikin duhu yaci karo da wani abu koda ya duka yaga yarinya ce bata wuce shekara bakwai ba, yace "Me kike yi anan ki tafi gun kwanciyarki"


Tana nishi da kyar tace "bazan iya ba"

Tausayin ta ya kama shi ya dauke ta yasan Baiwa ce sai yayi sasan bayin da ita yana zuwa yaga suna neman ta, masifa yafara musu "Yaya a wannan daren zaku na barin yarinya gashi bata da lafiya Idan cikin duhun daren nan wani abu ya cije ta fa"


Wata daga ciki tace "Allah ya taimaki Yarima muma nemanta muke kuma lafiyanta kalau ta fita, tun la'asar sai yanzu da ka kawo mana ita"


Koda uwar yarinyar ta amshe ta sai ta ga jini ta bayan zanin tace "menene wannan zan gani"

Yarinyar tace "Yarima Marwan ne"

Yarima ya sa kai zai fita kenan ya zuyo yace "Me ya mata "


Cikin kuka uwar tace "fyade ya mata"


Yarima ya waro idon sa sai kuma ya rasa abin cewa durkushewa yayi a gurin yana Allah wadai da halin dan uwan nasa,can ya dago da jajayen idonsa yace "ku dauketa ku kaita gurin sarki ku fada masa"


Uwar tace "A'a kada ka damu mun barshi da Allah "

Yace "kada kiji komai ku kaita kawai ni zanji da komai"


Tace "kayi hakuri Yarima bazan iya ba"


Baice komaiba ya tashi ya tashi ya nufi part din Yarima Marwan ganinsa yayi kishingide yana shan kayan Marmari yace "haba yaya me yasa kake haka, yaya zaka aike ma karamar yarinya haka"

Marwan yayi masa mungun kallo yace "Baiwa tace duk abinda naga dama zanyi dasu, kuma me kazo min dakina "


Yarima Amar yace "Amma kasan kaima bawan Allah ne ko, kuma zai iya hukunta ka duk Lokacin da yaso"

Marwan yace "Kai malam fita min a daki "


Yarima Amar yace "dole in fada wa me Martaba abin da kayi"



Yana gama fada ya fita part din Me Martaba shiga yayi da Sallamarsa ya durkusa ya gaida mahaifin nasa bayan ya amsa Sarki yace "Me ke tafe dakai Yarima "

Shiru yayi can ya dago kansa sai hawaye 


Sarki ya sauko daga kan kujera ya dafa kafadar sa yace "Yarima kuka kuma me ka nema ka rasa"

Yarima ya Fada wa sarki komai 

Sarki yace "ka fita waje ka samu wani kayaje yace ina kiran sa"


Bangaren Marwan kuwa Yarima yana fita yaje ya samu bayin saida yaci musu mutunci tsofin su da yaran su kuma yace "Idan Sarki ya kira ku kuyi saida kuka yadda kuka ce wani Abu ya shiga tsakanina da 'yarku saina sa an kashe ku" 



Yana gama fada ya fita 


Daidai zai shiga part dinsa  kenan sai yaji wani bawa yace "Allah ya taimaki Yarima Marwan Sarki yace yana son ganinka"



Marwan yace "A cewa Sarki gani tafe akan hanya"

Bai shiga ba ya nufi gun Sarki, 

Yana zuwa Sarki yace "Marwan na gaji da abin da kake min haka ka ishe ni yanzu kakai ga har kananan yara kake yiwa Fyade"

Marwan ya dafa kirji yace "Sarki wa ya fada maka haka yanzu kazafi za'a min"

Yarima ya tsorata da kalamansa ya rasa abin cewa sai sarki yace "Amar Yaya akai"

Yarima ya maimaita abin da ya fada

Marwan ya fashe da kuka yace "Yanzu Amar kana kanina sharri zaka dora min, wani irin kiyayya ce wannan dan uwana yamin sharri"

Yarima yace "Idan sharri nake a kira su mana "


Sarki yayi gyaran Murya yace "muje wajen bayin da kaina"


Su kuwa har zasu rufe kofar su sai dogarawa suka kwankwasa kofa wani dogari ne yace " Ga me martaba yazo da kansa"

Duk kansu suka risina Yarima ya kalli Mariya wacce itace uwar yarinyar yace "ki fada wa Sarki da bakin ki abin da Dan uwana ya yi wa 'yarki"

Mariya tace "wace 'ya ni ay ba abin da ya hada Yarima da 'yata"

Yarima yace "Bani na kawo miki 'yarki ba ki fada min Dan uwana yayi mata fyade"

Mariya tace "A'a Yarima ko dai ka manta ne amma baka kawo yarinya nan ba"

Yace "ku bani fitila in haska"

Aka mika masa yayi ta haskawa amma bai ganta ba, ya gama haske-haskensa amma bai ganta ba, ya dawo da jajayen idonsa da suka kada lokaci guda.


Marwan yace "Haba dan uwana me muka maka ne nida Ummana ka tsane mu"

Baice komai ba ya nufi part dinsa zuciyar sa na tafarfasa. 


Marwan ya dubi Sarki yace "Me Martaba ka gani ko menene amfanin haka"


Sarki ma baice komaiba ya tafi yana tunanin me yasa dansa da yafi so a duniya zaiyi karya

Marwan kuwa tafiya yayi yana farin cikin samun nasara 


Yarima Amar kasa yin barci yayi yana bakin ciki da kunya, tashi yayi yayi alola yayi Sallah raka'a biyu sannan ya dauki Alqur'ani me girma yana karantawa, sai karfe daya barci ya kwashe shi,


Washe gari kasa xuwa fada yayi saboda kunyan Mahaifin sa da dogarawan da suka san abin daya faru, gun Umminsa yaje koda ya fada mata komai ta tausaya masa, kwanciya yayi a cinyarsa yace "Ummina Ina jin kunyar yan fada bansan da wani ido zasu kallen ba zasu dauke ni makaryaci. "


Ummi tace "Aa kada ka damu kaje kawai ka gaisa da mahaifin ka komai ma ya wuce, kai kuma ka daina sa ido akan abin da ba ruwanka"

Yace "to ummina


Marwan kuwa "koda ya fada wa Ummansa cewa tayi itama yanzu zata tada nata"

Marwan yace "tayaya"


Tace "zanje ne in tadawa Sarki hankali ince ban yadda da sharrin da aka yiwa dana ba, ba yace ba kaine magajinsa ba, to kaga idan nayi haka a fada za'a dauki Amar makaryaci me Kazafi, kuma 'yan fada zasu ce basu yadda ya zama magajinka ba, kaga kai zasu ce"

Marwan yayi Murmushi yace "Yawwa Ummana tashi kije"

Tashi tayi ta nufi fada lokacin kuwa an cika a fada,

Amar kuwa koda ya tashi gun Umminsa Fada yayi a hanya yaji an bige shi yana juyawa yaga Umman Marwan ne

Gani yayi ta shiga fada sai yaji kamar ya koma amma sai yace kawai bari yaje tunda Umminsa ta bashi kwarin gwiwa, 

Yana shiga yaga ta gurfana gaba Sarki ta fasa kuka ga mutane da yawa a fada cewa take "Yanzu a matsayina na sarauniya a dora wa d'ana sharrin fyade wlh bazan yadda ba sai ka dauki mataki"

Dogarawa kuwa cewa suke "Allah ya ba Sarauniya hakuri "

Amar kuwa ji yayi ya kasa karasawa gaban Mahafinsa saboda kunya, 



Marwan ne ya shigo ya kamata yana cewa "Ummana ki bari komai ya wuce kinga mutane da yawa anan"

Ta bige shi tace "Idan komai ya wuce a gurinka to ni bai wuce ba, sai an bi mana hakkin mu"

Sarki yayi gyaran murya yace "ki tashi kije zan dauki mataki"


Tace "A haka zai zama magajinka ya fara yiwa mutane kazafi"


Marwan Yace "Umma tashi muje mana "

Tace "bazan je ba kai ka kyale sakaran banza kawai, duk abin da yaron nan da uwar sa suke mana idan zanyi magana kace in kyale su, haka jiya mafa ta gama min gori wai danta ne magaji, Ni Sarauta ma bata gaba na kwata-kwata amma ni gorin ne yake damuna"

Sarki yace "Nace kije zan dau mataki"

Marwan ne ya dagota suka fita


Ummi kuwa jakadiya ce tazo ta fada mata komai. 

Ummi tace "Ina Yarima"


Jakadiya tace "Yarima yana tsakiyar fada ko dago kansa ya kasa yi"


Tace "barin je da kaina"

Ummi ta tashi ta nufi fada a hanya ta hadu da su Marwan, Marwan ya take mata kafa ya wuce, 
Itako bata ce komai ba ta wuce

Page 6⃣



Shankuwa ce me tsanani ya shiga tsakanin Gimbiya Basma da Inda kullun suke haduwa wajen amsan abinci


Yau ya kasance Yarima Amar Zaizo dan tun jiya ya aiko daya daga Cikin bayin gidan su da takardar zuwansa. Dan haka tun safe Nasrin bata samu zama tana ta aiki koda Gimbiya ta lura Nasrin ta iya girki ita tace ta masa girkin, koda Nasrin ta tsara wa Yarima girki ta zauna ta ci a kicin dan a cewarta bazata yi aikin banza ba dan tasan Gimbiya baza ta bata ko dandaniba. Tana shiryo Abincin tana kawo wa Gimbiya saida ta dundeta a baya tace "Shegiya sai da kika dade"

Nasrin tasa hannunta a baya tana hararanta, Gimbiya tace "Zo ki doken maras kunya"


Nasrin tace "Ni nace zan dake ki ne"


Gimbiya bata ce komaiba sai dai cikin ranta tana mamakin Nasrin yadda  bata tsoron ta duk irin dukan da take mata ga azaba da take bata

A kan dokin sa Yarima ya  karaso  Cikin kwalliya  na alfarma  da dogarawan sa da suke take masa  baya. 

Jakadiyya ce ta masa iso har gun Sarki,  bayan ya gaisa da Sarki ne ya koma dakin baki ya zauna zaman jiran Gimbiya.


Kafin ta fito ta fada wa Nasrin ta kai masa kayan marmari kafin ta iso


Nasrin ta dauka ta tafi  
 cikin takun ta na kasaita ta shiga ta ajiye kayan marmarin a kan dan karamin table din dake gaban sa tadan risina tace "Barka da zuwa Yarima da fatan kana lafiya "


Yayi kayataccen Murmushi yace "Barkaki dai ya sunanki"

Ta dan sunkuyar da kai tace "Sunana Nasrin "

Yace "Suna me Dadi me dadi ke 'yar wani gari ne"


Tace "Samina"


Yace "ohhh kece Gimbiya tazo dake ranan da nazo har nace zan saye ki"

Tace "eh nice ka tuna ni kenan ay ni ban  manta ka ba"


Nasrin kan  ta sake jiki sai hiranta take da Yarima harda kyalkyala dariya. Ta manta ma waye a gabanta. Shiko ba karamin dadin hakan yaji ba. Yaso ma da gimbiya ta barsu kada tazo. Suna haka Gimbiya tazo taga yadda Yarima da Nasrin suke kyalkyala dariya.

Wannan page din naki ne 'yan group din *IZZAR MULKI* kuyi yadda kuke so dashi



Page 5⃣


Yarima Amar kuwa su Marwan suna fita ya mike kafafuwansa suna rawa ya durkusa gaban Sarki yace "Me martaba gani ka yanke min hukuncin "


Sarki yace "Dana ina son inji gaskiyar lamari da gaske ne kazafi kayi wa dan uwanka"


Dago kansa yayi sai hawaye ya gangaro Sarki ya jawo shi ya kwantar da kansa kan cinyarsa yana gigiza shi, daidai lokacin Umminsa ta shigo ta durkusa tace "Allah ya taimake ka a mana afuwa"


Yace "Baku min laifi ba amma kuna bani hakuri me yasa, Nasan halin dana bai taba min karya ba kuma a iya sanina yana matukar son dan uwansa ko ya yi ma wani kazafi shi bazai masa ba, ina son ka kwantar da hankalinka Yarima bana son ka sa damuwa a ranka, bani kadai ba har mutanen da suke nan gurin ma basu zaka iya yin kazafi wa dan uwanka ba"


Ya kalli Ummi yace "kama shi ki je ki lallashe shi kinsan irin zuciyar sa"


Tace "to" ta kama shi ta fita dashi tun a nan take masa nasiha irin ta lallashi"


Karfe 12 :00 yarinyar da aka yiwa fyade tace ga garin ku, basu bari sarki yaji ba suka yi jana'izarta suka binneta.





Nasrin kuwa tana zaune yana jawa Gimbiya Sumayya yatsu taji an buga kararrawa, Gimbiya  ta kalleta tace "ki dauki roba kije kibi layi ki amshi abinci"

Nasrin kamar bazata amsa ba sai kuma ta tashi cikin takun kasaita ta dauki roba ta nufi waje dan bata ma inda ake rabon abincin ba 

Tana fita taga bayi suna tafiya da da robansu itama ta bisu ko da suka isa tabi layin mata tabi ana ture-ture amma ita duk ta kasa karshe dai aka ture ta ranta ya baci ta fito daga layin taje gaba ta mika robarta sai ji tayi an zabga mata bulala a gadon bayan ta, ranta ya sake baci ta wurga robar a fuskar mutumin daya daketa tace "Wannan wani irin rayuwa ce abincin ma akan shi sai an dake ni, abin duka baya muku kadan wai, To kada wani ya sake dukana Abincin ma na fasa ci"


Ta koma ta samu Gimbiya Sumayya ta zauna can gefe Gimbiya tace "Ina son ki fada min ke wacece, da baza ki iya bin layi kici abinci ba, anzo an fada min abin da kika musu acan"

Cikin kasaita da *IZZA* Nasrin tace "Ni Nasrin ce' yar gidan Malam Iliya, kuma duk munafukin daya fada miki ma ya fadi gaskiya" tana magana tana murguda baki


Gimbiya shiru yayi tana Mamakin ta ta kalleta taga Sai harare-harare kamar idan tayi magana zata iya dukan ta, yace "Kin manta dukan gishiri ko"


A tunanin Gimbiya ko zata ji tsoro ne ga mamakin ta sai ji tayi tace "yo in dukan gishiri ne sai ayi tayi tunda ba kashe ni za'a yi ba, yo in kisan ne ina tsoro ne"

Ji tayi ta bige tama baki da takalmi ta kuma daukan wayar wuta ta zuba mata a gadon bayan ta, amma duk da haka Nasrin sai huci take kamar zata daketa, 


Gimbiya ta kuma mika mata kafarta tace "Ci gaba da abin da kike min"


Nasrin ta kama kafar ta ci gaba da ja tana kunkuni.


Da magrib taji an buga kararrawa ta tashi tabi layi nan ma sai ture-ture ake koda ta fahimci me ture mutane taje ta kwada mata mari tace "ke kinsan ni wacece kike ture ni," 

Uwani wanda take kimanin shekara sha biyar ta kalli Nasrin tace "Wacece ke zaki mareni"


Ta daga hannu zata dake ta taji muryan Gimbiya Basma "Kada ki Sake ki dake ta "

Ta sauke hannun tana kallon Gimbiya Basma da take takowa cikin kayan Sarauta, saida ta karaso tace "Akan Abinci zaki mare ta"


Uwani tace Allah ya taimaki Gimbiya ita ta fara marina 


Basma tace "ta mara dama ay ina jin labarinki kece me yawan ture mutane gun amsan abinci, kuma idan kika sake saina sa an hukunta min ke,"



Ta kama hannun Nasrin tace ta kaita gun zuba abincin tace "ga Nasrin daga yau ban yadda ta kara bin layi ba, idan tazo kawai ku bata abinci"

Ta tare suka ce "To ranki ya dade"

Daya daga cikin su yace "kawo robar a zuba miki"


Gimbiya tace "A'a ba bukata yanzu daga gobe nake nufi"


Kama hannunta tayi suka nufi gun Ummanta a hanya ne tace "Nasrin kiyi hakuri nayi iya kokarina dan Sarki ya 'yanta ki, yaki yace wai da ace a cikin bayin sa ne da zai iya 'yanta ki, amma tunda ke baiwar Gimbiya ce bazai iya ba"



Nasrin tayi murmushinta irin na kasaita tace "Nagode Gimbiya ban san da wani baki zan gode miki ba wlh"


Tace "Kada ki damu ina tare dake, kuma ki daina ce min Gimbiya ki kirani da Sunana Basma tunda mu kawaye ne"

Murmushi kawai tayi daidai lokacin sun isa part din su ta amshi robar ta tace "Daga yau idan kin fito amsan abinci kizo nan kina amsa dan nasan Gimbiya baza tana barinki kina fitowa ba,"


Abinci lafiyayye ta zuba mata harda kaji ta saka mata kayan marmari a leda tace "ta tafi dashi"

Nasrin tayi godiya ta kama hanya ta tafi Saida ta shiga part din Gimbiya Sumayya ta fara ci, dama tana jin yunwa rabon da taci abinci tun safe da taci agun Basma 

Tana ci tana sawa Basma Albarka Gimbiya ta shigo da kuyangi biyu a bayanta ta kalli Nasrin tace "me haka wa ya baki wannan abincin"


Cikin ko in kula tace "Gimbiya basma ce ta bani"


Gimbiya Sumayya tace "iye ke 'yar gata kop baza ki amshi abinci cikin 'yan uwanki bayi ba kinje tana baki ko to wlh baki isa ba, ta dauki abincin ta ta mika wa bayi tace gashi guje kuci, kayan marmarin ma ta amshe ta basu, itako Nasrin ko a jikin ta kuma tana jin yunwa wanda taci ma dan kadan ne.

Haka ta zauna karfe tara ta shigo da basu mata wanka suka cire mata kaya suka shiga da ita bayi, Nasrin tace "ohhh Gimbiya bata da ta ido wankan ma sai an mata, yanzu kartin matan sun gama kalleta"


Daga ciki Gimbiya tace "Ke me kike cewa"


Kanta tsaye tace "cewa nayi wankan ma sai an miki babba dake"


Gimbiya tace "Ohhh maimaitawa kike yi"


Nasrin tace "Ni bana karya ko za'a kashe ni"


Tace "Zan fito ay saikin fada min awa kike rashin kunya dan uwarki"

Shiru Nasrin  tayi tana murgudawa kofar bayin baki,


Gimbiya kuwa suna fito da ita taga Nasrin a kujeranta ta dora kafa daya kan daya. Tana girgiza na saman. Gimbiya ta rufe ta da duka duka take ta ko ina sai da  ta gaji dan kanta ta daina, bayan sun gama shirya suka ajiye ta kan gado suka saka mata kayan barci suka fita, 


Ta kalli Nasrin tace "Zo ki kwanta zanyi filo dake"


Nasrin tace "wai Gimbiya baki da f... "


Bata karasa ba sai ji tayi ta bige mata baki

Nasrin tace "Ahhh Gimbiya kinji hannunki kamar karfe "


Kamar ubanki ne "zo ki kwanta barci nake ji"

Bata kara maganaba ta kwanta tayi yadda tace , ita kuma tasa kanta  kan ruwan cikinta ba dadewa duk barci ya kwashe su. 

Asuba nayi Nasrin ta tashi ta yunkura ta zamar da gimbiya taje tayi Sallah sannan ta fara gyare-gyaren part din Gimbiya dan ta mata gargadi, tana tsaka dayi aka buga kararrawa, ta dauki kofi taje ta mika musu ba bin layi ta amso kunu da kosai ta dawo ta zauna tana sha, Basma kuwa tana ta jira ko zata zo shiru bata zo ba, dan haka da aka buga na rana Basma yaje ta tsaya gurin Nasrin tana zuwa ta tare ta tace "shine kika bari ina ta jiran ki ko"



Nasrin tace "Wlh ba haka bane " ta fada mata komai


Basma tace "To kina zuwa gun Ummina kina ci mana"


Nasrin tace "kada ki damu idan nace zanje gun Ummi zata ki yadda ki bari kawai ina cin wannan din"

Basma tace "shikenan ba damuwa Idan an buga dinning kullum zan na zuwa muna hira ko,"

" to Gimbiya "

"Gimbiya ya fita a bakinki fa ke kawa tace"

Murmushi Nasrin yayi.


Haka rayuwan Nasrin ta kasance Ba ranan da bazata sha duka gun Gimbiya ba, kuma kullun suna haduwa da Basma yanzu ta daina ce mara Gimbiya ma, da Basmana take kiran ta, itakuma tace Nasrin dina

Page 8⃣

Amma abin mamaki Ita wacce a ke dukan ma ko digon hawaye babu a idonta saima tana gama dukan taje ta sake alola dan bata sani ba ba mamaki alolanta ta karye


Tana dawowa ta fara sallah ita yanzu ba matsin Gimbiya ke damunta ba tana tunanin halin da Ummanta take ciki


Yarima Amar kuwa yana komawa ya sanar da Sarki yadda sukayi, Sarki yace "Masha Allah "



Ya kalli Marwan dake gefensa yace "kaima yakamata ka fito da mata ko, sai a hada da dan 'uwanka"


Kafin yayi magana Sarauniya wato mahaifiyarsa tace "ba yanzu ba, dan baza a hada auren dana da na wani banza ba"


Sarki nunata da yatsa yace "YAMUNA ki kiyaye duniya, dan nawa ne banza?"


Ummin Amar kuwa bata ce komaiba tana zaune gefe daya ta kalmashe kafarta. Sarki yace "Shikenan sai Kayi ta zama a haka shikan zan masa aure tunda yana so "


Koda Sarki yaje cikin lokaci kalilan aka gama komai inda aka sa rana wata shida sai kuma ko wani Bangaren aka fara shirye-shiye 



Ita kuwa Nasrin yanzu abin dake damun ta bai wuce tunanin Ummanta ba ko a wani hali take ciki 


Saura wata biyu biki Nasrin kuwa murna take tasan koba komai zata rabu da Gimbiya 


Tana kwance yayn da Gimbiya take kwance a cikin ta, itako maranta ne yake mata ciwo tun tana daurewa har ta fara kuka. Wanda Lokacin Gimbiya har tayi  barci. Cikin barcinta taji Kukan Nasrin a fusace ta tashi ta sharara mata mari tace "Wannan wani irin akuyanci ne zaki dame da kuka"

Nasrin ta dafa kuncin ta da hannunta cikin kuka tace "cikina ne yake ciwo"


Gimbiya tace "Dan cikin na ciwo sai ki dame da kuka. Dalla gyara mun in kwanta kuma kici gaba damin kuka in fasa miki baki shegiya 'yar iska"


Nasrin tayi yadda tace tana daurewa Cikin haka taji abu yana zuba ta kasanta. Saidai bata iya motsi ba Saboda bata son ta jawa kanta zagi da duka


A haka barcin wahala ya kwashe ta


Da asuba kuwa tana tashi taga jini ya jika mata zani har ya bata farin zanin gadon dake kan gadon 


Tsoro ne ya kama ta a ranta tace "shikenan yanzu haka saboda dukana da suke yi ne suka fasa mata wani abu a ciki"


Kuka tasa har saida Gimbiya ta farka ta yi mata rankwashe akai tace "me haka"

Cikin kuka Nasrin tace "tun jiya jini yake zubo wa ina tunanin wani Abu ne ya fashe min a  ciki"

Gimbiya ta dubi jinin kuma ta fahimci Jinin Al'adar tace tazo kuma wannan shine farko tunda ga alama bata sanshi ba

Ta kalli Nasrin tace "kamar yadda kika bata min zanin gado haka nake so ki wanke min abuna shegiyar yarinya,


Ba musu ta tattara taje ta wanke tana kuka 

Bayan ta gama ta shanya. Ta kasa sukuni ko amsan abinci ta kasa zuwa ta amsa ita ko Gimbiya da saninta taki fada  mata 

Haka Basma tayi ta jiran zuwanta amma shiru bata zoba har aka gama rabon abincin 


Haka Nasrin ta zauna a takure da yamma ko Yarima Amar yazo dama tun ranan da aka sa masa rana bai sake zuwa ba



Gimbiya tace da Nasrin ta kai masa 'ya'yan itace kafin tazo

Saida ta canja zaniNasrin ta tafi takai ta durkusa ta gaishe shi ya amsa cikin murmushi tana durkushe jinin ya zubo da sauri ta mike ta nufi kofar fita. Yarima Amar kuwa idon yayi ka zaninta kuma ya ga tana cikin damuwa ya tsayar da ita yace "Nasrin lafiyan ki kuwa "


Ay kuwa ta fashe da kuka cikin kuka tace "saboda dukana da ake yawan yi yasa wani abu ya fashe min a cikina gashi tun jiya jini yake zubowa"


Ya fahimci bata san wani irin jini bane kuma wannan karon na farko 

Tausayin ta ya kamashi yace "Nasrin ki kwanta da hankalinki ba abinda ya fashe a cikinki", ya mata bayanin jinin da yadda zata kula dashi, wani kunya ne ya kama ta dan sai yanzu ta tuna Ummanta ta mata bayanin sa.

Yana gama mata jawabi ta fito taje tayi yadda yace 



Da magrib ta tafi amso abinci tana zuwa taga Basma itako tana ganinta yace "Nasrin lafiyan ki kuwa yau ban ganki ba"


Nasrin bata boye mata ba ta fada mata komai,


Dariya Basma ta mata tace "amma wlh kinji kunya Nasrin tunda kika bari namiji ya miki bayanin wannan 


Nasrin tace "ashe ke kinsan shi"

Basma tace "sau uku nayi ranan da nayi na farko ammi ce ta min bayani komai "


Gimbiya ce tazo wucewa tahangi Nasrin da Basma suna ta hira cikin nishadi ta girgiza kai tace "Zanyi maganin Basma kuwa "

Page 9⃣

Karasawa tayi wajen su, Nasrin da sauri ta mike dan ita ba abin dake damunta irin zagin iyayenta da ake.

 Gimbiya tace "ya zaki tashi ba kuna hira da kawarki, nima nazo ne muyi hiran tare"


Ita dai Nasrin bata aminta da itaba tasan Akwai muguntar da shiyar amma Basma cewa tayi "yawwa Anty zo ki zauna ayi dake"


Gimbiya tace "yanzu dai ba wannan ba muje mu gaida Amminki"


Basma tace "Ammi bata nan amma dai muje"


Gimbiya ta kalli Nasrin tace "ke kije kici Abinci tukun,kema kije yanzu gani nan zuwa


Ba musu suka yi yadda tace 


Itako Gimbiya zuwa tayi ta sanu  gwava dan tasan Basma da son gwava. Ta samu syringe ta tsira ruwan guba a ciki ta rike tayi Murmushi tace "Basma yau zaki sha dadi"

Ta fita Nasrin kuwa Abinci take ci bata san lokacin da Gimbiya ta fita ba,



Gimbiya bata tsaya a ko
Ina ba sai part din Ammi tana zuwa ya buga kofar bata dade ba Basma tazo ta bude tana ganinta sa saki murmushi tace "Yaya ashe kece da kin ayko Nasrin ta kirani"

Itama murmushi ta sakar mata kamar da gaske tace ""ba nace dama Zanzo in gaida Ammiba barin jirata"

Basma tace "Ammi ta dawo ta tafi gun me martaba kuma sai da Asuba zata dawo"


Gimbiya tace "To muje in taya ki hira"


Basma ta dan matsa tana murmushi "shigo yaya ta"


Gimbiya ta shiga ta harde kan kujera Basma ta zauna tace "Yaya me zan kawo miki "

Gimbiya ta kishida tace "bana bukatar komai Basma, ba kuyangin da suke tsaronki ne ke kadai kike kwana idan Ammi tana gun me martaba"?


Basma tace "Akwai me tsarona shi yasa bana niman kuyangi, ni bana bukatar su wannan ra'ayi nane"


Gimbiya tace "hmmm yayi ga gwava na kawo miki dama nasan kika bukatar son gwava"

Basma ta sa hannu biyu ta amsa tana murmushi tace "kinga rabona da ita na dade saboda Ammi ta hanani wai yana daure min ciki da yawa, kuma yana bani wahala wajen yin Bayan gida"



Gimbiya tace "Kici wannan tunda kadan ne bazai miki komai ba"


Basma tace "barin je in wanke"


Gimbiya tace "Ay na wanke amma kya iya kara wankewa"


Basma tace "ba bukata"


Tasa baki ta gatsa ta lumshe ido tace "Yaya da dadi"

Gimbiya murmushi tayi tana kallon ta, yayain da Basma kuma taci gaba da ci, saida ta gama, Gimbiya ta fashe da dariyar mugunta tace "An gaya miki akwai son ki cikin zuciya tace,
Wato na kawo baiwa ke me tausayi kina ne ma ki yantata sarki ya hana yanzu kina shiga jikin ta ki sawa tana min rashin kunya, to bari kiji wannan da kika ci nasa guba a cikinta idan kin mutu sai inga ya zaki shiga jikin baiwata,"



Tana gama fada ta tashi zata fita Basma tace "Allah ya isa tsanina dake kuma bari kiji indai Nasrinnn..."  maganan ta makale sakamakon cikinta daya murda ta durkusa kasa ta daga kai tana kallon Gimbiya yayin da hawaye suka zuba mata


Gimbiya kuwa fashe tayi da wata dariyar da taciwa Basma rai ta fita

Nasrin kuwa saida cin Abincinta ta gyara part din tace "Barin je wajen kawata tunda yau har fuska aka sake min"
Can kuma ta tabe baki tace "uhmm wa ya sani ma kona makirci ne"

Tana fita taga Gimbiya zata shiga. Nasrin ta rusuna tace "barka da dawowa Gimbiya "


Gimbiya bata amsa mata ba itama bata damuba saboda dama idan da sabo ta Saba, saidai tana mamakin ta da bata tambayeta ina zata je ba.


Sa ka tayi ta tafi kai tsaye part din Ammi tayi tana zuwa taga kofar a bude tayi Sallama amma shi, Tanashiga ta hango Basma kumfa na fita a bakinta ta kama ciki da hannu daya hannunta daya kuma tana mika mata sho alamun tana bukatar taimako
taimako


Da sauri Nasrin ta karasa ta rumgumeta tace "Basma waya miki haka fada min wlh bazan gyale shi ko shi waye"


Basma ta bude baki zatayi magana sai kuma ta fara kalmar shahada, nan kuma rai yayi halinsa, daidai lokacin Ammi ta shigo tana ganinta Tace "Nasrin me kika bata taci bakinta kumfa, Nasrin guba kika ba basma data nuna miki kauna"
Tallafota tayi a hannun Nasrin ta daga hannunta tace "shikenan Nasrin kin kasheta "

Page 1⃣0⃣


Nasrin durkushewa tana kuka ta rasa abin da zata ce. 


Ammi kuwa kwantar da gawar Basma tayi ta na kuka ta kama Nasrin da duka tana cewa ki fada min me Basma ta miki kika kasheta, cikin kuka tace "wlh bani na kashe ta ba nima zuwa nayi na ganta a haka tana kokarin min magana saidai Allah bai bata sa'a ba"


"Kinji karamar yarinya da makirci sai kin fada min muje gaban Sarki "



Kamata tayi feee sai part din sarki tana zuwa falo ta kurma ihu da sauri sarki ya fito Nasrin ma kukan take saidai ita ma kukan rashin Basma takeyi. Dan bata fargaban hukuncin da za'a yanke mata.


Sarki yace "lafiya"


Ammy tace "Wannan ce ta kashe Basma"


Sarki ya kalli Nasrin da take Kuka yace "yarinya me Basma ta miki nasan haka kawai baza ki kashe ta ba"


Cikin kuka tace "wlh bani bace nima nazo na same ta a haka kuma ita ce ma tace nazo "



Ammy ta bata wani gigitaccen mari tace "karya kike ki fada min waya saki"


Sarki yace "muje inga gawar "



Suka tafi part din Ammi dukansu ukun ya bude gawarta yaga fuskarta yace "guba aka bata taci"


Ya kalli Nasrin yace "kije ki kwanta amma kisan da cewa gobe idan baki fada mana waya saki ba kashe ki zamuyi"


Ammy tace "ya zaka ce ta tafi idan ta gudu bafa"


Sarki ya daga mata hannu yayan da Nasrin tana kukan mutuwar Basma 


Bayan fitarta ta ne yace "bana son kowa yasan mutuwar Basma sai ke, ina son yanzu ki mata wanka ki mata sitira zuwa gobe da safe"


Nasrin kuwa tana shiga taga Gimbiya ta dira kafa daya kan daya tace "Gidan Ubanki wa kika je sai yanzu kika dawo"


Nasrin kara fashewa tayi da wani sabon kukan tace "Basma ta rasu guba aka bata"


Gimbiya ta mike da sauri tace "ba dazu kuka rabu ba"?


Nasrin tace "eh, bayan na dawo naci abinci shine naje gurin ta sai naga an bata guba kumfa na fita a bakinta tana son tamin magana ta kasa har ta mutu,"

Gimbiya ta hadiyi miyau a ranta kuwa murna take da ba'a gano itace sillan hakan ba ta kalli Nasrin tace "yanzun kowa yasan mutuwar "

Nasrin tace "Aa Amminta ma tace ni na kashe ta"


Gimbiya tace "Allah ya kara uban me ya kai, nidai ba ruwa na kisan yadda zaki fitar da kanki idan ba haka ba kashe ki Zasuyi"

Nasrin tace "ni yanzu bana tsoron mutuwa"


Gimbiya ta harareta "dalla wuce mu kwanta banza kawai wacce bata san ciwon kanta ba"


 A hankali ta wuce ta kwanta sannan Gimbiya ma ta kwanta tayi matashi da ruwan cikinta 

Tuni barci yayi awon gaba da Gimbiya yayin Nasrin ko runtsawa ba tayi ba tana tunanin Basma 



Ammi tayi wa Basma wanka ta mata sitira ta zauna tana tunanin alakar Basma da Nasrin. Ta tuna yadda tazo ta sameta akan gawar ta tuna kukan da Nasrin take. Ta lumshe idonta Cikin ranta tace "Anya Nasrin zata iya aikata haka"


Ta kalli Sarki dake gefe tace "Anya yarinyar nan zata iya aikata haka"



Sarki yace "Nima dai haka nake gani koda ita din ce ina tunanin tilasta mata aka yi, amma mu bari zuwa gobe, kuma bana son ki fadi cewa kashe ta akayi,  Basma dai lokacin ta yayi kuma ta rasu"


Bayan sallar Asuba aka sanar da rasuwan Basma a masallaci bayi da dogarawan gidan, kai har yan waje gidan sun koka, karfe bakwai akayi jana'izarta aka kaita Gidan ta na gaskiya.


Nasrin kuwa dama ko runtsawa bata yi ba har lokacin kuka take. Lokacin kuwa Gimbiya an mata wanka an mata kwalliya kamar ba gidan mutuwa ba. Wata baiwa ce ta shigo ta kwashi gaisuwa sannan tace "Gimbiya me martaba yace kizo keda Nasrin"

Tace "Acewa me martaba gani nan tafe"


Ta fita ita kuwa gimbiya ta kalli Nasrin tace "Sai kisan me zaki fada musu ni nawa ido"


Tana magana tana tabe baki


Gimbiya ce ta fara tashi ita kuma tana biye da ita a baya har suka isa part din sarki 


Sarki ne da matansa Uku sai mahaifiyar Gimbiya Sumayya. Da yake komai za ayi ana yi da ita sabida 'yarta

Gimbiya a kujera ta zauna yayin da Nasrin kuma ta durkuwa can gefe Sarki ya kalli Gimbiya Yace "Sumayya baki san anyi rasuwa a gidan ba"


Ta tabe bakinta tace "na sani mana"


Yace "shine ko gaisuwa baza ki mana ba, idan mu baki mana ba ay sai kiyiwa mahaifiyar Basma, kuma kalli irin kwalliyar da kika yi kamar ba gidan mutuwa ba"


Nan Sumaiya ta fara bubbuga kafa tana cewa "Haba ranka ya dade A gaban baiwata kake min fada salon ta raina ni"


Sarki yace "ya isa haka na lura kin sangarce da yawa zan gyara miki zama"

Ya kalli Nasrin da tunda ta shigo take kuka ga idanunta a kumbure duk ta canja yace "ke ya muka kwana a zancen mu za'a yanke hukuncine ku zaki fada mana wanda wanda ya saki"?


Nasrin tace cikin kuka "bani na kashe ta ba, amma na yadda a yanke min hukuncin saboda ko ba'a kashe niba damuwa zata kashe. Tunda tunda na fito daga garinmu nake fuskantar matsala. A hanya Babana ya rasu, aka rabani da ummata, ga wacce ta jawo jikinta itama mutuwa ta raba ni da ita. Dan Allah kuyi gaggawar yanke min hukuncin "


Ji tayi an rumgumeta ta baya aka saka kuka tana juyawa sai taga Ammi ce tace "Ammi ni na kashe Basma ki bari a kashe"


Tayi saurin rufe mata baki tace "ba ke bace Nasrin kuma ba wanda zai yanke miki hukunci akan laifin da ba ke kika aikata ba, nasan har yanzu kina son Basma a zuciyar ki nima ina sonki wlh,"


Kamata tayi ta kaita gaban sarki tace "Sarki Inna son a sayar min da Nasrin zan 'yanta ta ta maye min gurbin Basma.

*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*

Page 1⃣1⃣




Sarki ya kalli Gimbiya yace "ina son ki sayar min da baiwarki"


Gimbiya ta turo baki tace "Gaskiya bazan iya ba nima ina bukatar ta"


Tana gama fada ta tashi ta fita. Da sauri Nasrin ta bita dan kada tayi laifi Ammi kuwa ta bita da kallo cikin tausayawa. 




Suna fita Sarki yace "kiyi hakuri ki je cikin bayina ki zabo duk wanda ta miki ni kuma zan 'yanta miki ita"


Ammi ta kalle shi tace "Nagode ranka ya dade Amma ba wacce zata maye min gurbin Basma sai Nasrin tunda bazan same taba na hakura na nika duk kan lamura na ga Allah "


Sarki ya kalleta cikin tausayawa yace "Allah ya dube mu da idon rahma"


Tace Amin



Bangaren Nasrin kuwa Gimbiya na shiga ita ma ta shiga ta zauna can gefe. Gimbiya tayi mata mungun kallo tace "wato kina son a 'yanta ki ko? Zaki koma wajenta kiyi rayuwar gidan sarauta to baki isa ba keda sarauta har abada a haka zaki mutu"


Nasrin bata ce komai ba dama ita rayuwar sarauta bata dameta ba abin daya dame ta bai wuce tunanin inda mahaifiyarta take ba sai jimamin rasuwar basma


haka aka fara shirye-shiryen biki. Idan za'ayi biki bayin gidan suna bakin ciki saboda aikin da zasu sha. Amma wannan bikin murna suke saboda azzaluma zata bar gidan, 


Ranan juma'a aka daura auren Yarima Amar da Gimbiya Sumayya a masarautar Sarki Abdulbasid, inda Daurin auren ya tara manyan Sarakuna ciki harda Sarkin Samina Wanda babban Sarkine kuma ana ji dashi a fadin kasar.


Bayi sai aiki suke ko wanne fuskarsu akwai annuri idan ka gansu kace suna murnan auren ne saidai su suna murnan Gimbiya zata bar musu gidan ne


Anyi wasannin dokuna inda sai washe gari da sassafe aka sa Amarya  dokinta da yasha ado da yan rakiyanta aka tafi da ita,  



Sai yamma suka isa suna isa aka fara busa Ageta masu guda na guda. Gefe kuma ga marika suna nasu. Can kuma masu kida da rawa. Haka dai masarautar ta hargitse da hayaniya. Matane suka zo suka shiga da Gimbiya sasan Yarima Amar inda yasha Ado da kayan Alatu. 



Ana shiga da ita aka fara kiraye kirayen sallar magrib kowa ya fita neman abin yin Sallah inda Nasrin ma ta fita bakin rijiya ta nufa inda ta isa aka zuba mata ruwa tayi alola ta zo inda taga mutane suna sallah itama ta gabatar da tata. Tana idarwa ta nufi gun Gimbiya dan tasan idan bata ganta ba zata iya yin lefi. Kuma ba wuya bane ta doke ta



Tana tashi ta nufi sassan saita hango Yarima Amar shima yana ganinta ya sake mata murmushi ya taho gunta yace "Su Nasrin ce a garin mu"


Ta risina ta gaishe shi. Ya amsa yana murmushi. Zata wuce yace "Muje ki gaida Ummi ba"


Ba musu ta bishi saida suka shiga sasan ya rike mata hannu ita ko abin ya bata kunya tana son ta kwace amma tana jin nauyi har suka isa inda Ummi take itama lokacin ta idar da sallah tana kan sallaya suka shigo, tare suka durkusa ya sake hannunta ya karasa ya sa kansa akan cinyar ta a hankali yace "Ummi nah ga Nasrin din da nake baki labari"


Ummi ta washe baki tace "Ahh Masha Allah tubar kallah. Lalle kyakykyawace yaki nan 'yata"


Nasrin ta masto tana sinne kanta kasa ta gaishe ta ta amsa


Ummi ta daga kan Yarima tace "kai dan Allah tashi ba girma sai na jiki"


Nasrin tayi dariya tana rufe bakinta


Yarima ya kalli Ummi cikin shagwaba yace "Ummi kin ganta tana min dariya ko"


Tace "Tayi din gododo dakai sai kai ta hawa min cinya. Barin kawo miki abinci nasan baki ci komai ba"



Shiru Nasrin tayi yayin da Ummi kuwa ta tashi ta nufi kicin, bata dade ba ta dawo da Abinci a hannunta da kayan marmari ta zubewa Nasrin a gabanta Nasrin ta fara ci a hankali, Yarima ma yasa hannu yana ci, duk sai taji ta kasa cin abincin Ummi ta kalle shi tace "Kai tashi ka bar min 'yata taci abinci "


Ba musu Yarima ya tashi ya fita


Yana fita Ummi ta jawo abincin gabanta ta fara bawa Nasrin a baki tana cewa "ki saki jikinki tunda Amar ya bani labarinki naji ina sonki da 'ya yanzu tunda na ganki naji na kara sonki, dan Allah kema ki rike ni mahaifiya kinji" 



Nasrin ta daga kai tana amsa yayin da ta tuna mahaifiyarta sai taji tausayin kanta. Saida Ummi ta tabbatar ta koshi sannan ta kyaleta lokacin kuma aka fara kiraye-kirayen sallar isha,tare suka gabatar da sallah suna idar wa Nasrin tace "Ummi ni zan koma nasan Gimbiya zata neme ni"



Ummi tace "To ki gasheta insha Allah ana gama bikin nan zaki dawo nan"


Nasrin aranta tace "hmm baki san Gimbiya da taurin kai bane"



Bangaren Gimbiya kuwa tunda Nasrin ta fita ta fara nimanta a dabata ganta ba tace "wato daga zuwan mu har yariyar nan zata fara gantali ko to bari ta dawo zata ci uwarta. Shiru ba Nasrin ba labarinta koda taga haka sai tambayi sauran kuyangin da suka rako ta suka ce suma basu ganta ba. 

Sai da aka idar da Sallah isha Nasrin ta shigo Gimbiya na ganinta ta dura tama Ashariya tare da cewa daga ina kike


Nasrin tace "Yarima Amar ne ya kira ni"


Gimbiya ta sake dura mata wani ashariyan "ni banga idon mijin nawaba kika rigani ganinsa "



Ta kafa mata rankwashi akai ta kuma marinta Nasrin tas
 rike kuncinta tana hawaye tace "haba Gimbiya kada ki manta idan kin fini da matsayi a gidan duniya baki fini a lahira ba, a wajen Allah duk daya muke bai kamata kina cin zalina da yawa haka ba"




Gimbiya ta kama baki tana kallonta saida takai aya tace "eyeh! Wa'azi kike min? Harkinkai matsayin da zaki na fada min magana," ay ko ta ringa dukanta da hannun bibbiyu har saida sauran bayin da 'yan rakiyanta suka tausaya mata saida ta gaji dan kanta ta kyaleta tana cikin kukan ta wata tsohuwa
Cikin bayin Ta matso da kwanonta tace "Nasrin ga abincin ki duk munci abinci nak..."


Maganan ta makale sakamakon marin da Gimbiya ta watsawa tsohuwar nan. nanTace "ke waye ina magana zaki zo nan. Zaki wani bata abinci, to bari kiji tunda ta bata min rai haka zata kwana da yunwa.  Zaki bace min daga nan ko saina aika ki lahira"


 Da sauri ta koma wajen zamanta yayin da Nasrin ta mike tace "Haba Gimbiya! Idan ni ki dake ni kinyi daidai amma fa wannan ta haife ki"



Gimbiya ta nufi Nasrin ta shako mata wuya tace "yau sai kin fada min uban wa ya tsaya miki"



Nasrin tasa hannunta ta cire na Gimbiya ta koma bayan wata kujera da gudu tana cewa "dan Allah kiyi hakuri bazan sake ba"



Wata kanwar mamar Gimbiya tace "wai mi yasa kika bari baiwa tana miki rashin kunya ne baki aikata ba barzahu ba"



Gimbiya tace "kyaleta ay za'a tafi a barmu dake, kuma kizo zan kwanta abinci kan bazaki ci ba"


Nasrin ta taso ta maso a ranta tana cewa Abincin kan ko kin bani ma bana so



Gadon tahau ta kwanta Gimbiya ta dora kanta a ruwan cikinta barci ya kwashe ta. Nasrin kuwa saida tayi Addu'a ta yi barci



Washe gari da safe bayin gidan mata suka tashi da girki ganin bayin gidan sunyi kadan da yake yawancin su a 'yanta su saboda adalcin masarautar, wannan yasa aka sayo wasu bayin yanzun haka suna hanya,



Sa bayan sallar azhar suka iso Nasrin tana baki rijiya don Gimbiya ta aiketa debo ruwa taga aka shigo da bayin, layi sukayi suna taho, cikin bayin ne Nasrin ta kura wa wata mata ido. Sai kuma ta saki gugan ta fada rijiya tayi wajenta da gudu tana cewa...

Page 1⃣2⃣



"Ummana! Ummana!! Ummana!!!" tana isa wajen ta rumgumeta sai kuma ta saki kuka ita ma Umman kuka ta sake me ban tausayi, wanda yake kula dasu ne yazo ya zabgawa Umman bulala a baya, Nasrin tana tashi ba alamar tsoro a idon ta ta sharara masa mari tace "Waye kai a gabana zata zane min uwa" 


Ay ko ya daga dorunarsa zabga mata Umman ta tare aka zabgawa gadon bayanta. Ya hankada Umman da kafa ya riko wuyan Nasrin yayin da ita kuma ke zare ido



Yarima ne ya iso gurin yana zuwa ya kai masa naushi yace "Baka da imani ne, ko an fada maka nan masarautar ana zalinci ne zaka rikewa wannan 'yar yarinyar wuya"



Yana saketa Nasrin taje ta sake rumgume Ummanta tana kuka 



Yarima ya tsaya kallonsu can kuma ya matsa gurinsu yace "Nasrin me ke faruwa me hadinki da wannan matar"



Saida ta kalle shi ta sake fashewa da kuka tace "Ummana ce fa"


Zaiyi magana jakadiya tazo da sauri ta durkusa tace "Ranka ya dade Sarki yace kazo ka shirya za a fara gudanar da biki"



Be sake magana ba ya tafi can kuma ya juyo yace "Nasrin ina zuwa"


Yana tafiya Umma ta ta tashi tabi sauran 'yan uwanta bayi Nasrin ma ta bita tana kuka



Daga zuwansu ba maganan cin Abinci da hutawa suka fara aiki Nasrin ta tace "Umma koma ki zauna zan miki naki"


Tace "A'a Nasrin kije kawai anjima sai mu hadu kada mujawa kanmu duka"


Nasrin bata yi magana ba sai sa musu hannu da tayi suna aikin tare 



Gimbiya kuwa ta jira Nasrin shiru, ranta ya baci gashi har anzo mata wanka.  lalle da a gida ne da ba abin da zai hana ayi mata dukan gishiri 



Nasrin kuwa ta manta da wata aba wai ita Gimbiya sai aikinta take yi kusa da Ummanta 



Haka Gimbiya aka mata wanka tasha kwalliya da kayan kyale-kyaleta tayi kyau matukar kyau saidai zuciyarta tana tunanin irin hukuncin da zatayi wa Nasrin



Yarima Amar kuwa yana zuwa gun sarki ya bashi kaya irin na Alfarma yace "Kaje ka shirya za'a gudanar da biki kuma za'ayi wasan dokuna ka shirya na saka a ciki kaida dan uwanka Marwan"


Ya risina Yace "To ranka ya dade"



Zuwa yayi yayi wanka yasa ya shirya da kayan da Sarki ya bashi yana fitowa ya hadu da Marwan shima irin kayan ne a jikinsa. Suna hada ido Yarima Amar ya sakar masa murmushi yace "Kayi kyau dan uwa kamar kaine ango"


Marwan kuwa bata fuska yayi yayi tafiyarsa, duk da yasan hakan zai faru baiji dadi ba. Yaso ace sun hada kansa shida dan uwan sa amma shi yaki saboda Sarauta, Alhalin shikan ba son sarautar yake ba.


Fita yayi masallaci dan yaji an fara kiran Sallah la'asar 



Ana idar da sallah aka fara shirya filin taro da ado irin na sarauta aka sa kujeru irin na alfarma inda Amarya da Ango zasu zauna. 


Yarima Amar shine wanda yaje taho da Amaryansa yana shiga sasan bayin ciki suka fara gaishe shi yana amsawa yana murmushi yayinda 'yan uwan Amaryar shi ya gaishe su



Sannan suka tashi suka fita suka bar musu dakin. Suna fita karasawa yayi baki gadon da take kishingide ya sakar mata murmushi yace "Amarya kinyi kyaufa"


Itama murmushi ta sake tasa hannun ta ta shafo sajensa tace "kaima Kayi kyau. Ko yanzu ma zaka hana ni taba jikinka ne"



Kwantar da kai yayi kan kafadar ta yace "Ni na isa in hana ki. Da din ma bana son in karya dokar Ubangijinmu ne" 



Bata ce komai sai sa hannunta da tayi cikin rigarsa 



Ya kalle cikin damuwa yace "tunda na shigo banga Nasrin ba "


Tsaki tayi hade da yatsuna fuska tace "ya muna farin ciki zaka kawo min maganar wata"



Ya bata rai yace "Amma kinsan bana son tsaki ko"



Tace "Kayi hakuri amma yau duk gidan ba ne cetonta a hannuna"


Yace "akan me"


Tace "tun tuni nace ta debo ruwa a rijiya tayi wanka saboda bana son tana biye dani tana warin dauda amma har yanzu bata dawo ba Allah yau sai na kusan kasheta"



Da sauri Yarima ya mike, sai yanzu ya tuna yace wa Nasrin da Ummanta yana zuwa



Gimbiya tace "ina kuma zaka je"


Bai baye mata ba ya sanar da ita komai 


Tace "Ina ruwana da Ummanta nidai yau saina hukuntata"


Yace barinje wajensu


Tace "kazo dauka na ne fa amma kuma zaka sake fita , Yarima tun yanzu ka fara wulakanta ni"


Ta fadi maganar kamar zatayi kuka, shi kuwa gaba daya zuciyarsa ta karye ya sunkuya ya gyara mata mayafi suka fito. Suna fitowa waje ya barke da guda masu busa algeta nayi mawaka na nasu,



Nasrin kuwa da suka gama wankanta tayi ta shiga sasan da suka sauka ta tarar da 'yan uwan Gimbiya a ciki wata daga ciki tace "gantalalliya kin dawo"


Bata ce komaiba ta wuce wajen kayan tasa me dan dama-dama zata fita wata tace "Hala ta samu wani Namijin ne. Ay kamar yadda uwar ta haifeta a yawon bauta haka itama zatana haihuwa"


Ranta ya baci, dan duk abin da zaka mata kada a taba mata mahaifiyarta 

Ta juyo ta kalle taga kanwar maman Gimbiya ce tayi murmushin karfin hali tace "Mahaiyata ba a hanyar zina aka haifeta ba, amma ki tambayi 'yar uwar ki a hanyar me ta haifi Gimbiyar da kuke tare da ita inba Zina ba! Tunda Kwarkwara ce ba matar aure ba, kije cikin garin Samina ki tambayi gidan Malam Ilyas shine mahaifina"


Tana fada ta fuce wata baiwa tace "Nasrin kinsan me kika yi"


Tace "Na sani saidai a kashe ni" 


Ta shige filin bikin tayi inda ta samu Amarya da Ango sun zauna ana ta rawa da waka


Ratsawa tayi cikin jama'a taje ta tsaya a bayan Gimbiya yayin da ita kuma sai hararanta take duk Yarima na lure da su.


Saida aka sha waka da rawa mawaka shida ne suka yuwa ango da Amarya waka aci ansha anyi hani'an daga bisani kuma aka fara wasan dokuna inda mutane da dama sukayi nasu sannan aka kira Yarima shima yazo yayi, aka kawo masa dokinsa da yasa ado sannan aka fara. Inda daga karshe yarima ya lashe wasan


Sai magrib aka tashi inda kowa ya kama gabansa, Yarima kuwa saida ya kai Gimbiya har Sasanta Nasrin na bayan su, yace "Anjima ina nan zuwa fa ki min shiri"


Murmushi tayi tace "An gama miji na"



Ya juya shima yana murmushi yana tafiya ta kalli Nasrin tace "Na dawo kanki, dan uwar ki ina kika je"


Nasrin ta turo baki ta ce "Naga Umma na na gani"



Talatu kanwar maman Gimbiya tace "Wai nikan Sumayya wannan baiwarki ce ko kuma"



Tace "baiwa tace mi kika gani"



Talatu ta fada mata abin da Nasrin tayi.



Gimbiya ta juyo ta kalli Nasrin.

Page 1⃣3⃣


"Waike wacece? Waye ubanki da har kika iya fadan wannan magana"


Cafko wuyan ta tayi ta tana tura ta da baya har saida ta shigar da ita wani daki tace  "Kina shiga min hanci da yawa, badan kada ki bata min wannan daren nawa nawame cike da farin ciki ba wlh da yau a lahira zaki kwana, kada kiga ina kyale ki zan iya kashe in kashe banza, zan fada miki wani abu da baki sa sani ba, kinga Basma da ta mutu nine nan na kashe ta, na kashe me gata na kwana lafiya balle ke baiwar banza marar galihu"



Duk wahalar da taci a hannunta amma tana fada mata maganar kisan Basma sai taji ba wahalar saidai tana sake ta ta fashe da kuka tana cewa "Gimbiya me Basma ta miki kika kashe ta"



Wani gigitaccen mari ta bata saida ta fadi, tace "ki kiyaye ni idan ba haka ba zaki bita"


Rike kuncin ta tayi cikin zuciyarta kuwa tana Allah wadai da halin Gimbiya 



Haka ta fito Nasrin ta biyota a baya tana kuka Talatu tace "lalle Hukuncin kenan"



Gimbiya tace "Masarautar nan basu san da wannan magana kinga idan ta fito Yarima bazai ji dadi ba zaice na munafunce shi"



Talatu tace "haka ne kinyi magana amma ki taka tsan-tsan-tsan da wannan munafukar"



Wani sasan aka bude musu dan su kwana saboda yarima zai kwana dakin amaryar sa, da niyyar da sassafe zasu koma garinsu, 


Daga ita sai Nasrin a dakin, tana zaune a kan gado Nasrin kuwa tana can gefe a kasa a zaune,



Yarima ya yi Sallama ya shigo Nasrin ta gaishe ya amsa cikin murmushi ya karasa kan gadon ya kama hannun Gimbiya yana murmushi itama murmushin take yi, Nasrin kuwa da taga haka sai ta mike zata fita, Gimbiya ta yi saurin cewa "ke ina zaki bayan kin san yanzu zamu kwanta"


Daga Nasrin har Yarima kallon Mamaki suke mata Nasrin ce tace "eh naga zaku kwanta ne shi yasa zan je in kwanta nima"



Gimbiya tace "Dan uwarki idan kika tafi da uwarki zanyi pillow "


A hankali Nasrin tace "Nidai ba uwata ba"



Gimbiya tace "Zagina kike"


Nasrin tace "Nifa bance komai ba"



Yarima yace "wai me kike nufine, ki kyaleta taje ta kwanta mana"



Ta kalle shi tace "ita ce pillow na fa"


"Pillow kuma"


Tace eh


Ya kalli Nasrin yace "Jeki falo ki kwanta"



Ta fita, shi kuwa yace "Gimbiya Sumayya yau gaki ga angonki sai yadda kika yi dashi, amma kafin nan ki tashi muyi sallah raka'a biyu mu nuna godiyar mu ga ubangijin sammai da kasai"


Turo baki tayi tace "nidai gaskiya na gaji"



Yadan bata fuska yace "Sallan zaki ce kin gaji"


Ko da taga haka sai tashi tace "to barin je nayi Alola"



Tana shiga bayi ta wanke hannu da fuska da kafarta ta fito abinta dan gani take alolar ma aiki me yawa ne.



Tana fitowa ya shiga shima yayi alola yazo ya jasu sallah raka'a biyu, wanda ba karamin kokari Gimbiya tayi ba wajen karasa wannan sallah


Ya kama kanta ya yi ta mata Addu'a Sannan ya fara tambayar ta fannin Addinin ta yasha kayan haushi da yaji ko Fatiha bata iya ba, to wannan dame take sallah. Ya fara koya mata ta fara tura baki wai ita ta gaji barci take


Yarima ya dauko gashashen naman rago da yasa aka masa gashi na musamman da madaran shanu yace "to kizo muci abinci"


Ya ajiye ya debi naman kadan ya tsiyayi madaran a dan karamin cofi ya fita dashi falo dan kaiwa Nasrin, yana zuwa ya tarar da ita ta hada kai da gwiwa tana kuka, hannunta yadan taba ta dago kai yace "Lafiya"



Cikin shasheka tace "so nake inje gurin Ummana kuma kofa a rufe yake"


Yace "kiyi hakuri gari ya waye yanzu dare yayi ko kinje can ma sun rufe kofa"


Nasrin tace "ay gunda suke ba kofa"


Shiru ya tashi ya nufi kofa dan bude mata sai kuma ya tuna da Marwan ba mamaki yana nan kuma a wannan daren idan ya ganta me kwatanta sai Allah, Sai ya juyo yace "Nasrin Ina jiye miki tsoron hanyar nan kinga kina mace bai kamata kibi gurin ba, akwai mungwayen mutane a hanyar masu so su lalata yaran mutane kuma ba bana son wani abu ya same ki"


Ta haminci abin da yake nufi, kalle Yarima yana kaunarta da har yake gudun haka ya faru da ita


Tsinto muryar sa tayi yana cewa "idan kuma kina son kije muje in raka ki"


Murmushi tayi tace "Na hakura wlh gobe da safe naje"



Mika mata naman da madara yayi yace "gashi kici ko"



Ta amsa tace "Nagode"



Komawa yayi ga Gimbiya ya same ta zaune yace "ki hakuri kina jirana ko


Tace "ina kaje"


Baiji magana ba sai jawota da yayi kan cinyarsa ya dauki tsokar nama ya sa mata a baki, ta tauna tana lumshe ido dan ya mata dadi, haka yayi tayi har ya tabbatar ta koshi sannan bata madaran tasha, shima ya fara ci saida ya koshi shima yasha madaran, sannan ya dagata ya kaita kan gado yace "barin je nayi wanka sai inzo mu kwanta "


Tura baki tayi shi kuma yayi kamar be ganta baya fada bayi


Yana shiga ta fita falo ta samu Nasrin lokacin har ta cinye naman da madaran, ta fara barci. Ta kwada mata duka a cinya da sauri Nasrin ta mike tana sosa wajen 



Gimbiya tace "dan uwarki muje mu kwanta "



Ba musu Nasrin ta tashi ta bita a baya ta suna shiga Nasrin taja ta tsaya tace "To shi Yarima a ina zai kwanta"


Tace" Dallah kwanta ni na gaji da jiranki"



Ba musu ta hau kan gadon ta kwata Gimbiya tayi yadda takeyi.


Yarima yana fitowa ya zaro ido yace "me haka!"



Gimbiya ta kalle shi tayi murmushi tace "in matsa maka ne kaima ka kwanta a kanta"


Yace "Allah ya kiyaye min, me ma wannan ke nufi, sarauta ce ko zalinci"


Gimbiya tayi murmushi tace "wannan irin *IZZAR MULKI* na kenan, tunda na saye ta a haka muke kwana, yanzu kuma munyi aure da kai zaka iya zuwa mu kwana a kanta tare"


Yarima yace "Allah ya tsare ni da wannan, kuma bari kiji indai kina son mu ringa kwana gado daya dake dole kibar wannan zalinci"


Tace "Haba Yarima bai kamata kana fada min magana gabanta ga zata iya raina ni"


Yace "Nasrin tashi ki koma falo ki kwanta"


Tayi yunkurin tashi Gimbiya ta riko ta "Dan uwarki na baki umurnin tashi"



Da sauri Nasrin ta dawo ta kwanta


Yarima yace "Indai a haka zaki kwana to ni saidai in kwana a falo"


Tabe baki tayi ta ce "jeka"


Baice komai ba ya wuce falo abin sa, yayin da Nasrin kuwa sai tsine wa Gimbiya take a ranta.



Da Asuba ya tashi ya shiga ciki dan ya tashe su, Nasrin ya samu tayi raka'atainin fajr tana Addu'a, yayin da Gimbiya kuma an baje a gado sai shakar barcin ta take yi. 


Hawa kan gadon yayi yana shafa fuskarta a hankali, ta bude ido tana turo baki ta ce "haba Yarima kuma sai kazo ka tashe ni, ina barci na"



Yace "Asuba tayi ki tashi kiyi Sallah bakiga Nasrin karama da ita ta tashi. balle ke"


Sake turo baki tayi tace "To nida ita dayane, baka ga ita baiwa ce kuma wahalalliya"



Yarima yace "kema ay baiwa ce tunda kina da ubangijin ki"


Zatayi magana ya katseta yace "kije kiyi Sallah ni na tashi mallaci"



Yana gama fada ya fita. Yana fita ta koma ta kwanta


Yarima kuwa Ana idar da sallah wajen Sarki ya wuce dan gaishe shi, dama haka yake yi kullum


Yana zuwa falon ya isah gun mahaifin nasa ya gaishe, bayan sun gama gaisawa ne Sarki yace "Amar yau fa kai ango ne bai kamata ka fito a yau ba, ka koma gun matarka"


Yarima yace "Ranka ya dade, daga masallaci nake fa"


Sarki yace "Naji jeka gun matarka ka barta ita daya"


Ba musu ya tashi ya tafi gun Umminsa bayan sun gaisa ita cewa tayi, "tashi ka tafi gun matarka "


Nan ma tashi yayi ba musu ya tafi koda ya shiga samun Nasrin yayi a falo ta jingina da bango tana ta daga kai sama tana motsi da kafarta bakinta kuwa tana karatun alkur'ani mai girma cikin suratul Bakara, Zama yayi akan kujera dan ya saurareta, amma yana zama sai tayi shiru ta mike zata koma daki yace "Nasrin ina zaki je"


Tace "daki zan koma"



Yace "ki zauna kici gaba ina sauraron ki ne"



Ta koma ta zauna taci gaba shiko ya lunshe ido yana sauraron zazzakar muryarta,


Saida ta idar tayi Addu'a suka shafa tare yace "Nasrin dama haka kike da hadda, wani malamine ya koya miki haka"



Tace "Abbana"


Yace "Allah sarki Allah ya jikansa"


Tace "Am in 


Yace "kinga ni har yanzu banyi sauke hadda ba"



Tace "zaka sauke ne insha Allah "


Tashi yayi ya shiga ciki Gimbiya tana kwance ya kwanta gefenta yayin da Nasrin kuma ta fara gyara gidan, kafin ta fara hada musu kumallo saida ta gaba tsab tayi wanka ta dauki tsofin kayanta tasa, zata fita kenan Gimbiya ta fito mika ta ce "ke ina zaki je"


Nasrin tace "wajen Ummana"


Tace "Da izinin wa"


Nasrin tace "kiyi hakuri yanzu zan dawo"



Gimbiya ta ta mata nuni da yatsa akan tayi ciki


Nasrin ta dawo ciki can kuma tace "Ina bukatar amin wanka"


Nasrin ta tashi ta fita can sai gata da wasu mata biyu tana shigowa ta zauna su kuma suka shiga da Gimbiya daki yayin da Yarima kuma yana ta barci.


Cire mata kaya suka yi suka sa mata rigar wanka suka shiga da ita bayi, Cikin barcinsa yaji motsin ruwa a bayi, ya bude ido da Addu'ar tashi daga barci a bakinsa, ya tashi ya zauna a tunaninsa Gimbiya ce ke wanka ita daya


Yauna yana jiran fito warta domin shima yana son yin wankan


Can yaga sun fito da ita, sun sata a tsakiyar su, sun zauna ita suna goge mata ruwan jikinta


Yace "Ba lafiya ne"


Daya daga cikinsu tace "Allah ya ja kwanan Yarima wanka mukayi a Gimbiya"


Yace "A'uzubillah! Wanka!! Ita jaririya ce"


Gimbiya ta kalle shi tace "Nifa kullum wankan ake min ko a gida ma"



Yarima yace "Wankan ma sai an miki. Kai gaskiya Sarauta bata yi ba"



Tace "kada ka manta gidan ku nada Sarauta "


Yace "harda ta gidan mu, dan ni banso na fito gidan sarauta ba saboda bana sonta"



Gimbiya shiru tayi yayi da matan suka tubeta zindir suna shafa mata mai, haushi ya kama Yarima ya tashi ya shiga bayi yayi wanka lokacin daya fito har sun gama shiryata sun fita ya kalleta yace "Ina son muyi magana dake "



Tayi fari da Ido tace "Ina jinka"



Yace "Idan kina son mu zauna lafiya dake ki ajiye wannan sarautar taji, maganar wanka kina yi da kanki, kwanciya akan dan adam shima ban yadda dashi ba, yanzu idan ina da bukatar ki a gaban Nasrin zan nemeki, shin zaki yadda A sadu dake a gabanta"


Ta juya idonta tace "zan yadda saboda baiwa tace"
