  🌐 IZZAR MULKI
           💓💓💓💓

Story 
        &
          Writing by
UMMU MUFEED
      (Mrs Aliyu)


*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 1⃣4⃣



Yace "Saboda baki da kunya dai, idan anyi magana sai ki wani ce baiwa, kema ay baiwarce ko kina tunanin haka kawai kika fado baki da Ubangiji. Nidai Abubuwan da kike inma sarautar gidanku ne tace haka, To fa wani masarautar kika zo saboda ha ka ki canja bazan yadda da wannan kafircin ba"



Kuka ta fashe dashi cikin kuka tace "yanzu Yarima saboda ina gidanku shine zaka wulakanta ni"



Tuni zuciyarsa ta karye Saboda shi a rayuwarsa ya tsani kukan mace, rungumeta yayi yana lallashin ta, daga bisani yace "barin shirya muje mu gaida mai martaba"



Saida ya shirya tsab sannan ya kama hannunta, suka yi inda Nasrin ta shirya musu abinci suka karya bayan sun kintsa ya kama hannunta, yasan yanzu haka Sarki yana Fada, saboda haka fada suka wuce, mutane cike a fada ciki harda Ummin Amar da kuma Umman Marwan da Marwan din, 



Suna zuwa Fada suka durkusa suka kwashi gaisuwa gaban Sarki, sannan ya je gun Umminsa ita ma ya gaisheta. Ya zaga gun Umman Marwan wanda tun shigowarsu take musu mungun kallo, yaje ya gaishe ta. Amma bata amsa ba yasan haka zata faru Saboda haka ya wuce gun Marwan ya durkusa yace "Dan Uwa ga kanwar ka"


Shima kauda kai yayi amma cikin zuciyarsa ya kyasa da Gimbiya, kuma ya kuduri niyya a ransa saiya dandana zumarta,


Dawowa yayi gefen Umminsa da Sumayya ya ajiyeta ya kama hannunta ya hada na Sumayya  ya ce a hankali wanda ba wanda yaji sai ita "Ummi ga amanar matana nan"


Harara ta matso masa tare da damke hannun Gimbiya kamar tana tsoron kada wani ya kwace mata ita.


Gun Me martaba yayi ya zauna ta daya gefen hagu dinsa yayin da Marwan yana gefen damansa, Sarki ya sha kansa yace "Alhamdulillah yanzu dana ya zama cikekken namiji, tunda yayi aure, hakan yasa zanyi murabus nan da wata uku zan nada dana Yarima Amar a matsayin Sarki"


Marwan da Ummansa, Ummi da Gimbiya, har Amar kallon mamaki suke masa yayin da Fadawa suke masa kirari 


Amar ne ya fara magana yace muryan sa na rawa "Ranka ya dade bazan iya rike sarauta ba  bana son inje lahira a daddaure, ranka ya dade ga dan uwana shi yafi dacewa da sarauta saboda shine babba dani"


Caraf Marwan yace "haka ne Ranka ya dade saboda nine dan Sarki, kuma dan Sarauniya"


Umman sa tace "wannan haka yake, dana ya dace da sarauta bani ba"


Sarki kalli Ummi yace "ke  kuma me kika ce Aisha"


Kanta a sunkuye yayin da har yanzu tana rike da hannun Sumayya tace "kai ya dace ka zaba, ranka ya dade mudai Adalci muke bukata a masarautar mu"



Sarki ya juya ga sumayya yace "'yata Sumayya kema yanzu kin zama 'yar dangi me kika ce"


Kanta ta sunkuyar kamar mutuniyar kirki tace "bansan abin da zance ba, mudai a zaba mana mai Adalci"



Sarki ya juya ga waziri yace "kai me kace"

Yace "Yarima Amar shi ya dace saboda yana da tausayin talakawa"


Sarki ya juya ga  sauran yan Fada yace "ku fa me kukace"


Kowa yayi tsuru-tsuru da ido yana tsoron hukuncin Marwan
Sarki ya fahimci haka saboda haka yace "Alhamdulillah wannan ya nuna mana cewa zakayi Adalci idan ka zama sarki, saboda ka nuna mana kana tsoron Allah"


Kai kuma Marwan ina me baka hakuri abun da nake so da kai ka hada kai da dan uwanka,



Yarima Amar zaiyi magana Sarki ya katse shi ta hanyar daga masa hannu yace "ya isa! Mun gama magana"


Shiru yayi kansa sunkuye yayin da idonsa suka yi ja



Gimbiya Sumayya kuwa murna take cikin ranta zata zama matar sarki, kuma tasan zai nadata sarauniya har ta fara tunanin irin sarautar da zata aiwatar 

Marwan da Ummansa tashi suka yi cikin fushi suka bar fada


Amar ma fita yayi, ummi ma ta kama hannun Sumayya suka fita, 



Nasrin kuwa tana ganin sun fita gun Ummanta tayi tana zuwa ta fada jikin ta tace "Ummana nayi kewarki, naso inzo gunki mu kwana amma anki barina"



Tace "Nasan baza'a a barki ba ay nima hakuri nayi na kwanta. Ni godiya ta ga Allah da Allah yasa muka sake haduwa"


Nasrin tace "nima baki ji dadin da nake ji ba"



Umma tace "yaya zamanki a nan garin"



Nasrin ta bata labarin zuwanta hannun Gimbiya har zuwa i yanzu da wahalhalun da take bata da kuwa halarcin da Yarima ya nuna mata



Umma tace "Nidai abin da zan ce dake ki daina mata rashin kunya, kuma yanzu duk zalincin da take miki zata daina saboda Yarima "


Nasrin tace "To naji Umma, nima bani labarn bayan rabuwar mu"



Umma tace "ki tashi ki tafi kada su dawo suga bakya nan. 


Ba musu Nasrin ta tashi ta tafi


Amar kuwa Sassan Ummi ya wuce ummin da Gimbiya ma nan suka yi, suna zuwa suna saka same shi ya zauna kasa ya kifa kai kan kujera yana kuka,

Da sauri Ummi ta sake hannun  Gimbiya tazo ta kama shi ta dago shi sai ga hawaye tace "kuka kuma Amar kamar mace "



Ya dago da jajayen idonsa yace "Ummi ina tsoron Allah ya tada ni ranar kiyama na kuntatawa wani lokacin da nake gudanar da *MULKI* Ummi bana son sarauta, kisa baki a bawa Marwan"


Da sauri ta rufe masa baki tace "bazan sa baki ba, saboda kai ka dace ka gaji mahaifin ka, kaine zaka yi adalci ga talakawan wannan garin"



Yace "Ummi wai baki san ta yaya Allah zai dagani ba"



Tace "insha Allah a cikin bayi salihai za daga ka, kada ka damu zan ci gaba da maka Addu'a kaji"



Shiru yayi baice komai ba ganin haka yasa Ummi ta kwantar dashi akan cinyarta tana shafa gashin kansa


Saida taga ya huce tace "To kuje sassanku da Yamma zanzo muyi wata maganar"


Ba musu suka tafi


Bangaren Su Marwan kuwa yana zuwa sassan Umman sa ya fashe da kuka yana cewa "Umma kina ganin bambamci ga fili ko, a ga ban jama'a yake cewa zaiba dansa sarauta bayan yasan nine babba, ni ya dace da Sarauta ba shiba"


Ta dafa kafadarsa tace "kwantar da hankalinka basai yayi sarautar ba mu gani, ina raye bazan taba bari wani banza ya gaji sarautar ba sa kai, kaine magajin Sarki"


Marwan ya goge hawayensa yace yana dariya "kashe shi zakiyi"


Tace "ba kisa, yana raye zaka hau kujeran mahaifin ka, naje gun bokanmu na masa bayani kafin haka ta faru saboda dama nasan haka zata faru, yace idan hakan ta faru in je in fada masa, to gobe da sassafe zan shirya inje"


Marwan yayi dariyan farin ciki yace "Allah ya barmin ke umma na, nasan zaki iya komai saboda farin cikina"


Da yamma garin yayi ni'ima musamman yadda bishiyoyin gidan sukai koraye, ga tsuntsaye akai suna ta kuka me ratsa zuciyar me sauraro


A wannan lokacin Yarima Amar da Gimbiya  suna zaune a harabar sasan su, yayi da Nasrin ke gefe, Ummi ce ta shigo da kuyangu biyu a bayanta, suna ganinta duka suka mike har Nasrin, Yarima ne ya shiga da ita, yayin da ita kuma ta tsayarda kuyangin nata a waje, Tana zama Nasrin ta kawo mata kayan marmari da hadin lemon data hada, yayin da Gimbiya tunda ta zauna akan kujera take cin inabi, a takaice ta gaishe ta, Ummi ta amsa bata nuna damuwa ba, Yarima da Nasrin ma suka gaishe ta, Nasrin zata fita Ummi tace "Nasrin dawo ki zauna, zuwana a kanki ne ay"


Ta dawo ta zauna yayin da Yarima yana kasa gefen kafafunta ya dora hannunsa akan cinyarsa itaku Gimbiya tana kan kujera ta dora kafa daya kan daya tana cin Inabi


Ummi tace "Kasancewar yadda nake son Nasrin, shine yasa nazo domin ku bani ita, zan kawo muku masu aiki."

Page 1⃣5⃣



Yarima Amar ya shafa kansa yace "Ummina ba abin da zaki nema ki rasa, indai Nasrin ce mun bar miki"


Tayi murmushi tana shirin kiran Nasrin sai tsintar muryan gimbiya tayi tana cewa "Ban yadda ba, nice keda ita ba kai ba"


Gaba ki dayan su suka jiyo suna kallonta, Ummi ce ta boye mamakin ta da cewa "To shikenan ki siyar min ita zan biya ki ko nawa kike bukata"



Gimbiya ta sake nade kafa tace "Ban siyo ta dan na siyar da ita ba saboda haka, ki kyale min kayata"



Yarima ya mike yace "Sumayya wannan din fa Ummita ce, Mahaifiyata ko baki gane ta bane"



Ta juya ta kalli Nasrin tace "Ke zo ki samin tuffa din nan a baki na"

Tana magana tana yatsina baki tare da nuna tuffa din da yatsa



Nasrin ta zo ta sunkuyo ta dauko ta sa mata a bakinta ta gatsa sannan ta juya ta kalli Yarima tace "Na sani, ba kai ka nuna min ita a fada ba, to ay Ummin kace ba saboda haka, ka fada mata baza ta samu bukatar ta ba, idan bayi take so taje ta nima banda tawa"



Ummi tace "ku kuma haka ake gudanar da sarautar a garinku harda rashin girmama iyayen miji"



Gimbiya tace "To ay Iyayen mijine ba..."


Yarima ya katseta ta hanyar daga mata hannu tare daka mata tsawa "ya isa!!" ba gimbiya ba da aka yi dan ita har Ummi da Nasrin saida suka razana, yaci gaba da cewa...
"Ummin tawa kike fadawa magana, Ka mar sa'arki?, kash nayi nadamar aurenki Sumayya, daga daurin aurenki zuwa yanzu banga wani alheri sai...


Da sauri Ummi ta katse shi ta hanyar rufe masa baki tace "kul na sake jin wannan magana, kayi fatan ka samu Alheri nan gaba"



Yace "Amma fa Ummi..."


Tace "ku daidaita da matarka ni na tafi anjima ka same ni"


Tana gama fada tabar falon saida ya tabbatar Ummi da kuyanginta sunbar harabar gidan ya daga kai ya kalli Gimbiya, wata tsanarta da dana sanin auren ta ne ya sauko masa, tana dagowa suka hada ido, Mari ya bata ta bayan hannu ya same ta da kyau kuwa ya sake kai mata wani ya sake ta zai sake kai mata na ukune yaji an rike masa hannu, yana juyawa yaga Nasrin na hawaye tace "Dan Allah kayi hakuri"


Ya fincike hannunsa ya ci gaba da dukan gimbiya ita ko sai ihu take, Nasrin ce tazo ta shiga tsakaninsu tace "Yarima Dan Allah kayi hakuri Gimbiya bata saba da duka dan Allah kada ka ji mata ciwo"


Kallonta yayi yaga yadda take kuka tsakaninta da Allah komawa yayi da baya yana huci, ya zube akan kujera. Na nuna miki so da kauna wannan yasa na auro na kawo ki, ke kuma kinzo kina son ki maida min da Ummina tamkar Sa'ar ki, an fada miki son da nake miki zai sa in bari ki Wulakanta min mahaifiya ce, yanzu kin sa son da na ke miki ya koma kiyayya, na tsane ki! Na tsane ki Sumayya!!"

Tana jin ya kira kalmar tsana takara rushewa da wani sabon kukan




Nasrin kuwa koda taci Nasaran rabasu komawa gefe tayi tana tunanin rashin kunya irin na Gimbiya da Zuciya irin na Yarima, Lalle yau ta karyata maganan hausawa da suke cewa *Amarya bata laifi* yaudai ga Amaryar Yarima wacce yake so ta masa laifi ya lakada mata duka.

Bangaren Ummi kuwa tana isa sassanta ta fada duniyar tunani, "dama irin matar da danta ya aura kenan ba tarbiya ba ganin girman na gaba da ita, lalle indai haka ne zan sake yiwa Yarima aure, amma ina za'a samu Yarinya kalar wacce nake so." ta daga kai sama can kuma ta cije lebe tace "Allah ya shirya"



Yarima yana fita Gimbiya ta tashi ta rufe kofa ta jawo Nasrin tace "Dan ubanki saboda ke aka dake ni ko, kuma a gaban ki to akanki zan rama ta kama dukan tun Nasrin na kuka har ta kasa sai nishi da take yi, saida ta gaji dan kanta ta kyaleta.

Saida aka yi Sallar Isha Yarima ya isa sassan Ummi yana Isa ya durkusa a gabanta "Ummina kiyi hakuri bansan haka take ba kuma na mata hukunci wlh nayi nada..."


Tayi saurin rufe masa baki "ni banyi Nadama ba Yarima ina son kaima kada kayi nadama saboda idan ka dubi Sumayya Yarinya ce kuma ta fito daga gidan sarauta, a ko yaushe tana sawa ne a mata ba wai ita ake sawa saboda haka kada wannan ya dame ka, kace ka mata hukunci Amar banso ba nasanka da zuciya kada kaje ka mata mummunar hukunci, kaje ka lallashe ta kaji, ka sake hakuri."


Baice komaiba saima gyara wa da yayi yasa kansa a bisa cinyar ta ita kuma ta fara shafa sumar kansa tana fada masa kalamai masu dadi, abinka da mahaifiya duk sai yaji zuciyarsa tayi sanyi, ya tuna irin dukan da yayi wa  Sumayya, sai yaji tausayinta yariryar da bata da kowa sai shi yanzu haka yasan tana can tana zaman kadaici duk da yasan Nasrin tana nan



Ya daga kai yace "Ummina Ina makullin dayan dakin nan nawa"


Tayi masa kallon tuhuma, tuni ya gano nufinta ya sosa keya yace "Nasrin ce zata kwana aciki "


Da sauri ta tashi tace "Idai Nasrin ce barin dauko maka amma da kaine bazan bayar ba"


Yace "ni me nayi"


Tace "kai kana da dakin matar ka"


Gimbiya kuwa tana zaune tana tunanin ko ina Yarima ya shiga dan gaskiya tana son sa, zata bashi hakuri daga baya zata san yadda zata yi dan bazata zauna wannan uwar tasa tana shiga mata hanci da kudunduno ba, 

Tashi tayi ta fito ta wuce Nasrin a falo ta fito daga sassan baki daya tana kallon yadda fitilan solar ya haska filin yadda take yayin da can nesa keda duhu ko ba'a fada maka ba kasa wajen bayi,


'Yar rumfa ta gani da kujeru ta isa gurin ta zauna.



Marwan kuwa daga nesa ya hango mace cikin shigar sarauta saidai bai san ko wace ce ba, duk da haka ya fara washe baki ya isa gurin yana murmushi, ya zauna a daya daga cikin kujeran yace "Amma ke bakuwa ce daga wace masarautar kike"


Ta dago kai ta kalle shi tuni ya gano wacece

Sai ya wayince yace "keko ina Amar ya tafi ya barki ke daya, kodai sun fara nuna miki halin su ne shi da mahaifiyarsa"


Da sauri tace "Dama suna da wani hali ne"


Yace "Tab ay ni tunda aka kawo ki, naji tausayin ki ta kama ni dan kin shigo gidan wahala"


Da sauri ta mike ta nuna kirjinta tace "wlh basu isa ba, daga shi har ita, sai na sasu a tafin hannu na, wannan abin da suka min ma dan basu same ni akan shiri bane amma wlh da basu isa, kuma wannan shine na karshe."


Marwan yace "Wai me suka mikine"


Ta kwashe masa komai ta fada masa. Yace "kin fara gani kenan to wlh sai kin tashi tsaye akan su basu da mutumci, yanzu ki tashi muje gun Ummana kinga zamanki anan ke daya baiyi ba ga dare"



Tace "To"



Ya tashi ya tafi ta bishi a baya,   koda suka isa sassan a falo ya ajiyeta ya shiga ciki ya samu tana shirin kwanciya yace "Umma kinyi bakuwa"


Tace "wacece da daddaren nan ke son ganin sarauniya"



Yace "Sumayya Matar Amar "



Da sauri ta mike tace "me tazo yi"



Ya kwashe mata komai ya fada mata ya kara da cewa "Umma na lura yarinyar wawiya ce ta hannunta ma zamu iya cima burin mu"


Tayi shewa tace "ka cika makiri dana, lallai barewa baza tayi gudu danta yayi rarrafe ba"



Yace "yanzu ki tashi muje ki lallashe ta"


Ta tashi suka fito a tare da murmushi a fuskarta tana zuwa ta zauna kusa da Sumayya tace "Amarya sannunki da zuwa wajen Sarauniya"



Sumayya ta gaishe ta ta amsa da sakin fuska 


Sarauniya tace "Saifa kina yin hakuri da sirkarki dan ba mutuniyar kirki bane daga ita har danta"


Gimbiya ta kalleta tace "Ay kuwa a shirye nake dasu dan wlh bazan yadda ta taka ni in kyale ba yanzu ma likimo nayi saina gama karantar halayensu tukun"



Sarauniya tayi sake da baki tana kallon Gimbiya ta gano ba kunya ko kadan a idon ta kuma tayi farin ciki da haka

Tace "yawwa 'yata kada ki raga musu, nima zan taya "


Gimbiya tace "Ay na gode Allah dana hadu daku dan na lura Ummin Yarima munguwace"



Sarauniya tace "yanzu dai ki tashi kije ki kwanta gobe zamu hadu amma kada ki bari su sani"



Tace "To"


Sarauniya tasa kuyangu suka rakata har bakin sassanta sannan suka juya


Tana shiga taga Nasrin a gefe guda tace "Ina son zanyi Wanka"


Nasrin ta mike ta fita can sai gata da kuyangun Wanka suka kamata suka shiga da ita bayi suka mata wanka bayan sun fito ne suka mata shirin barci suka fita.


Yana shigowa Nasrin ya hango a takure a gefe daya ya isa gunta ya dan tsuguna yace "Nasrin akwai matsala ne"


Tayi murmushin karfin hali tace "Babu"


Yace "To tunanin me kike"



Tace "Ummana"



Tausayinta ya kama shi yace "kiyi hakuri gobe zaki yini gun Ummanki kinji"


Ta daga masa kai


Shiga yayi dakin da Gimbiya take ya same bakin gado da kayan barci a jikinta, ya isa gunta ya zauna gefenta ya sa hannu a kugunta yana sunsunar wuyanta yace "Kiyi hakuri kinsan Ina sonki ban so na taba fatar jikin ki da duka ba, zuciya ce bata da kashi, musamman da kika taba min Ummina "


Kukan kissa ta fashe dashi cikin kuka tace "nice zan baka hakuri na gane kuskure na dana taba Ummi, dan Allah ka yafe min mijina"


Rumgumeta yayi yana dan bubbuga bayanta ita ko a zuciyarta sai tsinewa Ummi take yi, 


Suna haka ne ya fara sauya mata salo yana murzan jikinta itama ta fara mayar masa da martani, saida suka gurji junan su Yarima yana cike da bukatuwa har ya cire kayansa yayin da yake kokarin rufe su da bargo Gimbiya tayi wuf ta tashi ta nufi falo tana zuwa tace da Nasrin "wai baki san lokacin barci yayi ba"



Nasrin tayi mata kallon mamaki can tace "Gimbiya naga kina tare da mijinki ne fa, kuma ba kyau fa"


Mari ta kwada mata "wa'azi zaki min dan ubanki, kizo yanzun nan Yarima na jirana"


Ba shiri Nasrin tabi bayan ta suka nufi dakin



Yarima kuwa tunda ta tashi bai rufe jikinsa ba yana jiran dawowanta dan baiyi zaton gun Nasrin zata je ba dan yayi zaton tabar maganan Nasrin din, sai ganin sun yayi tare da Sauri Nasrin ta kauda kanta tayi ta rufe idonta saboda ganinsa da tayi daga shi sai gajeren wando 


Shi kuwa janyo bargo yayi yana rufe jikinsa yana cewa "Subhanallah"

Wannan page din nakine Hajarancy ina jin dadin yadda kike comment da sharhi Allah yabar kauna

Page 1⃣6⃣



Gimbiya tace "zan tada kai da ita ne"



Yace "ni kuma fa ya zaki dani"


Tace "kamar yaya"


Yace "Hakkina nake so a wannan dare"



Tace "ay bance zan hana ka ba"


Ta finciko Nasrin ta tura ta kan gadon, Nasrin taki hawa tace "Abin da kike kokarin yi kafirci ne, kiyi hakuri"


Yace "ay za'ay Nasrin mu kwana da Nasrin a dakin nan"


Tace "Baiwa tace, ita ma ta zama baiwarka in kana so ma zaka iya amsar hakkin ka a wajen ta amma ba zancen yi ciki"



Nasrin da Yarima suka mata kallon mamaki, lalle sun tabbatar akwai jahilci akanta



Yarima yace "bakya kishi"



Tace "kishin me zanyi dan Allah ni barci nake ji muje mu kwanta"


Ta tura Nasrin, Nasrin kuwa taki hawa gadon, ta tura ta iya karfinta tare da bata wani mari sai ta fada kan Yarima. Da sauri ta sauko tace "Wlh saidai ki kashe ni amma bazan sabawa Addini na ba"


Zata sake marin ta Yarima ya rike hannunta yace "tsaya ni barin je can dakin na kwanta tunda na amso makullin a hannu Ummina, ku ku kwana anan "



Ya tattara bargon a jikinsa ya sauka daga kan gadon  yace "Nasrin hau ki kwanta kinji"


Tausayinsa ya kamata tahau gadon shiko ya tafi yana da dingishi saboda wani ciwon Mara da yake ji


Ita kuwa ko a jikinta kwanciya tayi kan Nasrin 


Yarima kuwa yana zuwa ya bude dakin dama a gyare yake a daddafe ya shiga bayi yayi wanka ya dauro alola yayi nafila raka'a biyu ya hau gadon ya kwanta saidai barci yaki daukansa sai azababben ciwon mara dake damunsa tun yana daurewa har ya fara murkususu a kan gadon, lokaci daya kuwa ya fita hayyacin addua ya dinga karantowa a bakinsa, har aka fara kiran sallah farko, da rarrafe ya shiga bayi ya dauro alola azo yayi raka'atainil fajr, koda aka kira sallah shiga masallaci bai iya zuwa masallaci yayi a gurin, bayan ya idar ne ya koma gado wanda a lokacin yayi sauki kadan kadan ne ke masa,



Yana kwance yana jiyo karatun Nasrin ya lunshe ido yana sauraro har barci ya kwashe, karfe 7:00 ciwo ya dawo sabo yana son ya aiki Nasrin ta kira masa Umminsa amma ba dama saboda ko tashi bazai iya yi ba



Nasrin kuwa saida ta gama aikinta tas lokacin Gimbiya ma an mata wanka ta fito dan ta karya ta kalli Nasrin tace "Ke Yarima bai fito bane "


Nasrin tace "ban sani ba ko ina kicin ya fito, amma da nake zaune anan banga ya fito ba"



Wucewa tayi dakin ba tare da tace komai ba ta murda kofar ta tura taga gadon ba kowa sai zanin gadon daya yamutse da bargon ta dawo tace "Yarima ya fita kije ki gyara dakin kafin nan ki hada min kayan kari ki kai min daki bazan iya jiransa ba dan yunwa nake ji"


Yarima kuwa yana kwance ya kasa ko motsi saboda azababben ciwo yana kallo ta turo kofa 


Nasrin kuwa saida ta gama mata komai sanan ta nufi dakin, tana shiga kai tsaye kan gadon tayi, yayi da Yarima kuwa yana kallonta, tana zuwa bargon ta janye sai taga mutun da sauri ta ja da baya sai kuma ta durkusa tace "Ran Yarima ya dade lafiyanka kuwa"


Cikin karfin hali ya kama yace "Nasrin ke kika san sirrina tsakanina da Matata, bana son Ummina ta sani hankalinta zai tashi, ina son kije ki fada wa Ummanki matsalata ko akwai maganin da zata bani"


Nasrin tace "Menene Matsalarka"


Yace "Ciwon Mara ke damuna"


Tace "shine baka son Ummi ta sani naga lalura ce"


Yace "Nasrin wannan ciwon ba kowa ake so ya saniba.

Tace "lalura ce fa"


Ya lura bata fahimta ba yasa yace "kije ki fada wa Ummanki sirrina sannan ki fada mata ina ciwon mara ita zata gane,"


Zatayi magana ya sa dan yatsarsa a baki yace "dan Allah kije kada na mutu"


Da sauri ta tashi tafita da gudu tayi ida Ummanta take tana isa kuwa ta hangota tana zuwa ta rumgumeta tace "Ummana nayi kewarki"


Umma tayi murmushi tace "Nima nayi kewarki 'yata"


Nasrin tace "Umma akwai Matsala"


Tace "tame fa


Nasrin tace "Yarima ba lafiya"


Tace "meke damunsa"


Nasrin ta kwashe komai ta fada mata har maganar da suka yi da Yarima 


Umma taji tausayin sa sannan tace "muje mu samo masa magani kikai masa"


Ta bayan lambu akwai ciyayi da yawa dan haka nan suka shiga basu sha wani wahala ba suka samu ganyen da suke nima suna dawowa ta wanke shi sannan tasa a turmi me kyau ta kirba ta juye a kwarya me kyau sannan tasa ruwa a ciki ta matse lemon tsami ta sa ludayin dima ta juya sannan ta mika wa Nasrin tace "gashi ki kai masa ki tabbatar yasha"


Nasrin ta amma ta tafi tanazuwa dakin ta samu Yarima ya kama cikinsa yana kela amai, Nasrin kuwa bata san lokacin ta ajiye kwaryan maganin ta hau kan gadon ta kama shi ba,  tana jera masa sannu, tace "barin kira Gimbiya"


Yace Aa ki barta, ko tazo ba abin da zata iya min saidai ta bata min rai, yanzu ki je ki hada min shayi in sha sai insha maganin "



Tace "barin to in hada maka ruwan wanka kaje kayi sai kwashe zanin gadon kaga ya baci"


Da karfin hali ya tashi ya cire kayansa ya rage daga shi sai gajeran wando, yayin da ita kuwa ta shiga bayi ta hada masa ruwan wanka, koda ta fito kallo daya ta masa ta kauda kanta,


Ya shiga ita kuma ta fara gyara dakin, koda ya fito dakin fes, ta canja zanin gadon ya kalleta ya sunkuya yana kama cikinsa saboda ciwon maran tare da hawa tsakiyar gadon yace "ki hada min shayin"



Ta fita bata dade ba ta dawo da shayi me kauri a ta mika masa ya sha amma bada yawa ba, ta mika masa maganin yadan kurba kada sai ya kalleta ya shagwabe fuska yace "Da daci fa Nasrin "



Ta dan zauna a bakin gadon tace "ka daure kasha kaji"


Yace to


Ya sake kurban kadan ya kalleta yace "Nasrin bazan iya ba daci"



Ta amsa kwaryan ta karasa hawa kan gadon ta durkusa gwiwar sa tasa dayan hannunta a wuyansa yayin da dayan yana rike da Kwaryan ta kai bakinsa tace "kaga har yanzu ciwon na damunka ka rufe ido  kasha ko zaka samu sauki, idan kuma kaki sha dole na kira Ummi"


Ya bude baki a hankali tare da rintse idon tasa masa a baki yana sha, sai zai kauda ka saita kara danna kansa a hankali saida ta tabbatar yasha da yawa sannan ta kyale shi, ta sauko da kwaryan a hannunta ta ajiye tace "Ina kayanka suke in dauko ma kasa"


Ya nuna mata inda kayan suke ta isa ta dauko masa masu saukin sawa sannan ta fita da kwaryan 


Shiko da yasa kayan kwanciya yayi barci yayi awon gaba dashi

Ita kuwa kayan daya bata ta tafi wankewa



Gimbiya kuwa koda ta gama karyawa wajen sarauniya ta nufa sarauniya na ganinta ta tare ta da murna cikin ranta kuwa tana raina mata wayo, bayan sun gaisa ne Sarauniya tace "Ya na ganki Yanzu dawa kika bar Amar din"


Gimbiya tace "Ni yau ban sa shi a Idona ba ma, Ina tunanin tun asuba ya fita shine nace barin zo gurinki mudan taba hira"


Sarauniya tace "yawwa dama akwai taimakon da nake son baki"


Gimbiya tace "Name kenan"


Sarauniya ta sa hannu a cikin tulun dake gefenta ta dauko wani kullin magani ta mika mata tace "kinga wannan kisa wa Amar a Abincin sa Idan dai yaci shikena ko tambayar Umminsa bazai nayi ba, da zaran ya ganta ma koranta zaiyi idan batayi sa'a bama ya mata duka"


Gimbiya tayi murmushi tace "Nagode Sosai, Ay kuwa wlh sai ta gane kuran ta "


Suna haka Marwan ya shigo gefen Gimbiya ya zauna har jikinsu na gogan na juna yayin da ita kuma take boye maganin da aka bata yace "Matar mu me kike boye wa ne"


Sarauniya tace "La bar boyewa yasan komai shi ya amso ma"


Marwan yace "wai maganin nan ne"



Gimbiya tace "eh"


Yace "Ay sha yanzu magani yanzu "


Haka hira ta sarke tsakanin su wanda Marwan ya makalo mata wuya da hannunsa


Nasrin kuwa da ta gama Abincin rana ta dora, bayan ta gama ne ganin har yanzu Gimbiya bata dawo ba ta sa a rufeffen kwano ta nufin sassan Ummi takai a falo ta same ta ta durkusa ta gaishe, ta amsa ta ajiye mata, Ummi tayi godiya kana tace "Ni yau banga Yarima ya shigo ba lafiya"


Nasrin tayi shiru alamun ba gaskiya, koda Ummi ta gane tace "Nasrin ki fada min ko wani abu ya faru da Yarima ne"


Nasrin tace "Ummi gaskiya bashi da lafiya amma yace baya son kiji amma yanzu ya samu sauki ma har yayi barci"


"Me ke damun sa"



Nasrin tace "ciwon mara"


Ummi tace "yasha Magani"


"Eh naje gun Ummana na amso masa"


Tace "jeki to idan anjima zanzo in duba shi"


Bayan ta tafine Ummi ta shiga tunani wannan ita tasan abin da take tuna ni


Karfe 12:00 Yarima ya tashi yana bude ido ya hango Nasrin tana nade kayan da ta wanke tana sa ko wanne a mazaunin sa, ita kuwa tana ganin ya tashi ta nufi gunsa tace "Ran Yarima ya dade Ina fatan ka samu lafiya"


Yace "Alhamdulillah"


Tace" Masha Allah Allah ya baka lafiya"


Yace Amin


Ya riko hannunta yace "Nasrin dan Allah kina kula dani irin haka koma yafi haka, naji dadin irin kulawar dana samu a wajenki yau"


A da da bashi da lafiya daya rike mata hannu bata ji komai illa tausayin sa da yake ji yanzu kuwa haushi ya bata ta fizge hannun ta tace "Me aikin matarka, bari kaji ni neman yadda zanyi in gudu daga hannun munguwar matarka nan ne ma, in koma garin dan sarautar garin mu me adalci ne"



Yace "Tabbas Ni kaina nasan Sarkin Samina mutum ne me Adalci da kaunar talakawansa, sannan Sarki mu da Sarkin su Gimbiya duk a karkashinsa suke, "



Nasrin ta yatsina fuska daidai lokacin ta gama shirya kayan tana shirin fita tace "to ni me ruwana"


Tashi yayi fada wanka bayan yayi ya dauro Alola ya sa kayansa ya tafi masallaci



Gimbiya kuwa bata tashi tafiya ba saida aka kira sallah sannan ta tatshi ta nufi sassanta, tana fita Marwan yace "Umma wai maganin me kika bawa wannan sakaryar"


Sarauniya tayi murmushi tace "wannan maganin idan har Amar yaci zai dawo dolo, zai rasa kwakwalwarsa zai koma tamkar yaro komai sai an masa, kaga idan haka ta kasance, ba daman bashi sarauta, saidai a baka"


Marwan Ya fece da dariya 



Yace "Ay ranan ra na zama sarki akan mutane zan na tafiya"


Sarauniya tayi murmushi tace


 "nasan zaka iya "

Page 1⃣7⃣


Lokacin da ta isa Nasrin ne kadai a falon lokacin har ta gama aikin ta ta kalleta tace "ke Yarima ya dawo kuwa"



Nasrin tace "Dama yana nan bashi da lafiya ne amma yanzu ya samu sauki ya tafi masallaci"



Ta sake yatsina fuska tace "Ince dai kin kula min dashi ba kin bar min shi yana shan wahala ba"


Nasrin tace "Na kula dashi Gimbiya "



Yarima kuwa bayan an fito sallah wajen mai martaba ya wuce ya kwashi gaisuwa, bayan sun gama gaisawa ne Sarki yace "Lafiya kuwa yau ban ganka da safe ba"


Yarima yace "Bana jin dadi ne ranka ya dade"

Sarki yace Allah ya baka lafiya


Yace Amin


Daga nan wajen Ummi ya wuce a falo ya same tana kishingide a kan tuntu ga kuyangu a gefen ta, tana ganin sa ta mike ta rungume shi tace "ji yadda dana ya rame wai meke damun ka ne"


Yace "Ba komai"


Ta harare shi tace "Bana son Karya fa Nasrin ta fada min baka da lafiya shine ni kake son min karya"


Yadan sosa keyarsa Yace "Dan zazzabine kuma na warke"


Ta kalli fuskarsa tana son gano wani abu a kwayar idonsa sai kuma ta ce "To Allah ya sauwake"


'Yar hira suka taba sannan ya mata sallama ya wuce sassan bayi 


Yana isa aka fara gaishe shi, inda ya hango Umman Nasrin akan buhu da alama Sallah tayi ya isa gurin ta ya dan tsuguna da sauri ta mike tace "Yarima kaine da kanka ay da sai ka aika nazo"


Ya sosa keya ya sake sunkuyar dakai, sai ya tsinci kansa da jin kunya dan yasan yanzu tasan Sirrin sa,


Tace "Akwai wani abu ne Yarima?"


Ya sake sunkuyar dakai yace "Umma dama nazo ne in miki godiya "



"Haba kada ka damu Yarima nida nake baiwa ai ba zancen godiya tsakanin mu, fatana Allah ya baka lafiya"


Yace Amin

Ya kara da cewa "Umma  Umma Insha Allah gobe da safe zan nemi izini agun Sarki In 'yanta ki, keda Nasrin in sa a maida ku garin ku, saidai kuma zanyi kewarku Musamman Nasrin "


Tayi murmushi tace "mun gode Sosai"


Ya zauna yana son ya jata da hira amma ita taki sakin jiki dashi haka ya hakura ya mata Sallama



Yana zuwa sassan su Gimbiya ya samu tana cin Abinci yayin da Nasrin kuma take gefen kicin  ya zauna ya sa hannu suka fara cin na Gimbiya tare saida suka gama yace "Sumayya gobe zan nemi izini gun Sarki na 'yanta Nasrin da Ummanta"


Ta yatsina fuska tace "Ince sai na yadda Nasrin din kan"



Yace "na fada miki ne dan in fita hakkinki amma ko ki yadda ko kada ki yadda Nasrin  da Ummanta zasu koma ga garin su"


Ta mike tace "Wlh baka isa ka 'yanta ta ba, dan ba baiwarka bace, kuma ba anan gidan na sameta da ita nazo"


Yarima ya tashi ya buga teburin gaban sa har saida Gimbiya da Nasrin suka tsorata yace "Kina ganin inabiye ki ina lallashin ki, kina abin da kika ga dama, to bari kiji na fiki rikici da fetsaranci, ke ni fetsaranci ba wanda ban iya ba, na ajiyeshi ne gefe saboda bashi da amfani, amma na lura a yanzu zai min amfani, zan fito miki da sabon fetsaranci na ki gwada da naki ki gani, idan anyi magana kice, *IZZAR MULKI* Ina IZZAR taki take, kisa Ido kiga yadda ake IZZA." Ya nuna Nasrin da yatsa yace "Ke kuma yanzun nan ki tafi gun Ummanki kuma kada ki dawo sai gobe da safe, Idan taga dama ta mutu dan bata kwana a kanki ba"



Yana gama fada ya fita, Nasrin ma tabi bayan sa da sauri ta fita tayi wajen Ummanta


Gimbiya kuwa abin duniya ya ishe ta kwata kwata kwanan ta nawa ne da auren Yarima amma sam baya barinta tayi abin da take so, tunowa tayi da maganin da Sarauniya ta bata, sai ta saki murmushi tace "Zan nuna maka na fika IZZA, Ke kuma baiwa saina koya miki hankali"



Nasrin tana zuwa ta fada wa Ummanta komai, Umma ita ma ta fada mata yadda aka yi 

Nasrin tace "Kai Umma gaskiya bazan taba manta halarcin da Yarima ya mana ba Saidai ina bakin ciki bai samu mata ta gari ba, Ina tunanin idan mun tafi halin da zai shiga idan ya tashi baida lafiya dan bazata kula dashi ba, kuma Umminsa ba sani zata yi ba"


Umma tace "kedai Addu'a zamu ci gaba da masa"


Nasrin tace "kai gaskiya sarauta ba ta yi ba Umma, ni na gode Allah ma da yasa ba fito daga gidan sarauta ba "


Da sauri Umma ta kalleta tace "kada ki sake aybanta sarauta saboda akwai masarauta me yawan adalci," 



Gimbiya kuwa sassan su Marwan tayi ta fada wa Sarauniya komai 



Sarauniya tace "Kada kiji komai kiyi hakuri da Nasrin na yau daya, ina me tabbatar miki gobe sai yadda kika yi dashi idan har kin yi amfani da maganin nan"



Gimbiya tayi murmushi tace " da daddare zan masa amfani dashi"



Saida aka kira isha ta koma sassan ta dan tasan ana idar da Sallah Yarima zai shigo, tana zuwa kicin ta shiga ta samu Madaran shanu tasa maganin da Sarauniya ta bata da yake maganin fari ne, bai wani nuna ba sai ya saje da madaran, ta mayar ta rufe ta tafi daki.



Ana Idar da Sallah yayi wajen Umminsa tana kishingide akan tuntu ya shigo ya zauna gefen ta tace "Yarima kana da matsala ne"


Yace "A'a Ummi ba matsala wlh kawai ji nayi ina son muyi hira"


Tace "kaci Abinci ne"


Yace "A'a akwai madaran da na ajiye idan naje zan dauka insha, kinsan ni ban fiya cin abinci da daddare ba"


Tace "To ka tashi kaje kaga ka barta ita daya daya zata yi zaman kadaici ko da yake ga Nasrin amma duk da haka kaje din"


Yace "Nasrin na turata wajen Ummanta gobe ma zan sa a kaisu garinsu"


Ummi ta ce "ta yaya Sumayya ta bari"


Yace "ko ta bari ko kada ta bari na riga nafada mata,"


Ummi tace "To ban yadda ba, zaka shiga hakkinta"


Yarima ya kalleta yace "Ummi ni ma fa ana shiga hakkina da yawa ta dalilin Nasrin"


Tace "kamar yaya"



Yarima ya fada mata komai

Ummi ta girgiza kai tace "kayi abin daya dace, ay ni ban san haka ba"


Yace "Ummi barin je gurin su Nasrin din daga nan zan wuce"


Ta shafa kansa tace "Allah ya maka Albarka"

Yace "Amin, "  har yaje bakin kofa Ummi ta kira shi da murya karyayya "Amarrrr"


Ya juyo ta isa har gabanta ta rumgumeshi tace "Yarima ka kula da kanka, bana son ka bari wani abu ya taba min kai,"


Ya tallafo fuskarta yace "Ummi kina tunanin wani abu ne"

Tace "Ina ji kamar wani abu zai faru da kai ne"


Murmushi yayi yace "Ummi bana son son da kike min ya hana ki sukuni, dan na tabbatar son da kike min ne yasa yake raya miki haka"


Itama murmushi tayi ta shafi fuskarta tace "ka kula da kanka ka gaida min 'yata Nasrin"


Ya fita yana murmushi ba tare da yace komai ba


Nasrin kuwa tana kwance a cinyar Ummanta Yarima ya shigo Nasrin ta tashi ta gaishe shi, ya gaida Umma yace "Ina fatan kuma kan shiri"

Umma tace "eh"


Ya kalli Nasrin yace "Da fatan zakina kawo min ziyara, kuma idan kin tashi yin aure ki fada min"


Tace "In sha Allah 

Yace "to ni na tafi"


Ya tafi Nasrin ta bishi a baya dan ta rakashi sada sukayi nesa da Umma ya kama hannunta yace "Nasrin Nagode da kulawar da kika bani, kuma ina son ki ci gaba da kula dani har yafi na da"


Tace "Nida zan tafi garin mu ina zan ganka"


Yace "Ay zaki zo wata rana kuma, idan ina son ganinki zanzo, kin min alkawarin zaki kula dani duk lokacin da matsala ta same ni idan kina tare dani"


Tace "Bana fatan matsala ta sameka amma na maka alkawarin zan kula da kai muddin ina tare da kai koda baka cikin matsala"


Yace "Nagode Nasrin saida safe"


Ya sake hannunta ya tafi itama ta juya 


Yana shiga sassan wanka yayi yasa jallabiya ya nufi kicin gun madaransa ya tsiyaya ya dawo falo yana sha, ba dade da zama ba Gimbiya ta fito, yace "Bismillah"


Tace ni nasha nawa"


Saida ya shanye ya kalleta yace "muje mu kwanta ko"


Tace "to "


Tayi gaba ya bita a baya yana mamakin yadda akayi ta sauya lokaci daya

  🌐 IZZAR MULKI

Story 
        &
          Writing by
UMMU MUFEED
      (Mrs Aliyu)


Wannan Page din naki ne Billyn Ussy ki yadda kike so dashi ina yinki over, wani abu ma sai Febuary 28, 2020



*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 1⃣9⃣



Yana kwanciya akan gadon kansa yayi irin sarawa duk dauriya irin na Yarima amma saida ya rike goshinsa, Gimbiya tace "ya dai Yarima me jiran gado"


Ya kalleta yana son mata magana saidai ya kasa sai karyar dakai ya suma


Gimbiya taje ta kama kansa ta jijjiga shi tana kiran sunan shi saidai shiru, da sauri taje ta debo ruwa ta yayyafa masa a fuska, sa gani tayi ya farfado suna hada ido yayi saurin mikewa ya sauka daga kan gadon ta biyo shi inda yake ya kara da gudu yayi falo


Fita tayi ta same shi ya kwanta akan kujera a takure tana zuwa ya kara tsorata zai gudu tace "Kai tsaya nikan ni zanje in kwanta ka daina min haka, ka samu ma ina biye ka"


Kallonta yake har ta shiga cikin daki, yayin da shi kuma ya kwanta a kasa ga sanyi



Da asuba bayan Nasrin tayi sallah Ta gaida Umma tadan taba karatun Alkur'ani me girma, bayan ta idar ne lokacin gari yayi haske Umma tace "Nasrin kije kidan taba wa Gimbiya dan aikace-aikace kafin muzo tafiya"


Nasrin tace "wani aiki kuma Umma muda zamu bar musu garin su"


Umma tace "A'a Nasrin kije dai"


Ba musu Nasrin ta tashi ta tafi, tasan yanzu haka Yarima yana Masallaci saboda haka zata samu kofar a bude saidai tana zuwa taji kofar a rufe tadan buga kofar 



Yarima kuwa da barci ya kwashe a kasa sai asuba ya farka ya tashi ya zauna dan baisan abin yi ba, ga fitsali ya matse shi, karshe a gurin yayi fitsarin, ya dade a zaune yana ta kalle-kalle can yaji ana buga kofa koda kawai sai ya tashi yaje jikin kofar yana kallo,


Daidai lokacin Nasrin ta sake bugawa, sai ya daga kansa sama yaga sakata, sai ya zare, tana sake bugawa sai kofar ta bude, Yarima ta gani a tsaye, ta saki Murmushi tace "Barka da asuba ranka ya dade"


Sai taga bakon abu, dan be amsa ba saidai kallonta da yake yi, sai ta raba ta wuce, ganin haka shi kuma ya koma inda yake ya kwanta a cikin fitsarin. Nasrin kuwa ta fito daga kicin ta ganshi kwance a kasa a takure gashi sai rawan sanyi yake, ta isa gurinsa tace "Yarima lafiya kuwa"


Sai ya kalleta kamar yana son ya mata magana sai kuma yawu ya fara zuba daga bakinsa zuwa jallabiyar dake jikinsa, Nasrin tace "Innalillahi wa inna ilaihir raju'un, Yarima kamin magana me ke faruwa dakai"

Kafadarsa ta rike sai taga ya kama hannunta ya tashi, tashin da yayi ne yasa taga gurin a jike ga jikinsa ma a jike, sai zarnin fitsarin da ya doki hancinta tace "Yarima ka min magana lafiya fa muka rabu jiya"


Har yanzu yawun ne yake ta dalala. 


Da sauri ta fita sassan Ummi tayi tana kuka kamar ranta zai fita, tana zuwa ta buga kofa da karfi, kuyanga ce tazo ta bude, ana budewa tayi ciki da gudu, Ummi na ganinta ta mike tana cewa "Lafiya"


Nasrin ta kamata tace "Ummi dan Allah kizo kiga Yarima yana cikin wani hali"


Ay da gudunta Ummi ta fita Nasrin ma ta bita a baya, tana shiga falon ta hango danta kwance yana rawan sanyi jikinsa a jike ga yawu da yake malala a jikinsa, da sauri ta rungume tana cewa "me ya faru dakai dana"


Kuka take Sosai kamar ranta zai fita yayin da Nasrin ma kukan take, saida suka gaji Ummi ta kalli Nasrin tace "Nasrin fitsari ne a jikinsa taimaka min mu kaishi bayi in masa wanka"


Nasrin taje ta daya bangarensa tana kama hannunsa ya mike yana takawa a hankali dakinsa suka shiga suna zuwa kofar bayin Nasrin ta dawo ta wanke wajen ta gyara tuni gurin ya dawo hayyacinsa, ta koma ta samu sun fito tasa shi a tsakiyan gado tana shafa masa mai har lokacin yawu ne ke zuba, ta shiga bayin ta dauko kayan da ta cire masa taje ya wanke, ta dawo ta samu Ummi na bashi ruwan shayi, bayan ta gama bashi ne tace "Nasrin ki zauna anan kada ki fita zan je na fada wa Mai martaba halin da ake ciki,"


Nasrin kuwa zama tayi tana kallonsa yayin da Ummi ta fita, Ummi na fita Nasrin ta matsa kusa dashi tace "Yarima me ya same ka, wa ya maka haka me suke nima da kai"


Duk wadannan tambayoyin ba amsa saidai kallonta da yake yi ga yawu zai zuba yake a bakinsa


Tana zaune Sarki da Marwan suka shigo Ummi na bayansu tana kuka


Suna shigowa Nasrin ta tashi tana gaida Sarki sannan ta fita,  shi kuwa Sarki hawa gadon yayi ya rumgumeshi bai damu da yawun dake zuba a bakin sa ba, yace "Magajina haka Allah ya aiko maka, me ya faru da kai," ba amsa 


Sarki yace "A fada wa Sarkin dawaki a hada amalanken doki za'a kai Yarima gun magani"


Yana gama fada ya fita yana me tausayin yarima, Marwan ma ya bishi yana farin cikin ganin halin da Yarima ya shiga



Ummi kuwa haushine ya kamata ganin duk abun da ake yi Sumayya bata fito ba, Dakinta ta nufa tana zuwa ta sameta ta baje a gado tana ta shakar barci, Ummi ta buga mata duka a cinya, ta mike da sauri tana sosawa tace "Me na miki"


Ummi tace "me ya samu dana tashi daya ya dawo haka"


Sumayya tace "Me ya dawo"


Ummi tace "Wato ma baki san abin dake faruwa ba, mijinki baida lafiya har kina iya samun yin barci a wannan lokaci, sai ki tashi kije ki ganshi, za'a tafi dashi gun magani"


Sumayya ta tashi tana yatsina fuska koda ta isa dakin taga Yarima yana zubar da yawu sai ta toshe hanci, Ummi tace "Me haka"


Tace "Wari bazan iya zama a dakin nan ba gaskiya"


Ta fita Ummi kuwa ta bita da kallo a ranta tana tausayin dan nata


Nan da nan aka shirya Yarima Sarki ne da kansa ya kwantar dashi a jikinsa sai dogarawa da suke biye dashi a doki, 


Yarima na kwance jikin Sarki Ummi ta shafa kansa tace "Sai kun dawo dana Allah ya baka lafiya, zan maka Addu'a, Nasan Nasrin da Ummanta da sauran mutane ma suna tare da kai zasu maka Addu'a"


Sarauniya da Marwan ma suka zo Marwan yace "Dan uwana ina maka fatan alheri" saidai cikin ransa farin ciki ne fal, sarki zai tafi yabar masarautar zaiyi gudanar da irin nashi sarautar


Sarki kuwa ya manta ma da wata sarauta ta dansa yake balle ya bada rikon kwarya


Sarauniya tazo tace "Amar ashe haka ya faru da kai to Allah ya kyauta, " ta shafa kansa wanda a zahirine amma wani maganin ta shafa masa tace "Ina maka fatan Alheri"


Ma'aikatan gidan da bayi suna ta kuka da Addu'a Allah yasa a dace. Haka suka tafi suka bar masarautar, Ummi ta hango Nasrin  yadda take ta kuka ba kama hannun yaro taje ta rumgume ta tace "Nasrin Addu'a zamu masa kinji"


Duk wannan abin da ake ba Gimbiya Sumayya tana can an mata wanka taci kwalliyarta,


Ganin kowa sai kuka yake ga Su Yarima sun tafi Marwan ya daka musu tsawa "Kai kowa ya koma inda yake! Idan ba haka ba in yayyanka naman ku"


Kowa ya fara tafiya yayin da har lokacin ciki harda Nasrin Ummi ce tsaye ta juya kenan sai Marwan ya biyota yace "Sarki da dansa sun bar gari yanzu sarauta ta dawo hannu na, zan mulke ku iya yadda nake so, dan haka kibi a hankali damu"


Ummi batace komai ba ta wuce Sarauniya ta tako tace "Aisha yanzu sarauta ta dawo gun dana dan haka sai ki san tayi, ko kiyi hakuri da irin sarautar da zai aiwatar ko ki koma gidan tsoho"


Ummi tace "Bazan koma gidan tsoho ba saboda mijina bai bani izini ba, dadin abin ma ba tabbata zasuyi ba zasu dawo duk wuya ina zaune gidan mijina"


Tana gama fada ta wuce sassanta,


Nasrin kuwa gun Ummanta tayi tana zuwa tace "Umma kinji abin da ya faru da Yarima ko"


Umma tace "Naji to yaya zamuyi sai Addu'a zamu masa Allah ya yaye masa"


Nasrin tace "Allah sarki Yarima Allah baiyi zaka cika mana Alkawarin da ka dauka ba"


Umma tace "ki tashi ki tafi gun Gimbiya fa nasan yanzu haka tana nemanki"


Nasrin ta tashi ta nufi sassan duk da tasan cewa Gimbiya sai taci zalinta, ay kuwa tana zuwa taganta a kan kujera wata kuyanga tana ja mata yatsan kafa,

Ta durkusa can gefe sai ji tayi Gimbiya ta kyalkyale da dariya tace "wadanda za'a 'yanta ne han yanzu ba'a yanta ba"


Nasrin shiru tayi Gimbiya ta tashi ta fincikota ta bata mari tace "wato saboda wani yace kije ki kwana a wani waje kika tafi kika barni na kwana ni daya ko"


Nasrin bata ce komai ba dan ko gezau idonta baiyi ba dan idan da sabo ta saba


Gimbiya ko jawota tayi ta sunkuyar da bayanta ta dora kafarta akanta



A Al'adar gidan idan akayi sallah akanje fada gaida sarki to yau sarki bayanan duk da haka masu gaida sarkin da sauran fadawan suna nan, 


Can kuma Marwan ya sa an jera masa hannun bayi maza, takai yabi ya har ya isa Fada inda ya zauna kan kujeran sarki. Mamaki fal cike a fuskarsu, koda ya musu wani mungun kallo da daidaya-da daidaya suka ringa zuwa suna gaishe shi. Bayan sun gama gaishe shi ne yace "Ina sarkin Fada"


Sarkin Fada ya amsa 

Marwan yace "Daga yau idan zaka shirya fada ka jera min 'yan mata san ka sama min wanda zan na kwana dasu kullum, "


Sarkin Fada yace "Ranka ya dade ba haka muka yi da mahaifinka ba"


Marwan ya daka masa tsawa "yanzu lokacina ne saboda haka abi umurnina"


Tuni Sarkin Fada  yayi shiru da bakinsa 



Bangaren su Yarima kuwa Tafiya kilometers 20 suka yi kafin suka iso kauyen suna zuwa gun mutane ne cike a gurin ko wanne yana son a bashi magani dan kowa da ka ganshi a gurin da matsalarsa saidai duk cikinsu matsalar Yarima yafi tsauri


Koda suka ga Sarki da kansa ay sai akayi sauri aka fada wa Malam

Da saurin Malam ya fito yana zuwa ya zube yace "Ranka ya dade ba sai ka ayka in zo ba ka zo da kanka" 


Sarki yace "Idan nace kazo ya zakayi da wadan nan bayin Allah, nima nasan kana da mutane yasa nace barin zo da kaina"


Malam yace "Me ya samu Yarima "


Sarki yace "Yanzu muka zo ka gama da bayin Allah nan mana muma layi zamu bi"


Ay kuwa a tare mutanen suka ce "Ranka ya dade a duba shi kawai baza muso Yarima ya zauna bin layi ba, saboda yana taimakon bayin Allah"



Sarki yace "Nagode muku"


Malam ya sake cewa "me damun Yarima "



Sarki ya masa bayanin abin da ya sani 


Malam ya kama hannun Yarima ya shiga dashi ciki, saida suka dau tsawon lokaci sannan suka fito.


Malam ya kalli Sarki yace "Ranka ya dade Yarima fa an masa asiri ne, an juyar masa da kwakwalwarsa ya koma tamkar karamin yaro yanzu dole komai sai ana koya masa,  yanzu abin da nake son fada maka, ku koma gida tunda yana da mata, matarsa kadai zata na lura dashi tana koya masa abubuwa, wanda bazai iya ba kuma ta masa, amma kada ana yawan zuwa gunsa, matarsa kadai ta isa ta kula dashi, kuma ku dage da Addu'a duk sati zan na zuwa duba shi"

Page 1⃣9⃣



Sarki yace "Malam yanzu ya maganar warke wan"


Yace "Insha Allah zai Allah zai bashi lafiya amma bamu san lokaci ba"


Malam ya kara da cewa "yanzu baisan kowa ba amma a hankali zaina gane wa, kuma idan ka masa magana yana jin ka, kuma zaiyi abin da kace, sabo da haka a kula dashi sosai"



Sarki yace "insha Allahu zamuyi yadda kace"



Sarki yace "Mu tafi ko Yarima"


Yarima yayi gaba da kansa ya shiga amalanken suka kama hanyar komawa gida.



Koda akayi Sallah la'asar Sarkin fada ya jera 'yan mata a fada 


Wannan karan ma bayi ne suka shimfida masa hannunsu ya taka ya isa fada


Yana zuwa ya karewa 'yan matan kallo sannan ya zauna kan kujera ya dagawa daya  daga cikinsu hannu alamar yazo. Ta tako har gaban ya kama hannunta ya ware kafa ya sata a tsakiyar sa yasa hannu ya cafko mata kirji, yarinyar tasa kuka shiko sai ci gaba yayi, duk cikin fadan rufe ido sukayi saboda kunya da takaici, gashi ba daman magana ko ka tashi kabar gurin. Wani tsoho ne ya yi karfin hali ya tashi yaje gabansa ya tsuguna yace "Ranka ya dade wannan bai dace ba, kuma wannan rashin kunya ne ay kasa a tara maka mata a fada bai maka ba zaka zo kana irin wannan a gaban mu"


Marwan yasa kafa ya haure tsohon ya daga murya yace "ashe har akwai wanda zai samin baki akan al'amura na, to yau zaka bakun ci lahira"


Da sauri tsohon ya kama kafar Marwan yana bashi hakuri amma marwan ya sake haurinshi ya yi wa wasu karti ido suka zo suka kama tsohon suka fita dashi yayin da Marwan ya tashi ya shiga ciki ya dauko sharbebiyar wuka ya fita, can bayan fada yayi inda aka ajiye tsohon yayin da mutane suka taru dan aiyi ta jita-jitan Marwan zai yi kisa Gimbiya da Nasrin ma sun halarci gurin, yana zuwa tsohon ya sake kama kafarsa amma ya sake haurinsa da kafa kartin suka kamashi suka durkusar dashi sannan suka sake shi, ba imani haka Marwan ya daga wukarsa ya zarewa tsohon nan kai,


Nasrin saida tayi hawaye har sauran yan gurin, Marwan ya daga murya yace "Wannan hukuncin zan ringa yi aduk wanda ya shiga gonata, kuma duk randa Sarki ya dawo wani ya yi masa gulma, irin wannan hukuncin zan masa"



Ya kalli kartin yace "ku binne gawarsa kusa bayi su wanke jinin"



Komawa yayi Sassan Sarauniya Gimbiya ta bishi suna isa tace "Irin wannan hukuncin naso yi aka ki barina a masarautar mu"



Marwan yace "Ay duk wanda ya maka ka kashe kawai"


Ya makalo mata kafada suka karasa shiga falon a tare


Karfe biyar su me Martaba suka iso, Marwan yana jin labarin ya kalli Ummansa yace "Umma dama ba dadewa zasuyi ba?" 


Tace "Nima nayi zaton zasu dade ne "


Gimbiya ta mike tace "barin je su same ni a can sassata, kada 'yan gulma sukai"


Ta tashi tana fita ta hango Yarima yana tafiya Me martaba ya rike masa hannu saidai kallo daya zaka masa ka gane bashi da lafiya saboda har yanzu yawun zuba yake duk ya jika gaban rigarsa



Da sauri ta shige sassanta kafin Sarki ya karaso dashi a falo ya zaunar dashi akan kujera, daidai Ummi ta shigo da Umman Nasrin, Sarauniya ma ta shigo


Sarki ya kwashe yadda akayi ya fada musu sannan ya kalli Sumayya yace "Ke fa yanzu saidai kiyi hakuri dashi, kina kula dashi tunda ke daya ce matarsa, da ace ma yana da wata matar ce da sai kuyi ku biyu"



Tace "ga Baiwata nan zata masa komai"



Sarki yace "Kece Matarsa ke zaki masa hidima, ita baiwar taki saita taimaka miki ta wani gun, amma tunda kinga bashi da lafiya ke ce me Alhakin kula da shi"



Gimbiya tace "Duk abin da kace Baiwata zata iya"


Nasrin tayi shiru ta na tuna maganar Yarima na jiya "Dan Allah ki kula dani"


Ay kuwa tayi karaf tace "Ni zan kula dashi ranka ya dade zan iya"

 
　Ummi tace "Nasrin baza ki iya ba, ciwon Yarima ba'a san ranan warkewansa ba, kuma nan gaba kadan kinga aure zakiyi, kinga mijinki bazai bari ki kula da namiji kamar Amar ba"



Tace "Ummi bazanyi aure ba sai Yarima ya samu lafiya, Ummi Na sadaukar ga duk kan rayuwata ga Yarima Ina tare dashi,"


Sarki Yace "bazai yuyuba saboda kuna baligai"


Nasrin tayi shiru can ta tuna Gimbiya baxata iya kula da Yarima ba saidai ta cutar dashi, ta kara sa gaban Sarki tace "Ranka ya dade ka amince min in kula dashi zan kare duk wasu dokoki da suke kanmu "



Sarki Yayi shiru can yace "ki bari zanyi tunani, akai yana gama fada ya tashi saida yaje bakin kofa ya kalli Ummi yace "kizo ina son yin magana dake"


Tace "To ranka ya dade"


Yana fita ta kalli Gimbiya ta ce "To Sumayya tunda bazaki kula da Mijinki ba, to ni zan aura masa wacce zata kula dashi"



Tace "aure a bashi da lafiyan nan ta fara zubar da hawaye tana cewa, dama nasan baki sona tunda gashi har kin fara maganan zai kara aure, ay tunda ni bazan iya kula dashi ba, baiwa ta zata kula dashi"



Ummi tace a ranta "Dama tana da kishi shine har take crwa ya amshi hakkinsa gun Nasrin "


Sarauniya da tun dazu batayi magana ba tace "Kin gani ba na fada miki"


Tana gama fada ta fita Gimbiya tace cikin kuka "Nidai wlh ban yadda a masa aure ba" 


Ummi ta taka ta isa gun Yarima ta zauna a gefensa ta goge masa yawun dake bakinsa tace "Dana Nasan kana jina kana ganin masu kaunarka ko"


Marwan yace "Mutumin da bashi da hankali yaushe zai gane me kaunarsa"


Gimbiya tace "Ba wani me kaunarsa da ta wuce ni tunda har baiwa zan bashi ta masa hidima"



Ummi ta kalle ta tace "kin amince zaki bar masa Nasrin?"


Tace "Na amince idai bazaki masa aure ba"


Ummi tace "Yarima ne fa zaina kwana da Nasrin bake ba, ke ki kwana a dakin ki, ita ta kwana dashi a dakinsa"


Gimbiya tace "Na amince"



Ta juya ta kalli Nasrin tace "amma kada ki sake ki kwanta masa a gado a kasa zaki kwanta"


Nasrin ta daga kai ita dai burinta abarta ta kula da Yarima tunda ta riga ta masa Alkawarin haka tun bata san haka zata faru dashi ba


Ummanta kuwa batayi magana ba sai Addu'a da take yiwa Yarima 


Ummi tace "Barin je gun me martaba ina zuwa "


Ta fita ta samu Sarki ta nemi guri ta zauna Sarki Yace "Aisha saidai fa muyi hakuri da ciwon Yarima dan ya rasa kwakwalwarsa, yanzu haka baida hankali "



Ummi tace "To yaya zamuyi saidai mu dage masa da Addu'a "



Sarki yace "To ya kike ganin ya kamata kuwa mu barshi da baiwar Yariyar na da tace zata kula dashi"


Gimbiya tace " Nasrin zata kula dashi fiye da yadda baka zato, "


Sarki yace "Amma fa kula da Yarima fa sai matarsa saboda wasu abubuwa"


Ummi tace "To me zani hana mu aura masa ita"



Sarki yace "aure"

Wannan page din nakine Maryam din Ameer ki yadda kike so dashi na baki kyauya Allah ya baku da ki sa masa suna Aliyu🤪


*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 2⃣0⃣



Ummi tace "Matarsa bazata iya kula dashi ba saidai ta cutar dashi, Nasrin kuwa zata kula dashi, idan muka aura masa ita kaga duk abin da ta masa daidaine"



Sarki ki Yace "Haka ne amma ya kamata muji ta bakin Mahaifiyarta tukunko"



Ummi tace "barin kira ta"


Ummi ta fita ba jumawa ta dawo da Umma a bayanta ta durkusa can gefe Sarki yace "kinji abin da 'yarki tace game da Yarima yaya kin amince da maganar ta"


Tunda yake magana kanta a kasa saida ya gama ta dago tace "Ranka ya dade, wannan maganan tata akwai Yarinta a ciki, bazai Yuyu ta kula da shiba saboda hukuce-hukuncen musulumci. Idan akayi haka an sabawa musulumci saboda haka a kyaleta kawai"


Sarki yace "To idan mukace zamu musu aure zaki amince"


Ummi tayi shi ta tuna Alkawarin da ya musu amma Allah bai bashi iko ba, sai ta fashe da kuka cikin kuka tace "Hakan da kukayi daidaine saidai ina tsoron lokacin da Gimbiya zata san Nasrin tana auren mujinta"


Sarki yace "Idan bakyaso ba dole fa, kada ki damu idan baki so wlh bazamu yi ba"


Tace "Ina so Wlh amma ina tunanin wani makoma Nasrin zata koma idan Gimbiya ta sani"


Ummi tace "kukan da kike fa"


Tace "Ina tausayin Yarima ne"


Sarki yace "Maganan tsoron kuma, dama auren za'a yishi ne cikin sirri ba wanda zai sani, Gobe zanje gun malam zan masa bayani sai mu daura auren acan"


Tace "ba damuwa amma fa ina tsoron ranan da Nasrin zata fada mata, dan Nasrin akwai ta da zuciya, idan Gimbiya ta bata mata rai zata iya cewa ta auri mijin ta"



Sarki yace "bana son ita Nasrin din ma ta san tana da aure akanta , kada kowa ya sani yadda muka yi maganan nan ina son a rufeta a nan"



Umma tace "shikenan ba damuwa"


Bayan anyi Sallah isha Nasrin ta shiga Dakin Yarima ta ganshi ya takure akan gado yanata rawan sanyi da sauri taje ta dafa shi taga fitsari yayi a gadon tace "oh Yarima idan kayi fitsarin nan baka san yana cutar dakai bane gashi yanzu dare yayi da an fitar da katifan tasha iska"


Kallota kawai yake yi ta shafi fuskarsa tana murmushi  tace  "kana kallona kamar baka sanni ba"


Sai taga shima yayi murmushin 


Ta kama hannusa suka shiga bayi tace "Yarima idan zaka yi fitsari daga yanzu haka zayi"


Ta zaunar dashi akan sit tare da cire masa wando da sauri ta kauda kanta tare da rintse idonta gam shi kuma sai kallonta yake tace "ka gani ko"


Ya dan mata murmushi ta cire masa kayan tare da kara rufe idonta. Idonta a rufe ta masa wanka saida ta gama ta daura masa towel suka fito tare, kan kujera ta kaishi ya zauna ya sa ruwa inda yayi fitsarin tasa tsumma tayi ta daddannawa har ya tsantsame tace "Barin kawo maka madara kasha sai ka kwanta nasan kana jin bacci"


Ta fita bayan ta dauko masa madaran ta ajiye ta fito masa da kayan bacci idonta a rufe tasa masa ta fara bashi Madaran a hankali yana sha, saida ta tabbatar ta koshi sannan ta kyale shi. Saida ya dan dau lokaci kadan sannan tace "Lokacin barci ne yanzu yakamata ka kwanta"


Ta kama hannunsa ta kaishi gefen da ba fitsarin tasa shi ya kwanta, tana shafa kansa har barci ya kwashe shi. Ta kalle shi tace "Allah ya baka lafiya yarima"


Addu'a barci ta karanto masa ta tofa masa ta shafe masa fuska, sannan ta tashi tayi falo ta samu Gimbiya da Marwan a zaune cinyar su na gogan na juna


Marwan yace "Gimbiya wannan yarinyar fa, ko itace baiwar ki da kike fada min"



Gimbiya tace "eh itace akwai wani abune"


Yasa hannu ta cikin maballin rigarsa yace "Gaskiya ina son ki bani ita yau in kwana da ita"


Nasrin tayi saurin juyowa ta kalleshi sai kuma ta juya da sauri cikin ranta kuwa ytayi ayi Allah wadai da halinsu, 

Gimbiya kuwa cewa tayi "ni kaina nan bazan kwana da ita ba da Yarima zata kwana kasan ni bazan iya da kazantar da yake zubarwa daga bakinsa ba"


Yace "Amma watarana zaki bani ita ko"


Tace "eh to idan mijina ya fara samun sauki zan baka ita"


Marwan yace "kina son mijin nan naki"


Tace "wlh baka ji yadda nake ji dashi ba ina son sa sosai," ta juya ta kalli Nasrin tace "me kika fito yi ne"


Tace "Nazo ne naji ko da abin da za'a miki "


Tace " ki je ki kwana dashi kuma ki tabbatar kin masa abin da yake so"


Nasrin batace komaiba ta juya dan bata san ganin fuskar su daga ita har Marwan dan ta tsane su


Gimbiya ta kalli Marwan tace "Nifa ina ganin kamar maganin da na bashi ne yasa shi wannan rashin lafiyan"


Marwan ya bata rai yace "kenan kina ganin zamu cutar daku ne, magani mun baki saboda 'yancin ki dan ya samu ciwo sai kice maganin da muka baki ne, nagode Sumayya munyi niyyar taimakonki amma mu kina mana kallon macuta, zan fadawa Ummana ta cire hannunta daga lamarinki"


Ya mike da sauri ta rike hannunsa tace "dan Allah kayi hakuri ba ina nufin zaku cuce ni bane amma dan Allah kayi hakuri "


Ya fizge hannunsa ya fice abinsa


Nasrin kuwa tana shiga ta zauna bakin gadon ta kama goshin sa ta yi masa Addu'a sannan ta dauki wani bargo ta shinfida a kasa ta kwanta


Da asuba sanyi asuba ya ratsa shi tashi yayi ya zauna ga bargo a kan gadon amma ya rasa yadda zaiyi dashi,  ya hango Nasrin a kasa tana barcinta ya nufi gunta ya kwanta a jikin ta ya rumgumeta ya matseta a jikinsa ta bude ido taga yadda yake rawan sanyi tace "Yarima lafiya, sanyi kake ji ko, to sake ni muje ka kwanta in rufeka"


A hankali ya sassauta rikon da ya mata ta tashi tayi kan gadon ya bita ta sashi ya kwanta sannan ta rufe shi da bargo barci ya kwashe shi ta tashi ta shiga bayi ta dauro Alola tazo tayi raka'atainil fajir, sannan tayi sallah asuba, tadan taba karatu kadan ta tashi ta shiga kicin, tana shiga taji an fara buga kofa, taje ta bude, Me Martaba ne da Ummi ta gaishe su suka tambaye ta ma jiki tace "Da sauki"


Suka wuce dakin suka ganshi rufe da bargo yana barci Sarki ya shafi fuskarsa yace "Allah ya baka lafiya Magajina"


Yarima ya bude Ido yana kallonsu sai ya hango Nasrin a bayan su ay sai ya mika mata hannu yana matse kafarsa yana kallonta, ta gane fitsari yake ji amma tana jin kunyar su Sarki, Ummi ce tace "Nasrin ko yana son wani abu ne"


A hankali tace "Fitsari zaiyi"


Sarki ya kama hannunsa zai shiga dashi bayi amma Yarima ya fizge hannunsa ya je ya kama hannun Nasrin yana janta bayi, Sarki yace "ke yake son ki kaishi"


Cikin kunya Nasrin ta shiga dashi tayi masa yadda tayi masa jiya sai gashi yayi harda bayan gida. Koda Ummi ta fahimci haka sai tace "Nasrin fito kije ki aikin ki"


Ta fito, koda ya gama Sarki da kansa ya masa sarki, sannan ya masa wanka bayan ya gama yace "Nasrin ta shirya shi" shi kuma dama ya bada Umurnin a shirya masa doki zaije gun malam


Bayan ta shirya shi ta koma kicin taci gaba da aikinta saida ta gama ta jerawa Gimbiya nata ta dauki nasu ita da Yarima tayi daki dashi.


Tare suka ci tana bashi itama tana kaiwa bakinta har suka koshi


Koda Sarki da tawagarsa suka isa gun Malam ya shiga gunsa shi daya ya masa bayanin komai Malam yace "To ba komai waye waliyyin Yarinyar"


Sarki yace "Nine, kai kuma ka zama waliyyinsa"


Malam ya tara shedu aka daura auren Yarima Amar da Nasrin, wanda koda Badawan Sarki basu san an daura aure ba.


Koda Sarki ya dawo ya fada wa Ummi komai yasa aka kira masa Umma itama ya fada mata komai tare da mika mata Sadakin Nasrin.

Page 2⃣2⃣


Gimbiya ta zaro ido tace "Dama abin da kike koya masa kenan, Iskanci kike koya masa, dama ke 'yar iska ce "



Nasrin ta zaro ido tace "wlh ba haka bane ki tsaya kiji"


Ta kwada wa Nasrin mari Yarima ya juya ya dauki takalminsa dake ajiye ya doka akai ya sake daukan wani ya bita dashi ta kwasa da gudu ya bita har dakinta da taga bata tsira ba tayi waje, sasan Sarauniya ta nufa ita kuwa Nasrin ba daman fita haka dan dole yasa ta sa kayanta sannan ta bisu,


 suna shiga ta buya bayan Sarauniya shiko sai binta yake Marwan ne yazo ya rike shi Amar kuwa sai mutsu-mutsu yake, marwan da yaga zai fishi karfe sai ya hada masa da duka, daidai lokacin Nasrin ta shigo taje ta kama Yarima shiko Marwan sai dukansa yake, koda Yarima ya ga haka shima sai ya fara dukansa Da takalmin hannun sa, dukan da Yarima yayi masa ya masa zafi yasa Marwan yayi masa naushi a kuncin wanda hakan yasa Yarima ya fadi, bakinsa na zubar da jini, da Sauri Nasrin taje ta taro shi ta rumgumeshi tana kuka tana cewa "Dan Allah ka kyaleshi haka ya tuba bazai sake ba. Kaga har bakinsa ya fashe"


Da sauri Gimbiya tazo gurin tace "Haba Marwan kaga ka fasa masa baki ko. Kashe shi zaka yi"

Yayi tsaki yace "idan na kashe wannan Mahaukacin menene to"



Sarauniya tace "Wai akan me ya biyo ki"


Gimbiya  ta fada mata komai har abin da Marwan ya fada mata,


Nasrin tace "Wlh ba nufina in koya masa wani abu ba"



Sarauniya tace "to ke wannan mahaukacin me zaki dashi dan an koya masa wani iskanci, ni in nine ke tuni na barshi dan ba abin da zanci da mahaukaci"


Gimbiya tace "Amma wlh sai na fada wa iyayen sa abun da take masa 


Nasrin kan dagoshi tayi ya mike ta jashi zasu tafi amma Yarima yaki tafiya sai kallon Marwan yake yana nuna shi da yatsa yana kuma motsi da bakinsa yawu da jini yana fita

Da kyar yana kokarin haka saiga magana ta fito da kikkina "ku-ku-ku-ku-ma-ma- sehhh na fa-da wa- sa-sar-sarrrr-ki"

(Yana nufin "kuma sai na fada wa sarki")

Da sauri Nasrin ta rumgumeshi bata damu da yawu da jinin da ke bakinsa ba ta sumbace shi a kumatu tace "Yarima magana ka fara"


Sai kuma ta fara hawaye sai gani tayi yana share mata h
awaye yace "Ki-ki-ki- dai-daina ku-kuuuuka"


Tace "Na daina Yarimana muje ka kwanta"


Saijin muryan Marwan tayi yace "Na fada miki ta maida shi dan iska"


Gimbiya tace "Zaiyi barci ay saina nakasa ta yau da daddaren nan"


Yarima ya nunota da yatsa yace "ke-ke-

Nasrin tayi saurin janshi suka fita suna fita sasansu suka yi tana shiga ta sake masa wanka ta canja masa wasu kayan baccin tayi alola tayi nafila ta dade tana godiya wa Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala da yasa Yarima yin magana har ta kwanta gefenshi ta juyo tace "Yarima bazan iya bari sai da safe in fada wa Ummi kana magana ba. Ina son ita ma ta kasance cikin farin ciki ka zauna a nan ina zuwa" ta tashi da sauri ya riko ta kalleshi tace "menene"


Ya ja hannunta zuwa waje ta biye masa ya jata sassan Ummi, Ummi tana kishingide tana ganin su ta mike tace "Nasrin lafiya "

Nasrin ta kalli Yarima shima ya kalleta sukayi murmushi 

Ummi tace "Nasrin me haka"


Nasrin tace "Ummi Yarima ne ya fara magana"


Ummi tace "Magana kuma "


Nasrin tace "Yarima ka gaida Ummi"


Yayi murmushi yace "Saaaa sannuuu Ummi"

Ummi tace "Nasrin garin yaya yayi magana"


Nasrin ta fada mata komai abin daya faru 

Ummi tace "Nasrin da gaske ne kina wanka a gabansa "


Nasrin tace "Ummi wataran ne idan ya tada rigima yace bazai zauna shi daya ba"


Ummi tace "Amar ka daina mata rigima kana barinta tana shiga bayi ita daya"


Ya makale kafada Yace "Uhm uhm"


Tace "Nasrin wannan maganan a barta kada ki fadawa me martaba saboda hankalinsa zai tashi, ga damuwa akan ciwon Yarima, kada a sake sa masa wata damuwar"



Nasrin tace "Bazan fada ba insha Allah "



Yarima ya makale kafada yace "Uhm-uhm ni-dai se se na-na fa-daaaaa"


Nasrin tace "Ay kuwa bazan baka Madarata ba"



A fusace ya tashi zai fita wai shi yayi fushi  tayi saurin binshi ta kama hannunsa Tace "yi hakuri zan baka ni na isa in hana ka Madara ne"


Murmushi yayi ya dawo ya zauna

Nasrin tace "Ay baza ka fada ba ko"


Ya daga kai alamun eh


Ummi tace "Nasrin kuje ku kwanta Allah ya muku Albarka"


Ta kama hannusa suka tafi Ummi ta bisu da kallo tana jin dadin Yadda Nasrin ke kula da danta



Washe gari bayan ta gama shirya shi ta bashi tsummansa ta shiga kicin tayi aikinta ta fito bayan sun karya ne suna zaune a falo Sarki da Ummi suka shigo yana ganin Sarki yayi gunsa da murna bayan Nasrin ta gaida sune Yarima ma yayi yadda tayi saidai ba gaisuwa yayi ba cewa yayi "Sarrrr-ki "


Sarki ya daga kai ya kalleshi yace "Magaji magana naji kayi"


Ya nuna Dakin Gimbiya yace "Wannnn-car ma-ma-ma-tarrrr tana duuuukan Nassssriiina"


(Yana nufin waccar matar tana dukan.Nasrina)


Sarki Yace "Akan me"


Yarima ya fara bashi labari da in'ina saidai bai gama ba sarki ya katse shi dan bai gane abin da yake nufi ba

Yace "ya isa haka huta"


Ya kalli Nasrin yace "me kika mata ta dake ki, kuma naji Yarima yana kiran Sunan Marwan ko ya masa wani abu ne"



Kafin Nasrin tayi magana Yarima yayi karaf yace "Duuuukana yayi A-a-anan "


Sarki yace "Duka kuma, to bazan yadda ba kuwa saina ci masa mutumci"


Ya kalli Nasrin yace "Me kika yi wa Sumayya ta dake ki"


Nasrin tayi shiru dan tana kunyar ta fada wa Sarki suna shiga bayi tare


Ganin haka yasa Ummi tace "wai dan bata gama abin ci da wuri bane "


Sarki yace "Kira min Sumayyan"



Ummi ta tashi ta shiga dakin da kanta yaga sai lokacin ta tashi tace "Ga can me martaba a falo yana son magana dake kuma yanzun nan zaki fito"


Tana gama fada ta juya Sumayya a ranta tace "Wannan matar zanyi maganinta"


Sumayya kuwa fuska ta wanke kawai ta fito dan so take ta tonawa Nasrin asiri


Tana zuwa Yarima ya tashi ya koma gun Nasrin ya kama hannunta ya makala a wuyansa ya kwanta jikinta ita kuwa duk kunya ya kama ta, Da Ummi ta fahimci haka tace "Yarima me haka tashi a jikinta mana "


Yarima ya harareta ya makale kafada yace "Naki"



Sarki yace "Ina ruwanki dasu"

Sannan ya juya ya kalli Sumayya yace "Me yasa kike dukan Nasrin "


Sumayya ta fadi karya da gaskiya dan har cewa tayi ta kamasu suna Zina


Nasrin ta rufe idonta hawaye masu zafi suka fito tayi shiru tana jiran abin da sarki zai yanke shi kenan yau tasan za'a rabata da Yarimanta

Page 2⃣3⃣


Sarki yace " To Sumayya mutumin da bashi da lafiya menene dan ta masa wani abu,  ni a ganina ko yankan naman jikinsa tayi tayi daidai tunda ita keda Alhakin kula dashi"


Sarki ya kara da cewa ko kina bukatar a raba aurenku da shi ne


Gimbiya ta girgiza kai


Yace idan kina bukata za'a raba dan vazamu tauyeki ba



Tace "Zan jirashi har ya samu sauki"


Yace "to shikenan daga yau ba ruwanki dasu, idan mijinki ya samu lafiya zai dawo gare ki"


Gimbiya batace komai ba sai bakin cikin rashin daukan mataki da yaki yi 

Tashi tayi tabar gurin Ummi tayi murmushi Sarki kuwa baice komai ba ya tashi ya fita, Ummi kuwa ta dafa Nasrin tace "Kiyi hakuri kinji, kedai komai zakiyi kiyi tsakanin ki da Allah "


Nasrin ta rintse idonta hawayen da take boye wa suka zuba 



Ummi ta kama hannunta tace "Nasrin me haka nace kiyi hakuri "


Yarima ma ya dago yanashare mata hawayen da rigarsa



Gimbiya kuwa koda ta je gun Sarauniya ta fada mata komai. Sarauniya tayi tsaki tace "Me zaki zo kina fada min wannan magana, ba kin zabi zama da mahaukaci ba, kadan kenan kika gani


Zuwa yanzu Gimbiya ta fara yadda da Maganan su dan haka tace "Ay nima na fada ne kawai dan kada suce bana tare da dansu"


Sarauniya ta harareta tace "Zauna nan kiyi biyu babu, ba Sarauta ba Miji dan nan bada dadewa ba Sarauta zata dawo gurin Marwan dan Sarki ya tsufa"



Gimbiya tayi shiru tana tunani tabbas Sarki ya nan gaba kadan za'a ba Marwan idan kuwa ba haka ba Sarki zai iya mutuwa kuma idan ya mutu dole Marwan ne da Sarauta, tunda ba'a baiwa mahaukaci sarauta kuma Yarima baida ranan warkewa, gwara ta ringa lallaba Marwan idan ya samu sarauta ta zai iya nada ta sarauniya, tunda ko a gidansu ba wani karfi gareta ba tunda ita 'yar kwarkwara ce kuma har yau ba aure tsakanin Sarki da Mahaifiyarta 


A haka Sarauniya ta fita ta barta har Marwan ya shigo bata sani ba saida yayi tafi saita jiyo a firgice yace "Tunanin me kike"


Tace "A gaida Yarima me jiran gado"


Ya juya ya kalli bayansa ba kowa yace "Mijin kin fa, da Marwan kike tare ba Amar ba"


Tace "Kuma in ina tare dashi, zanyi haka da mahaukaci ne"


Yace "to shine me sarautar mula ay"


Tace "A haukan za'a nada shi"

Yace "kin dawo kan hanya kenan"


Yace "ya maganan soyayyar mu"

Tace "Tana nan, kana tunanin zanci gaba da zama da mahaukaci ne"


Haka suka ci gaba da hiransu suna sukan Yarima karshe ma a jikinsa ta kwanta 




Ha rayuwa taci gaba inda Yarima kullum yana makale da Nasrin ita kuma komai zata iya yi dan ta faranta masa, kuma a kullum Addu'a take masa Allah ya bashi lafiya, saidai tana tunanin duk ranan da Yarima ya samu lafiya rabuwa zasuyu, kuma yanzu tana ganin bazata iya rabuwa dashi ba ko na second dayane


Da yamma Suna dawowa daga gun Ummanta Yarima ya hango wata Baiwa ta goya kanwarta suna tafiya Yarima ya kalli Nasrin yace "Zoooo nima Innnnn go-goyaki"


Nasrin tace "Taya zaka iya goyona Yarima "


Ayko ya tada rigima yana nima ya fadi kasa. Da sauri ta rike shi tace "Ka bari muje daki ka goya ni kaji Yarimana"


Yayi shiru suna shiga falon ya duka Yace "Too-tooohhh mu-mu-munnnzoo sehhh kihau "


Tace "Yarima bana son Abin da zai wahalar dakai"


Ya turo baki yana bubbuga kafa yayi cikin dakinsu


Tace "ay saidai kayi fushinka"

Abincin dare ma da hannunsa yaci saboda yana fushi da ita 


Dare ne yayi yana kwance Nasrin tana gefensa tace "Yarima wai yau me ya hana ka barci ne da wuri ko har yanzu fushin ne"


Yace "Tooo-toto ba-ba-bake bane kin -ki-ki ingoyaki ba"



Ta leko fuskarsa tace "Shine har yanzu kake fushi dani"


Ya rufe fuskarsa wai duk a cikin fushi


Tace " zo ka goyani bazan iya bari kayi fushi dani ba"


Da sauriya tashi ya duka a gaban gado ita kuma ta hau kan gadon ta tsaya sannan tahau bayansa ya mike a hankali ya tashi abinka da marar nawi ya ringa zagaya dakin da ita, harda yin gudu sai dariya suke. Kwata-kwata Nasrin ta manta tare da Gimbiya suke gaba daya dariyansu ya cika wa Gimbiya da Marwan kunne, Gimbiya tace "kodai itama ta koma mahaukaciya ce"


Marwan yace "muje mu duba"


Suna shiga suka ga Marwan ya goyata suna dariya kamar wasu masoyan da suka shekara da shekaru  suna soyayya 



Yarima ya sauke Nasrin yace "ki-ki-kice mu-mu-su su fita mana a daki"


Gimbiya tace "Yarima zo mu fita kasan shi zai iya dukanmu da abin da yagani kusa dashi"


Marwan Yace "Nifa Na kyasa da Yarinyar nan bazan fita ba saina dandani zumarta"


Ta harare shi tace "me zakayi da wannan karamar Yarinyar 


Ya kalli kirjinta yaga yadda ya cika yace "Ni naga Abin da na gani ay"


Nasrin kuwa komawa tayi bayan Yarima 


Gimbiya kuwa cewa tayi ni na tafi


Yace "Kin bar min ita


Tace "Na bar maka ita ku kwana ma"



Yace "wannan mahaukacin fa"


Tayi murmushi tace "Wlh in kaga dama ma ka kashe shi"


Tana gama fada ta fita tare da jan koma


Marwan kuwa yin inda Nasrin take yayi ita kuwa ta fara kuka tana Magiya tana cewa dan Allah kada kamin fyade ka kyale mu kada ka cutar damu"



Yarima kuwa dama tun ranan da Marwan ya fitar masa da jini yake tsoron sa.


Marwan yana zuwa ya fincike Yarima ya yi gefe dashi yayin da Nasrin kuma tayi kasa tana ihu kamar ranta zai fita

Page 2⃣4⃣

Jin ihun da Yarima yaji Nasrin tayi ne yasa ya bude kofa ya fita kicin ya nufa yayi kan Marwan dashi Marwan kuwa da yaga haka sai ya nemi kare kansa dan yasan Yarima zai iya yin komai dan ba hankali gare shi ba. Da gudu ya fita daga dakin ya shiga dakin Gimbiya yana shiga ya rufe kofar


Shiko Ya bishi Nasrin ma ta bishi a baya yana zuwa kofar dakin Yarima ya ja ya tsaya yace "Da din mana ka tsaya ka gani idan ban yanka maka wuya ba, kazo kayiwa Nasrin dina mugunta ne irin inda kamin"


Nasrin ta tsaya ta rufe bakinta da tafin hannunta tace "Yarima maganarka ta dawo daidai"


Yace "ki kyale ni da wannan yaro kema ki daina jin tsoron sa"


Yana kara fadan haka Nasrin ta fadi kasa tayi sujjada, shima yayi yadda tayi tana tashi shima ya tashi suka shiga daki


Suna zuwa ya kwanta ita kuma ta karato masa Addu'a ta shafa masa



Da sassafe Ta tashi ta shiga kicin tana aikinta Marwan ya shi yana zuwa ya shiga kicin din ya cire mata dan kwali ya kama gashinta yaja ya dauki wuka ya sa mata a wuya yace "Idan naji kin fada wa wani abin daya faru jiya saina yanka ki, ay a gabanki na yanka wani daya so ya shiga harkata"


Yana gama fada ya cillata gefe ya fita abinsa, 


Tayi murmushi tace "Bazan fada ba amma ina tare da Yarima ba me taba ni ya kwana lafiya"



Ta tashi taci gaba da aikinta 



Haka rayu taci gaba musu inda take cin gaba da kula da Yarima shi kuwa Alhamdulillah Yana samun sauki, Yanzu maganarsa daidaine amma kuma irin ta Yara ne, kuma yanzu ya daina zubar da miyau. Idan ka ganshi baza ka taba ce masa mahaukaci ba harsai yayi magana haka Ummanta ma takan taimaka mata ta wani Abun

Ganin hakane yasa Sarki yace "yakamata ya fara zuwa fada"


Randa zai fara zuwa kuwa Nasrin ta shirya shi da kayan sarauta suka kafin taje raka shi tace "Yarima Idan anje gurin sarki ba'a magana da kuwa kayi shiru sai sarki ya tambayeka sannan ka bashi amsa, kaji"

Ya daga ta kai


Tace "Idan kuma kayi Magana da wani ina kallonka daga nan ba ruwana da kai"



Ya sake daga kai



Abinda yasa ta fada masa haka tasan A suruntunsa zai iya tona musu sirrinsu ita dashi kuma zai iya fadan maganan da baidace ba a matayinsa na Yarima 


Suna Isa fada aka ringa gaishe shi Shiko bai amsa ba, suko basu damu ba saima kara gaishe shi suke da Adduan samun lafiya. dan sunsan Yarima ba haka yake ba.



Nasrin tana gaishe da me martaba zata yafi Yarima ya kama hannunta wai zai bita tadan duka a hankali tace "Yarima ka zauna anan"


Yace "Ni saina biki"


Ta bata rai tace "Ka zauna ga Sarki nan, Anjima zanzo mu tafi, idan kuma ka bini ba ruwana da kai bazan sake baka madarata ba kuma"



Ya zauna yace "To amma ki dawo da wuri"


Tace "To Yarimana"



Sarki ki yayi murmushi yace "Yarima na son madara tun yana yaro gashi yanzu ma kina lallashi da madara"


Murmushi tayi ta tashi ta tafi


Yarima yana zaune gefen Sarki kowa yazo gaishe shi amma Yarima baya magana wai shi a dole yabi Umurnin Nasrin saboda tace tana kallonsa


Koda sarki yaga baya magana yace "Yadai Magaji lafiya daiko"



Yace "Nasrin tace idan nayi magana da wani tana kallona kuma baza sake bani madara ba. Tace dakai kawai zanyi magana"



Sarki yace "To kayi shirunka Nasrin tayi daidai"


Ana haka Marwan ya shigo da sauri Yarima ya tashi ya koma bayan sarki Sarki ya kalli Marwan yace "Marwan kana cin zalin Magaji"


Marwan yace "Aa shidai kawai baya son ganina ne"



Sarki yace "Yarima dawo ka zauna ba abin da zai maka," yace " to kana kallon sa fa"


Sarki yace "Ina kallonsa "



Yarima ya dawo ya zauna saidai yana Kallon Inda marwan yake



Bangaren Nasrin kuwa da ta koma yaji duk ba dadi saboda ba Yarima lalle tana da babban aiki a gabanta dan tasan cewa Yarima yana samun sauki rabuwa zasuyi na har abada. Ta kwanta kan gado tayi ta juyi kamar taje ta dauko shi tace da Sarki zata masa wani abun ne, amma ba yadda zata yi kawai gwara ta kyale shi


Yarima kuwa koda aka kira Sallah Sarki da kansa ya jashi suka tafi masallaci, bayan an idar Sarki ya kamashi ya kaishi har sassan sa sannan ya sake shi ya shiga. Yana shiga Sumayya ya tarar a falo da gudu ya wuce ta yayi dakinsu yana fadin "Nasrin! Nasrin! na dawo batasan akwai Gimbiya a falo ba dan haka ta fito ta rumgumeshi tana dariya tace "Yarima dama haka kewa take, yau daya dana rabu dakai naji babu dadi"



Yace "Nasrin kinga yadda manya sukaita gaishe ni ne harda tsofi fa, kai gobe zaki kara kaini ko"



Tace "eh zan kara kaika amma sai in baza kayi magana da kowa ba"


Ay kuwa ya fara tsalle yana murna itama ta biye shi suka cika gidan da ihu,  ihunsu ya cikawa Gimbiya kunne ta tashi tace "kai dallah kun cika min kunne," ta nuna Nasrin tace "Ke kuma randa na kama ki ke daya saina karya ki ba kinga yana dukan mutane akanki bane yasa kike abin da kika ga dama"


Yarima ya kama hannunta yace "Nasrin mu tafi dakinmu" ya juyo ya kalli Gimbiya yace "ki cinye gidan ma shegiya da kwala-kwalan ido kamar na mujiya"


Yana gama fada yaja Nasrin da gudu suka shiga daki ya tura kofar


Ita kuwa taji haushin abin daya fada mata ta kwanta tana tunanin abin yi saiga Marwan ya shigo yana zuwa ya dagota yace "me ya faru ne Sarauniya ta"


Ta fada masa abinda ya faru yace "Dalla share wadancan mahaukatan, Nazo ne yau ki nuna min irin soyayyar da kike fada kina min"


Shiru tayi dan tana jin zafin abin da Yarima ya mata


Yace "Barin mantar dake haushin da kike ji"


Ya fara sumbatarta a wuya tuni ta fara mayar masa da martani saida ya fara cire mata kaya ta fara ja da baya tace "Uwar mahaukaci zata iya shigowa"


Ya kama hannunta ya kaita daki suka ci gaba da Abin suke


Ashe duk iskancin Sumayya bata bari wani ya cire mata budurcinta ba sai yau da Marwan  ya rabata dashi, yayin da tasha bakar wahala shi kuwa ya shiga cikin farin ciki da Annushuwa

Kuka take tsakaninta da Allah yana lallashinta da taki yin shiru yace "Barin je zan turo miki kuyangu su miki wanka, ni na tafi zanje gun ummana"


Haushi ne ya kamata tace "Marwan kai ya dace kamin komai ba kuyangu ba"


Yace "Haba Ina magajin Sarki in tsaya ina wahala, barin turo miki su kawai"


Yana fada ya sauka daga kan gadon ya fita.

Page 2⃣5⃣


Shiru tayi ta kwanta tana jin zafi a kasanta tana hawaye. 



Can kuyangu ne suka shigo guda biyu wanda dama daga gurin Sarauniya suke suka zo suka daga ta tana dan tsayawa kadan ta kwalla ihu suka rike ta suna bata hakuri 


Bayi suka kaita suka sata a ruwan zafi da kishiri tadan zabura dayar suka kara dannata cikin ransu kuwa sai tsine mata suke 


Bayan sun gasata suka suka mata wanka ba maganan wankan tsarki kan suka fito da ita kayan maras sa nauyi suka saka mata 


Ta kwanta sai barci


Da la'asar Sarki yazo ya sake daukan Yarima suka tafi masallaci ko da ya dawo dashi shiga yayi yana cewa Nasrin yanzu ma suna ya gaishe ni wai saboda me suke gaishe ni ne, Nasrin tayi murmushi tace "Saboda ka cancanta su gaishe ka ne "



Gimbiya kuwa sai karfe 5 ta tashi da kyar take takawa ta zo falo Nasrin ta gani Yarima na kwance a jikinta tazo ta zauna a daya daga cikin kujeran zatayi magana Yarima yace "Malama kinga dai mun rigaki zama anan yanzu kan saboda haka ki tashi kibar mana nan"


Nasrin tace "Haba Yarima wannan din fa matarka ce Gimbiya ka bari tana zuwa kusa da kai"


Yace "cab di Allah ya sauwaka wannan ta zama mata ta, kalleta fa, ay tamin..."


Nasrin ta katse tace "ya isa haka "

Gimbiyatace "kyale shi ya zagen in fasa masa baki Mahaukacin banza kawai "


Yace "Ke ce Mahaukaciya shegiya me kan bera "



Ta taso kamar zata dake shi yace "Idan kika zo min nan saina ballaki"


Tuni ta koma ta zauna tace " ke jashi kuyi daki dan Iska kawai"


Yace "ke ce... Nasrin ta rufe masa baki tace "haba Yarima kai baka ji ko"


Yace "to ki mata magana ta daina zagina"



Nasrin bata ce komaiba sai janshi da tayi sukayi daki suna tace "Haba Yarima ka daina mata haka mana tana da matsayi fa a gunka matarka ce ta sunna me yasa kake mata haka"



Ya fizge hannunsa yace "me yasa zakina ce min matata Allah ya kiyaye min ta zama mata ta, ni bazan yi aure ba ma"


Tayi saurin rufe masa baki daina fada Yarima aure kan zaka yi Allah ya baka lafiya dai


Gimbiya kuwa bata kara ganin Marwan  ba sai washe gari da daddare yana zuwa ya sameta a dakinta ta kwanta ya shafi kuncinta yace "ya jikin naki"


Tace "kazo ne kaga ko naji sauki ka sake nakasa ni"


Yayi murmushi yace "Haba tawa baxa muyi haka ba, da din ma saboda Umma na nema nane kiyi hakuri"


Haka yayita lallashinta har saida ta sauko daga bisani kuma ya ringa sumbatarta koda taga zai wuce gona da iri ta fara masa magiya ay ko bai saurareta ba, saida ya cika burinsa saidai bata ji wani zafi ba 



Haka suka ci gaba da Rayuwa inda Sarki ke zuwa daukan Yarima suje masallaci har Sallar asuba, da asuba kuwa idan ya dawo ya samu Nasrin tana karatu zama yake yace saita koya masa haka zata zauna tana koya masa yana amsawa, 



Gimbiya kuwa yanzu da Marwan take kwana a dakin ta 


Saboda karatun da Yarima yake ne yasa yanzu ya fara samun nutsuwa yana iya yin komai da kansa, yanzu Nasrin ma ta daina kwana dashi a gado saidai ta shimfida bargo a kasa ta kwanta. yanzu idan kaganshi baza ka taba tunanin ya taba ciwo ba balle kasan akwai ciwo akansa komai lafiya saidai hankalinsa ne da be dawoba



Sarauniya da taga haka ta koma gun bokanta ta masa bayanin Yarima fa yana son ya samu sauki, boka ya hada mata magunguna ya bata koda ta koma ta kira Gimbiya 


Koda tazo tace "Sumayya ga wannan maganin, ki zuba wa Yarima a Abinci ko abinsha idan yaci zai na tsoron ki kuma bazaina tsallake umurninki ba, sannan kinga ya fara samun sauki ko ya samu sauki umurninki zaina bi ki gwada ki gani"



Gimbiya tace "Dama na rasa yadda zanyi tunda naga ya fara samun sauki kada ya samu lafiya yaga bana kula dashi yace baya sona"



Sarauniya tace "ki gwada wannan 'yata"


Tace "To ay kuwa zanyi yadda kika ce Nagode "



Gimbiya kuwa tana zuwa ta kira Nasrin koda tazo tace "Ga wannan magani ne na amsarwa Yarima ki saka masa a madaran sa ya sha ance zai samu sauki"


Nasrin ta amsa tace "Allah yasa mu dace"


Da yamma fulanin dake kawo madara ya kawo Nasrin ta tsiyaya masa a kofi ta sa maganin  nufi daki dan ta kai masa...
