Page 2⃣6⃣




Umma ce ta shigo sai ta dawo da kofin a hannunta Umma tace "Ina Yariman"



Nasrin tace "Yana daki zan kai masa madaransa ne ma"


Dake kofin ana kallom abim dake ciki Umma ta hango abubuwa suna yawo a ciki


Umma tace "Nasrin muga Madaran nan"


Nasrin ta mika mata, Umma ta amsa ta duba tace "Nasrin me a ciki"


Nasrin tace "Magani ne "


Umma tace "waya baki wannan maganin kuma "



Nasrin tace "Gimbiya ce"



Umma tayi sauri taja da baya tace "Gimbiya kuma! Nasrin baki da hankali ne, Gimbiya ta baki abu ki zuba masa kuma ki zuba masa saboda ciwon hauka ya kama ki"



Nasrin tace "Umma meke faruwa"


Umma batace komaiba ta fita da madaran taje ta zubar ta dawo tace "ki tsiyayi wani ki kai masa kuma ba a wannan kofinba ki bashi a wani kofin kizo muyi magana"


Nasrin tayi yadda tace "mata takai masa ta dawo"


Nasrin ta zauna kusa daita tace "Umma gani"


Umma tace "Kinga ciwon Yariman nan, ba komai bane illa asiri ne aka masa kuma ina tunanin da sa hannun matarsa a ciki"


Nasrin ta gwalo ido tace "Umma matarsa ce fa"


Umma tace "Tunda Yarima ya samu wannan ciwon ta taba zuwa duba shi"


Nasrin tace Aa


Tace "ta taba kawo masa magani"


Nasrin tace Aa 


To bari kiji yanzu taga ya fara samun lafiya ne yasa tace "bari ta kara haukatar dashi, amma hasashe nane ya nuna min haka, ban tabbatar ba,  haba Nasrin kinsanfa wacece Gimbiya ya kamata ki dagota, Yanzu abin da nake son dake ki nuna mata kin bashi kuma idan ta kawo miki wasu magunguna ki amsa ki ringa kawo min "



Nasrin tace "to Umma "



Umma ta mata Sallama, Nasrin tace "Ummi baza ki shiga ki ga Yarima ba"


Tace "kyale shi ya huta "


Nasrin kuwa shiga tayi ta sameshi a kan gado ya jingina baya da gado ta isa ta kwanta a jikinsa Tace "Yarimana ana son nida hannuna in saka a matsala"


Yace "wa yake son ki sani a matsala"


Tace "makiyanka"


Yace "Allah ya fisu "


Tace "Kaidai Allah ya baka lafiya, kullum abin da nake addu'a kenan Allah ya baka lafiya, saidai Nasan duk lokacin da ka samu lafiya ni nawa ciwon zai tashi, saboda bazan iya rayuwa ba kaiba"


Yace "Wai wa yace miki bani da lafiyane, kuma wa yace miki zan rabu dake,"



Ya kamo hannunta yace "Nasrin me raba ni da ke, sai Allah saboda haka ki cire tunanin zamu rabu,"


Tayi murmushi saidai cikin ranta tana tausayin kanta duk lokacin da aka ce Yarima ya samu lafiya 


Washe gari Zata shiga kicin Gimbiya ta kira ta tace "Ke kin yi masa amfani da maganin nan"


Nasrin tace "Eh ranki ya dade ya sha ma"


Gimbiya tace "Ya to kina ganin sauki kuwa"


Nasrin a ranta tace "kaji makira, to barin nuna miki nima na iya duniyanci"


Tayi fuskar tausayi tace "Saidai ina bashi yana sha jikinshi ya rikice, kamar ma ciwon ya dawo sabo"


Gimbiya tayi saurin tashi ta fita da sauri Nasrin kuwa tace "Makiran mata, wlh duk ranan dana samu dama saina nakasa ta"


Gimbiya kuwa wajen sarauniya tayi tana zuwa ta durkusa tace "Ki binciki bokan nan ina tunanin be baki maganin daidaiba"


Sarauniya tace "me kika gani "



Gimbiya tace "jikin nasa ya kara tashi ciwon ya koma sabo"


Sarauniya a ranta farin ciki tayi saidai a fili tace "Ayya kice baki gane ba,  ay dama yace saboda mahaukaci ne shi ciwon nasa zai dan dawo sabo kafin ya dawo daidai, yana miki biyayya"



Gimbiya  tayi shiru tana tunani,

Sarauniya tace "munyi shekara a tare dake amma har yanzu kamar baki yadda dani ba. Ni kuma a raina na dauke ki kamar 'yata ta cikina ne amma ke kina tunanin zan cuceki"


Gimbiya ta karyar da murya tace "kiyi hakuri na tsorata ne wlh"


Sarauniya ta kauda kai tace "Sumayya tashi ki tafi gun mahaukacin mijinki, su mulke ki shi da uwarsa"


Tace "Aa kada kimin haka wlh ba nufina ba kenan"



Haka tayi ta mata magiya har saida ta nuna mata ta hakuri 


Nasrin kuwa daki ta koma ta samu Yarima tace "Yarima na,  daga yanzu ka daina fita falo bana son makiyanka suna kallonka, idan zaka tafi Masallaci kuma kana bari ina duba ma, sai ba kowa zaka fita"


Yace "Wai me kike tsoro ne"


Tace" Yarima kayi abin dana ce kawai bana son musu, makiyanka suna son ganin bayanka"


Yace "To shikenan "


Haka Yarima yabi Umurnin Nasrin ko fada idan zaije sai yace "Ki duba min ko makiyana suna nan"


Takan fita ta duba musu idan taga ba kowa sai tadawo tace "zo ka tafi ba kowa"


Haka idan zai dawo ma da sanda yake shigowa, sai yaga ba kowa a falon sai ya shigo


Haka sukaita 'yar buya, itako Gimbiya shiru tayi dan tana jiran taga Yarima ya nemeta dan ta mulke shi . amma shiru take ji


Sarauniya kuwa murna take Yarima ya koma gidan jiya duk da bata ganshi ba,



Gimbiya kuwa ta gaji da jiran Yarima dan haka Marwan yana fita dama a gun ta ya kwana ta nufi dakinsu tana shiga ta ganshi a kan gado a zaune yayin da Nasrin kuma ke bashi madara, tana bashi a kofi yana kurba, zama tayi a bakin gadon, nan da nan Karnin madaran ya hautsina mata ciki tayi yunkurin amai, sai tayi bayi da gudu, Yarima kuma ya kwashe da dariya yace "kin gani ko ban fara magana ba ma kin fara ji a jikinki, ke ni ganina kawai mutum yayi sai yaji a jikinsa balle ace yazo neman fada na, idan ba niman fada ba me ya kawoki dakinmu, gashi kin gani ay Allah ya kara"



Nasrin kuwa bata saurare shi ba tabi Gimbiya ta kama kafadarta tana mata sannu


Bayan sun fito Yarima yace "kinzo neman masifa gashi masifar ta kare ka kanki"


Yana magana yana yarfa mata hannunsa


Nasrin tace "Yarima me haka"


Yace "kyale ni da wannan me idon mayun tukun"


Nasrin ta harare shi, shima hararanta yayi yace "In ke kina tsoronta ita bata isa inji tsoronta ba,"


Ya nuna Gimbiya da hannu yace "Tashi kibar min dakinmu"


Gimbiya tace "bazan tashi ba, mahaukacin banza kawai"


Ay ko yace "keeee nine mahaukaci, " ya nufeta Nasrin ta rike shi, ita kuma da sauri ta tashi ta fita, shiko cewa yake "kinyi Sa'a an rike ni da saina balla ki, yanzu ma in kika sake muka hadu saina balla miki kafa"


Gimbiya kuwa tana fita falo jiri ya kwashe ta ta fadi kasa, Nasrin kuwa tana ta fama da Yarima a daki 


Gimbiya kuwa tana ji tana gani ta kasa tashi ga wani azababben ciwon kai da yake damun ta. 



Ummi ne ta shigo kai tsaye dakin su Yarima tayi tana zuwa bakin kofa taga Gimbiya da sauri ta isa gareta ta daga ta tana cewa "Sumayya lafiya kuwa"


Ta dago ta tallafi kan ta, ita kuwa gimbiya ta dago idon suka hada ido da Ummi, da sauri Ummi ta mikara da ita ta zauna tace "Sumayya ciki gareki, bayan mijinki bashi da lafiya"

[14/12 18:35] Matar Aleey✍🏻: 🌐 _*🇭ιĸιмaн 🇼rιтer'ѕ 🇦ѕѕocιaтιon*_
   🌐( _*Kυngιya Daya Taмĸar Dυвυ*_)

loadιng...........✍🏻

 
  🌐 IZZAR MULKI
           💓💓💓💓

Story 
        &
          Writing by
UMMU MUFEED
      (Mrs Aliyu)


Wannan page din naki ne daughter teemar ina sonki over 🥰🥰🥰


*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 2⃣7⃣



Gimbiya ta waro ido tana kallon Ummi, tabbas tasan cikin Marwan ne, amma baza ta iya rabuwa da Yarima ba duk da yana cikin hauka kuma dole ciki ya dawo nasa



Ummi ta jijjigata tace "Sumayya ya aka yi kika yi ciki "



Gimbiya fashewa tayi da kuka cikin kuka tace "Ummi me yasa kike tambayana bayan kinsan ina da miji"


Ummi ta waro ido tace "Naga mijinki bashi da hankali ya za'a yi ya miki ciki"


Sumayya kuka ta kara fashewa dashi tana kuka tana cewa "Na shiga uku Yarima ka cuce ni, shikenan yanzu gashi za'a sheganta min dan cikina, nasan da wuya za'a yadda nakane tunda baka da lafiya, kaima idan ka samu sauki bazaka yadda naka bane"



Ummi ta sassauta murya tace "Ya isa haka tashi muje dakinki ki kwanta sai fada min"



Ummi ta dagata suka tafi dakinta ta kwatar da ita tace "Sumayya, ina ce Yarima da baiwar ki Nasrin yake kwana, to yaya banga ciki a jikin Nasrin ba kuma na gani a jikin ki"



Gimbiya sunkuyar da kanta tace "Ummi kiyi hakuri da abin da zan fada miki, Duk da bama kwana tare, amma ni ina zuwa dakinsa da daddare idan Nasrin tana barci, saboda bana iya hakuri dashi, nakanyi wasa da sassan jikinsa, ina koya masa abubuwa da zaina min har ya koya...." tana zuwa nan ta kuma fashewa da kuka tana cewa cikin kuka "Ummi kiyi hakuri"



Ummi ta kauda kanta tace "Ya isa haka Sumayya Yarima mijinki ne, duk abin da kikayi dashi kinyi daidai, Allah ya raba lafiya barin samo miki magani, kwanta



Ta kwanta tana nishi da kyar



Ummi kuwa ta fita tana tunanin rashin kunyar Sumayya da ta iya fada mata wannan magama amma wata zuciyar tace "ay ba komai tunda saboda ta fitar da kanta daga zargi ne"



Sumayya kuwa Ummi tana fita tayi dariya harda shewa tace "Yarima ka shirya amsar magaji"



Nasrin kuwa bata san wainar da ake toya wa ba, ita dai taji kukan Gimbiya da maganganu amma batasan me tace cewa ba,


Ummi kuwa zuwa tayi ta samo mata magani ta ta kawo mata, a kwance ta same ta a hankali ta dagota tace "sha zai taimaka miki wajen samun karfin jiki, dakyar ta samu tasha sanda ta tabbatar tasha sannan Ummi ta kyaleta ta fita da maganin cikin ranta kuwa bata so wannan cikin ba taso shine lokacin da uban cikin ya samu lafiya a same shi a jikin Nasrin 



Zuwa tayi ta samu sarki ta fada masa cikin Sumayya Sarki ya mike da sauri yace "Ban yadda ba tayaya Yarima zai iya saduwa da mace bayan baya cikin hayyacinsa "



Ummi ta kwantar da murya tafada masa Abin da Sumayya tace mata sannan tace "kuma ranka ya dade ba yadda za muyi mu ja da ikon Allah"



Sarki yace "Shikenan aci gaba da kula da ita, har Allah ya sauketa lafiya, amma fa Aisha har yanzu zuciyata tana raya min abubuwa da dama"


Tace "ka yakice wannan a ranka shedan ne, kuma nima haka nake ji a zuciyata"



Yace "To akula da Sumayya Allah yaba Magaji lafiya"



Tace Amin


Yarima yana falo kwance akan cinyar Nasrin Gimbiya ta fito ta zauna kujera Yarima ya kalleta sai yaja tsaki ya harareta Nasrin tace "Yarima me haka "



Gimbiya tace "kyale mahaukaci kawai ka kusa daina raina ni, tunda yanzu haka ina da cikinka na kusa haifa maka yaro"



Da sauri Nasrin ta mike har saida Yarima ya fadi kasa ya buga kansa tace "Gimbiya kin kusa haifa masa yaro kuma, tayaya Yariman da bashi da lafiya"


Gimbiya ta dubi Yarima daya dafe kai tace "Banza mahaukaciya zaki kashe min miji"


Da sauri Nasrin ta durkusa ta dafe inda yake dafewa ta mikar dashi sukayi daki ta kwantar dashi tayi ta mulmula masa har barci ya kwashe, sannan Nasrin ta fito ta samu Gimbiya tace "Gimbiya wai me kike cewa"


Gimbiya tace "Zaki dawo da rashin kunya ko"



Nasrin ta kwantar da murya tace "A'a ba haka bane ranki ya dade, gani nayi Yarima bashi da lafiya ta yaya zai samu Yaro"



Gimbiya tace "To idan ma menene To Ina da cikin Yarima kuma zan haifa masa a gidan nan"


Nasrin tace "Haba Gimbiya wlh badai Yarima ba"



Tace "To ubanki ne"


Nasrin tace "oho ki nemi Uban danki amma wlh ba Yarima ba, Dan nasan komai tsakaninki da Yarima"



Gimbiya tace "Da daddare nake zuwa dakinsa kina barci a kasa nake hawa gadonsa"


Nasrin tace "To wlh karya kike tunda Yarima ya samu tabin hankali tare muke kwana akan gadonsa, ni banyi ciki ba sai ke, to wlh ki nemi uban danki"


Gimbiya tace "Ni kike yiwa magana haka"



Ta taso gadan gadan Nasrin tayi dakinsu da gudu ta kulle dakinsu



Gimbiya ta dawo tana jin haushin abinda Nasrin ta mata kuma tayi alwashin saita yi mata mummunar hukunci



Nasrin kuwa tayi daki tana tunanin yaushe Gimbiya take shigowa dukda tasan tana da makullin dakin 


Ta kalli Yarima tace "Bazai yuyu ba Ba yadda za'ayi ka sadu da Gimbiya ban sani ba, wannan uban son mulkin nata"


Kwanciya tayi gefen sa bata dade ba aka kira magrib ta tashi tayi saidai ta tashi Yarima amma bai tashi ba haka ta kyaleshi, har isha nan ma ta tashe shi shiru bai tashi ba, kuma koda ta dube shi barcinsa yake hankalinsa kwance ta zauna tana jiran ya tashi ta bashi abinci amma har karfe sha biyu, ta kwanta a kasa barci ya kwashe ta sai asuba tana tashi taga Yarima har yanzu barci yake ta tashi tayi raka'atainil fajr, sannan tayi asuba taje ta kama hannun sa ta rike tana jijjigawa ta shiru tace "Yarima dan Allah ka tashi kada kasa zuciyata ta karye ta shafi fuskarsa taga shi ta bubbuga shi nan ma shiru, tasa kunnenta a kirjinsa taji zuciyarsa yana bugawa, taji sanyi a ranta ta koma ta jingina da gado ta fara jero karatunta cikin kira'a me dadi, yau ta kasa tashi ta shiga kicin har karfe takwas tana karatu


Karfe Takwas da minti goma Yarima ya farka yana tashi yaji muryar Nasrin tana karatu ya saukar da kafa kasa cikin kasaitacciyar muryarsa yace "Nasrinnnnn"


Da sauri ta juyo dan taji irin muryansa na da can da yake da ita, dan cikin kasaita yayi maganar



Nasrin tace "Yarima nahhh,"

  🌐 IZZAR MULKI
           💓💓💓💓

Story 
        &
          Writing by
UMMU MUFEED
      (Mrs Aliyu)


Wannan Page din naki ne Smart beauty ina jin dadin comment dinki sabo da haki kiyi yadda kike so dashi na baki kyauta, nasan kina son Matar Aleey, to sha kuruminki, matar Aleey ma tana yinki irin tohhhhhtally din na💛💗💔



*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*




Page 2⃣8⃣



Yarima shiru yayi yana mata kallon Mamaki



Ta iso ta zauna a bakin gadon ta gefen sa tace "Yarimanah, muje kayi wanka ko"


Tana magana ta sa hannu tana faman cire masa riga, cikin ransa kuwa tunani yake "yaushe Nasrin ta zama haka, kai wannan ba Nasrin bace saidai Yayarta ne, dan Nasrin Yarinya ce bata kai wannan ba, wannan kuwa komai na budurci ya bunkasa a jikinta,



Har ya gama cire masa riga yana tunani sai ji yayi ta sa hannunta a kugunsa tana nema ta cire masa wando, da sauri ya rike hannunta Yana kallonta, ita kuwa murmushi tayi tace "Yarima kada Gimbiya tayi fushi damu, kaga banyi aiki ba, ka tsaya in cire maka kaya kayi wanka, sai ka huta kafin ka je fada na gama maka abin da zaka ci"



Kallonta ya sake yi ba tare da yayi magana ba kuma, saida kuma ya tuna a dakin Gimbiya ya kwanta bayan ya gama shan madara yaji kansa na ciwo daga nan barci ya kwashe shi, 


Koda Nasrin taga haka tace "Yarima meke damunka kodai wani abu kake so"


Nan ma shiru yayi



Nasrin kuwa ta bata raitare da mikewa tace "Shikenan Yarima tunda kaki min magana ba ruwana da kai, kuma bazan baka madarata ba"



Ta tashi ta fita a dakin wai ita tayi fushi. Tana fita shi kuwa tashi yayi ya shiga bayi yayi alola yayi sallah asuba, dan a tunaninsa ana binsa ita, bayan yayi addu'o'i da neman gafaram Allah ya tashi yayi wanka yasa kayan sa sannan ya tsaya yana duba dakin komai ya canja masa yace a fili "Meke faruwa dani ne, wa zai bani wannan amsa, daga jiya jiya, komai ya canja"



Fita yayi Nasrin tana kicin har ya fita bata sani ba, wanda ita kuwa tunanin Yarima take ko me ya same shi



Kasa jurewa tayi ta koma dan ta duba shi sai taga wayam tayi murmushi tace "Yaje gun sarki kenan, gashi da yunwa a cikinka"



Yarima kuwa dakin sarki yaje a hanya dogarawa suna gaishe yana suna cewa Allah ya baka lafiya Magajin Sarki


Wani dogari ya samu yace "Kaje ka cewa Sarki ina son magana dashi"



Koda dogarin Yaje isar da amsa durkuwa yayi yace "Ran sarki ya dade Yarima Amar yana cikin gida yace yana son ganinka "



Sarki yace "Yarima da rigima maimakon yaje, to barinje inji"



Tashi yayi ba bata lokaci, yana zuwa yaga Yarima a durkushe, Yarima kuma yana kallon Sarki ya shigo da sauri ya mike saida sarki ya zauna sannan ya tsuguna ya gaishe shi.



Sarki yaji mamakin wannan gaisuwa dan lokacin da yake da lafiya ne, yake masa wannan gaisuwar



Yarima Yace "Ranka ya dade, nazo ka fada min meke faruwa dani. A daren jiya bayan nasha madara na kwanta dakin matana, gashi har na makara kuma ina tashi na ganni a dakina tare da Baiwa Nasrin, kuma tana min wasu abubuwa wanda ba halinta ba, kuma na fito, mutane ke ce min Allah ya bani lafiya "



Tunda Ya fara magana sarki yayi shiru yana sauraronsa saida ya gama yace "Alhamdulillah Yarima ka samu lafiya kenan"



Yarima yace "na samu lafiya kuma "


Sarki ya bashi labarin komai kuma yace "Yarima Wannan abin da kake gani ba komai bane saidai asiri da aka maka, amma bamusan wanda ya maka ba. Kuma muna son sanin ko wanene Abin da nake so da kai kada ka bari wani yasan ka warke ka koma gun Nasrin kaci gaba da zama ita ma kada ka bari ta sani,koda Umminka ne kada ka bar ta sani"



Yarima yace "To Ranka ya dade"


Can kuma yace "Ranka ya dade Yanzu Nasrin matata ce"



Sarki yace "Yarima Idan baka sonta a matsayin matarka zaka iya sakinta saboda Abin da mukayi da Mahaifiyarta kenan, sannan Nasrin bata san ka aure ta ba saboda haka kabi a hankali kada ka bata mana shiri"



Yace "To Ranka ya dade zanyi yadda kace "



Ya tashi ya tafi yana tunani sai yanzu ya fara tuna abubuwan da yayi cikin tabin hankali kamar a mafarki



Yana shiga daki ya wuce yana shiga yaga Nasrin lokacin ya jera masa kumallonsa ya zauna a bakin gado ta harare shi da sauri yazo gabanta yace "Kiyi hakuri na tuba"



Dariya abin ya bata ta shafi fuskarsa tace "Nayi hakuri yarima na"



Ya shafa cikinsa yace "Yunwa nake ji"


Tace "Bazanka jirani nayi wanka nazo muci ba"



Ya makale kafada yace "Nidai yanzu"



Tace "To shikenan kasan bana son bacin ranka"



Ta zauna tana bashi yana ci cikin ransa kuwa yana sa mata Albarka 




Saida suka koshi, sannan ta kwashe kayan ta kai ta wanke ta dawo tace "Yarima barinje nayi wanka. Sai tashi yayi zai bita"



Tace "Yarima yaushe rabonka da ka rakani wanka, yaune tuna"


Ya bata rai kamar zaiyi kuka yace "Nasan ta taga zaki gudu waje"



Nasrin tayi dariya tace "Ka jika tayaya zan gudu "



Yace "Aa wlh ban yadda ba"



Tace "To naji zan bude bayi ka zauna a bakin gado ka ringa kallona kada na gudu"



Yace to



Tace "Rigimammen Yarima kawai"



Yace "eh din," cikin ransa kuwa farin ciki yake, dan dama da biyu yayi hakan dan ya karewa suran jikinta kallo



Ta bude kofar bayin ta shiga ta cire kayanta Yarima da ya kalleta Suman tsaye yayi cikin ransa kuwa dakyar ya furta "tsarki ya tabbata ga ubangijin daya halicci wannan halitta"



Nasrin ta fara wankanta shiko yana karewa duk kan jikinta kallo



Saida ta gama taga halamar baya hayyacinsa dan har kusa dashi ta matso ta yarfa masa ruwan hannunta tace "Kallon me kake min"



Sai jawota yayi ya sata a cinyarsa ya kai bakinsa kunnenta yace "Kin min kyaune"


Ta bugi kafadarsa tace "na fika kyaune"

Yace "Kin fini mana"



Tace "Barin shirya muje mu gaida Ummi "



Baiyi magana ba sai murginata da yayi kan gado yana sumbatar wuyanta. Wani irin yanayi taji a jikinta wanda yasata lumshe ido, ya gangaro kan kirjinta yana sumbatar tsakiyar kirjinta da sauri ta mike tace cikin daure fuska "Yarima me haka, Kasan bakyau fa"


Komawa gefe yayi yana meda numfashi sama-sama dan ta bukace yake 



Ita ko shiryawa tayi tasa kaya bayan ta gama tahau gadon tace "Yarimana tashi muje gun Ummi, shiru yayi dan ya kasa saboda munguwar sha'awata "



Ta masa kiss a kumatu tace "Yarima fushi kake dani, dan na hanaka abin da Allah baya so"



Yace "Bai haramta ba fa"



Tace "muje gun Ummi to"



Cikin karfin hali ta ya tashi yana cewa harda Umma ma"



Tare suka tafi suna rike da hannun junan su har suka isa gun Ummi Yarima bai sake mata hannu ba. Itama bata sake ba dan haka suka saba tafiya. Suna zuwa suka gaishe ta bayan sun dan taba hira yace "Muje gun Umma"



Ummi tace "Yau tun yanzu sai kace ana koranku"



Yace "Nasrin tashi mu tafi"



Nasrin kuwa ta biye masa Ummi tace "Ke din ma biye masa zaki yo"



Nasrin tace "Ummi kada yayi fushi"



Tafiya suka yi gun Umma nan ma basu dade ba yace "Su koma gidan su" dan shi so yake yaji jikinsu na manne dana juna



suna komawa kuwa akan kirjinsa ya kwantar da ita yana shafa bayanta, saidai ba karamin daurewa yayi ba gun danne sha'awarsa



Sallar Azhar ya fitar dashi, nan ma yana idarwa ya koma yana shiga falon yaga Gimbiya a falon zai wuce tace "Kai mahaukaci ina Mahaukaciyarka"



Kamar zaiyi magana sai kuma ya shige daki. Yana shiga yaga ta idar da sallah tana Addu'a ya tsaya suka shafa ta tare


Ta tashi zata fita yace "Ina zaki je "



Tace "Zan yi girki kaga yaufa kaki barina na yi aiki"


Yace "Musha madara mana"


Tace "idan mu idanmun sha to Gimbiya fa "


Ya bata rai, ita kuwa fita tayi shi kuma ya bita suka wuce Gimbiya a falo suka shiga kicin din, Gimbiya ta biyo su tace "Ke uban me kike da baki dora mana girki ba"



Yarima ya dauki wuka yace "kina zuwa saina yanka miki wuya, fita ki bar mana kicin"


Tace "Wawa kawai ay zamu hadu da ita ne saina nakasata"


Nasrin kuwa bata ce komai ba tana kokarin dofa tukunya 


Yarima yace "Saikin dafa min madara kafin ki dora wani girki"



Tace "An gama"



Ta dafa masa ta juye a abu me murfi ta tsiyaya masa a kofi yana sha da duminta ita kuma tana aiki



Sai la'asar ta gama ta tafi tayi sallah ta dawo ta zuba na Gimbiya ya wuce daki dana su, Yarima yana dawowa suka fito harabar gidan suna shakatawa



Sai Magrib Yarima ya tafi masjid ita kuma ta wuce ciki, saida akayi isha Yarima ya dawo ya samu Nasrin tana shirin shiga wanka, shima cire kayansa ya yi koda taga haka sai tace "To ka shiga ka fara "


Ya bata rai yace "yau ni zan miki wanka ba da ke kike min ba"


Ta waro ido tace "bazai yuyu ba"



Ya bata rai kamar zaiyi kuka. Da sauri tace "muje kamin"


Tayi gaba ya bita a baya, suna shiga ya cire mata kaya, ya mata wanka tas ya daukota ya kawota tsakiyar gado shima yaje yayi, koda ya dawo yaga har ta shirya tana son kwanciya a kasa yace "Anan fa zamu kwana yau bazaki kwanta kasa ba"



Tace "To muje" a niyarta idan yayi barci ta tashi



Saidai tana kwanciya rumgumeta yayi yana sumbatar ta ta ko ina ta bude baki zatayi magana ya hade bakunan su yana tsotsan harshenta. Tana son ta tureshi ta kasa, cikin salo da dabara ya rabata da kayan jikinta duka, idan zatayi magana saiya hade bakinsu waje daya, gani tayi yana neman cire gajeran wandonsa da ya rage a jikinsa tayi sauri tace "Yarima Haram"


Cikin kasala da kasaita yace "Ba haram bane Halak ne"



Zata sake magana taji shi yana shiganta, wani azaba ne taji wanda bata taba jinshiba tunda Uwarta ta haifeta, ihu tayi tay saidai shi bama jinta yayi ba dan ya fita hayyacin sa, saida ya gamsu ya tashi tare da kallon aika-aikan da yayi suna hada ido ta kauda kai, tausayin ta ne ya kama shi. Sam baiyi zaton zai sameta da budurcinta ba kasancewarta baiwa, da ya sani da ya kyale ta sai kowa yasan ita matar sa ce, da yasan da budurcinta da ya kyale ta anyi biki, in sun kwanta ya nuna a duniya cewa matar sa budurwa ce, Yanzu kenan haka za'a wanke zanin gadon nan a banza ba tare da masarauta sunga jinin budurcin Nasrin dinsa ba. Hawaye ne ya zuba masa yana ganin ya cuceta, indai ba ayi bikin budurcinta ba, ga kuma Alkwarin daya daukarwa Sarki...

Koda dogarin Yaje isar da amsa durkuwa yayi yace "Ran sarki ya dade Yarima Amar yana cikin gida yace yana son ganinka "



Sarki yace "Yarima da rigima maimakon yaje, to barinje inji"



Tashi yayi ba bata lokaci, yana zuwa yaga Yarima a durkushe, Yarima kuma yana kallon Sarki ya shigo da sauri ya mike saida sarki ya zauna sannan ya tsuguna ya gaishe shi.



Sarki yaji mamakin wannan gaisuwa dan lokacin da yake da lafiya ne, yake masa wannan gaisuwar



Yarima Yace "Ranka ya dade, nazo ka fada min meke faruwa dani. A daren jiya bayan nasha madara na kwanta dakin matana, gashi har na makara kuma ina tashi na ganni a dakina tare da Baiwa Nasrin, kuma tana min wasu abubuwa wanda ba halinta ba, kuma na fito, mutane ke ce min Allah ya bani lafiya "



Tunda Ya fara magana sarki yayi shiru yana sauraronsa saida ya gama yace "Alhamdulillah Yarima ka samu lafiya kenan"



Yarima yace "na samu lafiya kuma "


Sarki ya bashi labarin komai kuma yace "Yarima Wannan abin da kake gani ba komai bane saidai asiri da aka maka, amma bamusan wanda ya maka ba. Kuma muna son sanin ko wanene Abin da nake so da kai kada ka bari wani yasan ka warke ka koma gun Nasrin kaci gaba da zama ita ma kada ka bari ta sani,koda Umminka ne kada ka bar ta sani"



Yarima yace "To Ranka ya dade"


Can kuma yace "Ranka ya dade Yanzu Nasrin matata ce"



Sarki yace "Yarima Idan baka sonta a matsayin matarka zaka iya sakinta saboda Abin da mukayi da Mahaifiyarta kenan, sannan Nasrin bata san ka aure ta ba saboda haka kabi a hankali kada ka bata mana shiri"



Yace "To Ranka ya dade zanyi yadda kace "



Ya tashi ya tafi yana tunani sai yanzu ya fara tuna abubuwan da yayi cikin tabin hankali kamar a mafarki



Yana shiga daki ya wuce yana shiga yaga Nasrin lokacin ya jera masa kumallonsa ya zauna a bakin gado ta harare shi da sauri yazo gabanta yace "Kiyi hakuri na tuba"



Dariya abin ya bata ta shafi fuskarsa tace "Nayi hakuri yarima na"



Ya shafa cikinsa yace "Yunwa nake ji"


Tace "Bazanka jirani nayi wanka nazo muci ba"



Ya makale kafada yace "Nidai yanzu"



Tace "To shikenan kasan bana son bacin ranka"



Ta zauna tana bashi yana ci cikin ransa kuwa yana sa mata Albarka 




Saida suka koshi, sannan ta kwashe kayan ta kai ta wanke ta dawo tace "Yarima barinje nayi wanka. Sai tashi yayi zai bita"



Tace "Yarima yaushe rabonka da ka rakani wanka, yaune tuna"


Ya bata rai kamar zaiyi kuka yace "Nasan ta taga zaki gudu waje"



Nasrin tayi dariya tace "Ka jika tayaya zan gudu "



Yace "Aa wlh ban yadda ba"



Tace "To naji zan bude bayi ka zauna a bakin gado ka ringa kallona kada na gudu"



Yace to



Tace "Rigimammen Yarima kawai"



Yace "eh din," cikin ransa kuwa farin ciki yake, dan dama da biyu yayi hakan dan ya karewa suran jikinta kallo



Ta bude kofar bayin ta shiga ta cire kayanta Yarima da ya kalleta Suman tsaye yayi cikin ransa kuwa dakyar ya furta "tsarki ya tabbata ga ubangijin daya halicci wannan halitta"



Nasrin ta fara wankanta shiko yana karewa duk kan jikinta kallo



Saida ta gama taga halamar baya hayyacinsa dan har kusa dashi ta matso ta yarfa masa ruwan hannunta tace "Kallon me kake min"



Sai jawota yayi ya sata a cinyarsa ya kai bakinsa kunnenta yace "Kin min kyaune"


Ta bugi kafadarsa tace "na fika kyaune"

Yace "Kin fini mana"



Tace "Barin shirya muje mu gaida Ummi "



Baiyi magana ba sai murginata da yayi kan gado yana sumbatar wuyanta. Wani irin yanayi taji a jikinta wanda yasata lumshe ido, ya gangaro kan kirjinta yana sumbatar tsakiyar kirjinta da sauri ta mike tace cikin daure fuska "Yarima me haka, Kasan bakyau fa"


Komawa gefe yayi yana meda numfashi sama-sama dan ta bukace yake 



Ita ko shiryawa tayi tasa kaya bayan ta gama tahau gadon tace "Yarimana tashi muje gun Ummi, shiru yayi dan ya kasa saboda munguwar sha'awata "



Ta masa kiss a kumatu tace "Yarima fushi kake dani, dan na hanaka abin da Allah baya so"



Yace "Bai haramta ba fa"



Tace "muje gun Ummi to"



Cikin karfin hali ta ya tashi yana cewa harda Umma ma"



Tare suka tafi suna rike da hannun junan su har suka isa gun Ummi Yarima bai sake mata hannu ba. Itama bata sake ba dan haka suka saba tafiya. Suna zuwa suka gaishe ta bayan sun dan taba hira yace "Muje gun Umma"



Ummi tace "Yau tun yanzu sai kace ana koranku"



Yace "Nasrin tashi mu tafi"



Nasrin kuwa ta biye masa Ummi tace "Ke din ma biye masa zaki yo"



Nasrin tace "Ummi kada yayi fushi"



Tafiya suka yi gun Umma nan ma basu dade ba yace "Su koma gidan su" dan shi so yake yaji jikinsu na manne dana juna



suna komawa kuwa akan kirjinsa ya kwantar da ita yana shafa bayanta, saidai ba karamin daurewa yayi ba gun danne sha'awarsa



Sallar Azhar ya fitar dashi, nan ma yana idarwa ya koma yana shiga falon yaga Gimbiya a falon zai wuce tace "Kai mahaukaci ina Mahaukaciyarka"



Kamar zaiyi magana sai kuma ya shige daki. Yana shiga yaga ta idar da sallah tana Addu'a ya tsaya suka shafa ta tare


Ta tashi zata fita yace "Ina zaki je "



Tace "Zan yi girki kaga yaufa kaki barina na yi aiki"


Yace "Musha madara mana"


Tace "idan mu idanmun sha to Gimbiya fa "


Ya bata rai, ita kuwa fita tayi shi kuma ya bita suka wuce Gimbiya a falo suka shiga kicin din, Gimbiya ta biyo su tace "Ke uban me kike da baki dora mana girki ba"



Yarima ya dauki wuka yace "kina zuwa saina yanka miki wuya, fita ki bar mana kicin"


Tace "Wawa kawai ay zamu hadu da ita ne saina nakasata"


Nasrin kuwa bata ce komai ba tana kokarin dofa tukunya 


Yarima yace "Saikin dafa min madara kafin ki dora wani girki"



Tace "An gama"



Ta dafa masa ta juye a abu me murfi ta tsiyaya masa a kofi yana sha da duminta ita kuma tana aiki



Sai la'asar ta gama ta tafi tayi sallah ta dawo ta zuba na Gimbiya ya wuce daki dana su, Yarima yana dawowa suka fito harabar gidan suna shakatawa



Sai Magrib Yarima ya tafi masjid ita kuma ta wuce ciki, saida akayi isha Yarima ya dawo ya samu Nasrin tana shirin shiga wanka, shima cire kayansa ya yi koda taga haka sai tace "To ka shiga ka fara "


Ya bata rai yace "yau ni zan miki wanka ba da ke kike min ba"


Ta waro ido tace "bazai yuyu ba"



Ya bata rai kamar zaiyi kuka. Da sauri tace "muje kamin"


Tayi gaba ya bita a baya, suna shiga ya cire mata kaya, ya mata wanka tas ya daukota ya kawota tsakiyar gado shima yaje yayi, koda ya dawo yaga har ta shirya tana son kwanciya a kasa yace "Anan fa zamu kwana yau bazaki kwanta kasa ba"



Tace "To muje" a niyarta idan yayi barci ta tashi



Saidai tana kwanciya rumgumeta yayi yana sumbatar ta ta ko ina ta bude baki zatayi magana ya hade bakunan su yana tsotsan harshenta. Tana son ta tureshi ta kasa, cikin salo da dabara ya rabata da kayan jikinta duka, idan zatayi magana saiya hade bakinsu waje daya, gani tayi yana neman cire gajeran wandonsa da ya rage a jikinsa tayi sauri tace "Yarima Haram"


Cikin kasala da kasaita yace "Ba haram bane Halak ne"



Zata sake magana taji shi yana shiganta, wani azaba ne taji wanda bata taba jinshiba tunda Uwarta ta haifeta, ihu tayi tay saidai shi bama jinta yayi ba dan ya fita hayyacin sa, saida ya gamsu ya tashi tare da kallon aika-aikan da yayi suna hada ido ta kauda kai, tausayin ta ne ya kama shi. Sam baiyi zaton zai sameta da budurcinta ba kasancewarta baiwa, da ya sani da ya kyale ta sai kowa yasan ita matar sa ce, da yasan da budurcinta da ya kyale ta anyi biki, in sun kwanta ya nuna a duniya cewa matar sa budurwa ce, Yanzu kenan haka za'a wanke zanin gadon nan a banza ba tare da masarauta sunga jinin budurcin Nasrin dinsa ba. Hawaye ne ya zuba masa yana ganin ya cuceta, indai ba ayi bikin budurcinta ba, ga kuma Alkwarin daya daukarwa Sarki...

Page 3⃣0⃣



Nasrin tashi tayi ta fita da gudu tayi sassan su tana shiga Ummi ta gani tana bawa Gimbiya magani Ummi tace "Lafiya kuwa Nasrin "


Kafin tayi magana Umma ya shigo ta jawo Nasrin, Nasrin ta saka ihu daidai lokacin kuwa Yarima ya shigo da sauri yaje ya kamata yace "Umma me ta miki"



Umma ta kiyi magana sai dukan Nasrin da take yi


Ummi ta daka mata tsawa tace "Dan Allah sake ta, kashe ta zakiyi, komai ta miki baza ki hakuri ba sai ki ringa dukanta"



Umma ta saketa yayin Yarima yake rumgume da ita, Nasrin kuwa tace kalle shi tace duk kai ka jamin wannan abin Yarima ya zama dole na barka 



Yace "kin manta da Alkawarin mu, "



Tace "yau na karya shi, kaje ka fada ma kowa, amma ina fada maka duk wanda ka fada wa, kanka ka tonawa Asiri bani ba"


Ummi tace "Kun sani a duhu"


Umma tace "ke ki fada min menene"


Nasrin ta fashe da kuka cikin kuka tace "Yarima ne"



Umma tace "Yarima ya miki me"


Nasrin tace "Shi yamin ciki"



Gaba dayan su suka mike Gimbiya kuwa cikinta ne yace kuuuuu 


Umma tace "sharri zakiyi wa Yarima dan kinga bashi da lafiya, Nasrin yaushe kika zama haka"


Ummi ta ce " ba sharri bane, saboda ki dubi Sumayya mana har ciki ya tsufa"


Yarima yayi saurin juyowa yace "Ciki ya tsufa, kuma aka ce muku nawa ne?"



Ummi tace "To na waye ba kaine mijinta ba"


Yace "nasan cikin dake jikin Nasrin nawa ne amma na jikinta bansan dashi ba"


Gimbiya ta fasa kuka tace "wlh naka ne"



Yarima ya mike da Nasrin a hannunsa Yace "Na gaji da boye-boye dole in kira sarki ya warware komai"



Ummi tace "Yarima ko ka samu lafiya ne bamu sani ba"


Bai mata magana ba ya wuce, Ummi ta kalli Umma tace "Kin gane min wannan daurarren abu"



Gimbiya kuwa sai kuka take yi yayi da Nasrin ma kukan take, Ummi tace "kuyi shiru mana"



Gimbiya tace "Wlh idan da gaske Yarima ne kika bari ya miki ciki saina kashe ki"


Nasrin ta dago ta kalli Gimbiya tace "Idan kika barni da rai wlh baki cika 'yar Sarki ba"



Ta tashi zata isa gun Nasrin da niyar dukanta daidai lokacin Yarima ya shigo da sarki a bayansa yace "Idan kika sake kika dake da ciki a jikinta saina ballaki"



Sarki ya zauna Yarima ma ya zauna yace "Ranka ya dade ka fada musu komai sannan ka fada musu duniya cewa Nasrin matata ce"



Gimbiya da Nasrin suka ce tare "Matar ka"



Sarki yace "ku zauna muyi magana"



Gimbiya tace "Ba zaman da zanyi, an cuce ni a ce na zauna"


Yarima ya mike sarki ya rike shi yace "Ranka ya dade ka bari in babbala ta, cuta ya wuce naki da kikayi cikin shege"


Ta koma ta zauna tana kada kafa cikin ranta kuwa tana tunanin irin hukuncin da zata yiwa Nasrin data aura mata miji



Sarki yace "Alhamdulillah, ke Nasrin munyi miki wani abu bada sanin ki ba, amma da sanin mahaifiyarki, lokacin da Yarima ya samu lalura, kinyi Alkawarin zaki kula dashi har iya karshen rayuwarki, kuma gaskiya mutum kamar Yarima matarsa ita keda Alhakin kula da Al'amuran sa, ita kuma tace bazata iya ba, sai mukayi shawara da Malam akan me zai hana mu muku aure, koda mukayi shawara da mahaifiyarki tace ta amince, Mahaifiyar Yarima ta amin ce, a kauyen su Malam aka daura auren ki, nine waliyyinki yayin da Malam shine waliyyinsa, sadakinki mun bawa mahaifiyarki, wannan magana ba wanda ya sani sai mu hudu ni, sai mahaifiyarki sai mahaifiyar Yarima, Amma yanzu ku tashi muje fada anan zan fada kowa yasan cewa Nasrin matar Yarima ne,"



Sarki yana gama fada yatashi ya fita, Ummi ta kalli Nasrin tace "Nasrin Yarima ya samu lafiya ne"


Nasrin tace "Nima ban sani ba, nidai koya warke ko bai warke ba na barshi kenan, bazan kara zama dashi ba"



Yarima ya kalleta yace "Bakya son na kenan 


Tace cikin tsawa "eh bana sonka"



Umma tace "ke me haka bana son rashin kunya Yarima sa'anki ne"



Nasrin ta fashe da wani sabon kuka tace "To Umma ya za'ayi amin aure ban sani ba, kuma fa har ciki yamin saboda bamu da galihu sai ayi ta cutar mu"



Ummi da Yarima suka ji tausayin ta Yarima ya matso ya kama hannunta ta fizge tace "Na haramta a gare ka, kada ka sake taba ni"



Ummi ta matso ta kama ta tace "Yi shiru Nasrin kiyi hakuri munyi hakan ne dan mu kare hakkin Addininmu"


Nasrin ta kalleta tace "Son kai dai, danje gun sarkin ay tunda su suka daura wannan aure sai sun kunce shi kuma cikin nan dole na zubar da Shi "



Tana gama fada ta fita


Duk fetsaran da take bai damu Yarima ba amma da yaji tayi maganan zubar da ciki da sauri ya mike ya je ya kama jafar Ummanta yace "Umma kiyi wani abu akai kada ki bari ta zubar da cikin nan wlh ina son sa"



Ummi kallon take tace "Yarima kana gwada min abubuwa kamar me hankali shin ka warke ne"



Yace "Ummi na warke tun wata uku da suka wuce"


Tace "Shine baka nuna mana ka warke ba, ka samu yarinyar mutane ka dirka mata ciki ko"


Ya sunkuyar da kai yace "Ayi hakuri, nidai Umma a taimaka kada wani abu ya samu cikin nan"



Umma tace "kada ka damu da izinin Allah ba abin da zai same shi"


Gimbiya tace "Ay kuwa yau zata bar duniyar, kun manta baiwa tace"



Yarima yace "Umma muje fada fa"



Suka tashi suka tafi, suna zuwa suka samu fada ta cika Nasrin tana tsakiya tana ta yin kuka, gefen Sarki kuwa Sarauniya ce da Marwan


Gimbiya kuwa tana fita sasan su Marwan tayi ta dauko wata sharbebiya takwabi ta soke cikin wandon ta ta ciki ta nufi fada 


Bayan sun zauna sarki ya fadi komai game da Nasrin da Yarima 


Yan fada kowa sai sam barka suke, suna murna da samun saukin yarima, kuma suka yaba da Matar Yarima dama ba son waccan suke ba,



Sarauniya tace "Shine mu aka ki fada mana Yarima ya samu lafiya "



Sarki yace "itama uwarsa yanzu ta sani"


Sumayya ta matso gaban sarki tace "Ranka ya dade, Nasrin Baiwa tace, a ka'idar sarauta duk abin da nayi wa baiwata daidai ne, saboda haka zan kashe baiwa ta yanzu"


Sarki yace "Hakane amma ina son ki siyar min da ita"


Tace "Bazan sayar da ita ba da hannuna nake son na kashe ta"


Sarki yace "To kiyi hakuri ta haifi dan cikin ta"


Tace "ban yadda ba"


Sarki yace "Yarima zo ka bata hakuri ta kyale maka mata"



Nasrin ta mike tace "Wannan wani irin masarauta ce da ba adalci"



Yarima zai matso Gimbiya da Nasrin suka daga masa hannu ta tare suka ce "tsaya daga nan"


Ya tsaya yayin da Nasrin ta tsuguna gaban Gimbiya tace "gani ki kashe ni"



Gimbiya ta ciro takwabin yayin da mutanen fada sai kuka suke Yarima da Umminsa kuka suke, Sarauniya kuwa da Marwan sa murna suke, Umma ce ta mike tace "Sumayya idan kika kukura ko sa takwabin kika yi a wuyan Nasrin ina me tabbatar miki, sai an kashe dukkan 'yan masarautar ku, sannan a dawo kan wannan masarautar da akayi a gaban sarkin, kinsan wacece Nasrin kuwa? To yau zan fallasa sirrin dake boye, badan naso ba"



Gaba daya akayi zuru aka zuba mata ido. Har Yarima da Umminsa da suke kuka, suka tsaida kukan, ita kanta Nasrin dagowa tayi tana kallonta, sarki ne Yayi karfin halin cewa "wacece Nasrin "

Page 3⃣1⃣


Umma ta taka ta isa gun Gimbiya tace "Idan maki sani ba in fada miki Nasrin ya daya tilo ga Sarki Abdulraham wanda ke sarautar Samina da kewaye 


Da sauri Gimbiya ta yadda takwabin ta, daga Sarki har 'yan fada sakan baki sukayi suna kallonta Nasrin tace "Umma ki kyale ta ta kashe ni, kada ki fadi karya aguna"


Umma tace "Ba karya zan fada akanki ba, abin da na sani yau zan fallasa sirrin da aka hanani fada, saidai ki gafarce ni Gimbiya BILKISU"



Yarima yace "Umma ki bani labarin Nasrin "



Tace "jin labari na baida amfani a gare ka dan ayau dole in bar ka, sarki kuma daya daura auren saiya kunce shi"



Umma tace "Nasrin bansanki da rashin kunya ba ki tsaya kiji wacece ke"



Umma ta fara


WACECE NASRIN 

A Sarki Abdurraman na  masarautar Samina Sarki ne me Adalci Adalcinsa yakai, zai iya cutan kansa dan talakansa yaji dadi, yakan bi gida-gida ya zaga yaga ko akwai gidan ba basa cikin wadata ya taimaka musu 


Matan Sarki biyu amma bashi da kwarkwara, dan shi baida ra'ayin su 


Yakan zauna cikin bayi yayi hira dasu dan ya ji matsalilinsu 


 Sarki Abdurraman Tun lokacin da ya taso a gidan bai taso ya ganshi da dangi me yawa ba, sun taso ne shida kaninsa Sahabi kansu a hade suka taso koda mahaifinsa ya rasu sai aka nada Abdurraman a matsayin sarki, yaso ya nada kaninsa matsayin waziri, saidai masarauta tace "Aa yabar Asalin wazirin mahaifinsa "


Shiko waziri ba Allah a ransa a tunaninsa Shi za'a baiwa sarauta tunda yaransa samari ne sai gashi an baiwa babban dan sa samarin, daga nan sai ya fara niman yadda zaiyi ya kashe shi ya kashe dan uwan tunsa basu magada ay dole a bashi sarauta.


Lokacin ne Sahabi yace yakamata yayi aure saboda bazai yuyu ya zauna ba mace ba, 



Sarki yace "To 'yanzu a ina zan samu mata dan uwana"


Yace "Ga Gimbiya Bilkisu  'yar Sarkin jera"



Sarki yayi murmushi dan dama an sosa masa inda yake masa kaykayi, dama lokacin da mahaifinsu keda rai sukan ze, kuma duk gidan da ita ya saba ,


Sahabi ma murmushi yace "dama ay nasan kana son ta"



Koda suka je sukayi magana aka karbe su hannu bibbiyu tunda dama yarinyar tana sonsa


Ba'a dau lokaci mai tsawo ba akayi auren 


Gimbiya Bilkisu yarinya 'yar shekara 14 wanda bata cika magana ba, maganar ma cikin kasaita take yinta da yanga,  kuma akwai  Izza, dan bata yadda mutum ya raina ta ba, a mujinta kawai take wa Dariya, Ko Sahabi ma bata yadda yana yawan hira da ita saboda a tunaninta Raini zai shiga tsakanin su, Saidai kuma akwai Adalci wanda bai fahimceta ba cewa suke tana da Girman kai, wanda kuma ya fahimceta kuma sai yace dan ita jinin sarauta ne,



Tsawon shekaru biyar aka dauka da auren amma shiru ba labarin haihuwa, Sarki ya damu tun baya nuna mata har ya fara nuna mata, Tana ganewa tace "Sarki kayi aure mana mu samu magaji a gidan nan"


Yace "Aa Gimbiya ba maganan aure kuma ke daya kin isa"


Tace "Ina magane akan Magaji "


Yace "Tunda ban samu a wajenki ba, zamu samu gun Sahabi,"



Tace "Sahabi bashi da niyyar aure"



Yace "idan na masa magana zaiyi"


Yana masa maganar aure da maganan da suka yi da Gimbiya Bilkisu 


Sahabi yace "maganan Gimbiya zaka bi, kayi auren nima zanyi"


Shiru yayi can yace "A ina zamu samu matar "


Yace "'yar Sarkin Suraimi"


Koda Sarki yaje ya fada wa Gimbiya Bilkisu murmushi tayi tace "Ni dama abinda nake so kenan wlh"



Ba 'a dau lokaci aka auro sa'adatu Gimbiya Bilkisu ta riki sa'a tamkar kanwarta itama sa'a bataa da matsala saidai itama shekarunta uku ba zancen samun haihuwa, Abin yana damu Sarki da Gimbiya saidai ita bata nunawa dan bata son wadanda suka rabe su musamman Sahabi 



Lokacin ne Sahabi ya yi aure ya auri wata baiwa Amra, duk da ta zama matarsa bata yadda ta raina 'yan uwanta bayi ba. 


Cikin lokacin ne kuma Waziri yake bakin ciki a ganin sa tunda sun tara iyalai ko yaushe zasu iya haihuwa dan haka sai ya gama da Iyalensa tukum


Cikin haka ne Amra da Sa'adatu  Suka samu ciki a tare, murna gurin Gimbiya da Sarki ba'a magana 


Lokacin ne kuma waziri ya samu masu aiki Sarkin Fada da jakadiya


Jakadiya tana zagawa tana gaishe su duk dan ta samu taji sirrin su, amma amma bata samu. Saidai suna bata girmanta


Sa'adatu ta fara nakuda Jakadiya ce ta amshi haihuwa ta haife 'ya mace lafiya, amma saboda rashin imani na jakadiya faki idon sa'adatu ta murde ma jaririyar kai ta mutu , koda aka sanar Gimbiya Bilkisu ta damu, hawaye ne kawai ke fita a idon ta dan kukan ma ta kasa, Amra kuwa na ji saboda gigicewa ta fara nakudan dole, itama jakadiya ce ta amshi haihuwan itama mace ta haifa saidai tana haihuwa jakadiya ta murde kai ta mutu 


Jin wannan al'amari yasa Gimbiya Bilkisu ta kasa yadda da duk wadanda suke kusa da sarki da dan uwansa, ta fara bincike cikin bincikenta ne ta fara zargan Waziri, koda ta samu Sarki da maganan bata rai yayi yace "shi wazirinsa me adalci ne, kuma idan. Ya kara jin haka zai bata maya rai,



Gimbiya Bilkisu mace ce wacce bata son bacin rai dan haka bata sake magana tafi son ya gane da kansa


Amma taci gaba da bincikawa cikin bincikenta ta gano sun hada kai da sarkin fada da jakadiya, 



Cikin haka Gimbiya da Amra suka samu ciki inda suke kula da cikinsu, cikin haka kuma akayi musu shigar bazata da yaki inda aka ci garin ciki harda kashe Sahabi da akayi.

Page 3⃣2⃣
 


Sarki yaji munguwar dan uwansa haka Gimbiya Bilkisu da sa'adatu ma Amra kuwa saida ta kwanta ciwo, 



Gimbiya ta lura da jakadiyya tana yawan shiga gun Amra dan, kuma dama tana zargin su, dan haka tace "dole Amra tabar garin har saita haihu dan bata san me suke kulla mata ba, zasu iya cutar da ita duk da in sunga ada ita baiwa ce



Haka ta samu sarki ta fada mata kudirinta na tura Amra wani gari ta haihu.


Yace "Ba damuwa duk yadda kikayi daidaine "


Itako bata bi takan cikin dake jikinta ba, ko wani abu ya same shi ba matsala bane idai ta tseratar da Amra da marayan cikinta



Haka ta tura Amra garinsu akan idan ta haihu zasu zo su ganta 


Lokacin Gimbiya Bilkisu isa haihuta da daddare ta tashi nakuda,  duk zafin nakudar da take ji, da taga Jakadiya sai taji ba zafin nakudar saboda tasan da nufi tazo, tunda tajisu suna maganan duk ranan da zata haihu tazo ta amshi haihuwan 



Da kyar ta tashi ta fita ta nufi dakin Sarki ta kwanta, Sarki ya kalleta yace "Bana tura jakadiya ta amshi haihuwan ki ba ki koma dakin ki"


Tana cije lebe tace "Ranka ya dade, ban yadda da jakadiya ba, "



Sarki ya bata rai yace "Jakadiya fa a hannunta aka haife mu, yanzu zaki ce baki yadda da ita ba, bana son wannan magana ki tashi kije"



Ba musu ta tashi ta fita saidai maimakon tayi daki sai tayi waje ta fita tayi kan hanya...



Ummi tayi shiru ta kalli Nasrin tace "kinsan ina taje"


Nasrin tace aa


Tace "gidan mu taje, alokacin ni kuma na haihu saidai dan baizo da raiba muna jira gari ya waye Malam ya binne shi"



Sai muka ganta ta dukushe a gurin saboda azababben ciwon nakuda naje na kama ta koda hasken farin wata ya haska ta na gane Gimbiya Bilkisu ce 


Da kyar tace "Ki taimaka min na haihu a gunki"


Na jata muka shiga daki, ba'a dau tsawon lokaci ba Cikin taimakon Allah ta haifi 'yarta mace bayan na shirya ta nace "Gimbiya muje in kaiku gida"


Gimbiya Bilkisu tace "ki taimaka min "


Na zube a kasa nace "Me kike son na miki"


Cikin izza da kasaita tace "Ina son ki bani gawan danki in tafi dashi sai ki rike wannan yarinyar "



A tare muka kalli juna ni da malam tace "Nasan zakuyi mamakin hakan dana yanke, amma zan baku labarin komai"



Gimbiya Bilkisu "ta fada mana komai "



Malam yace "Amma bakya tunanin ko mu mungwaye ne, ko mu gudu miki da yarinya"



Tace "Tun lokacin da Sarki ya auro ni ya kawoni nan nake jin labarin ku, ku mutanen kirki ne, kuma naga zahiri a idona baza ku cuce ni ba"



Malam yace "Amma Sarki zai sani ko"



Tace "Bana son sarki ya sani, ina son ku bani gawar danku idan na je, zance shine dan dana haifa kuma ya mutu, ku kuma ku rike wannan yarinyar tamkar wacce kuka haifa, zan na zuwa duba ta"



Malam yace "Yakamata a mata huduba"


Tace "Ka mata Huduba da NASRIN "


Malam shi ya miki huduba a gaban Gimbiya Bilkisu, sannan ta dauki gawar yaro ta tafi dashi tana zuwa ta mika wa Sarki tace "Yaron ya mutu"



Da yake sarki yana dakinsa baisan ma Gimbiya ta fita ba yayi zaton ne a dakinta ta haihu, itako jakadiya koda taji labarin Sai taje ta fada wa waziri cewa ita ta kashe shi, haka mukayi rayuwa a gidan nono na kika sha, Gimbiya kuwa kusan kullum sai tazo har kuka saba da ita da kika girma kuwa har daukanki tana yi ta tafi dake saidai abin da yake damun ta Amra shiru ba a jin labarin ta



 koda kika girma nacewa Gimbiya ta fada wa Sarki gaskiya tace "Wai sai ya gane Waziri munafukaine"


A haka ne aka zo yaki aka ci Samina da yaki aka tattaramu a matsayin bayi, bansan halin da Gimbiya Bilkisu ta shiga ba bayan babu mu.


CI GABA



Kowa yayi shiru yayin da Nasrin ce keta kuka, 



Yarima ne ya matso ya kama ta yace "Kiyi shiru haka kinsan bana son kukanki"


Tayi saurin mikewa ta ja da baya tace "Ka daina taba ni, "



Ta kalli Gimbiya Sumayya tace "Har yanzu ina matsayin baiwarki ki kashe ni"



Sarki yace "Idan Mahaifinki Sarki Abdulbasid bai taba fada miki wanene Sarki Abdurraman ba, ni zan fada miki, Sarki Abdurraman shine wanda gaba ki daya garuruwa Ashirin da shida da Sarakunan su suke karkashin su, kuma ciki harda garinku gauda da nan mula, yana da daman da zai.kira sarakunan bayin sa, yana da ikon shafe garinku da garin nan ya zauna Lafiya, Yanzu ga 'yarsa a gabanki, shawara ta rage taki, ko ki kasheta ko ki barta, amma bari kiji muddin sarki yaji labarin yarsa tana nan, zaizo kuma idan ya ganku a haka, zai iya shafe duk masarautarki, dan haka ki mata biyayya, nima biyayya zan mata saboda 'yar sarki nane"



Gimbiya ta yarda wukar ta wuce fuuuu



Nasrin ta mike tace "Umma muje ki kaini gun dangi na, ta juya ta kalli Sarki tace yadda ka daura wannan aure to ka kunce shi, dan bana son sa,"



Yarima ya rarrafo yace "Nasrin Nasan yanzu kin fini matsayi bani da ikon in saki dole, to amatsayina na mijinki ki taimaka min wlh ina sonki "



Ta kalleshi tace "Amar kake ko wa, Masarautar ku bata da Adalci tunda har zata iya daura aure ba tare da sanin mutum ba"


Yace "Sunyi hakan ne dan su kare hakkin addinin mu"



Tayi kayataccen murmushi tace "To Amin Adalci kenan da aka daura aurena ba tare da na sani ba, kuma nayi jinyarka, ka warke, amma kaki nuna min ka warke, cikin haka kamin fyade yanzu ciki ne a jikina, yanzu kai a ganinka amin adalci, Amar bazan iyaci gaba dazama da kaiba"



Ta juya ta kalli Umma tace "Umma muje ki kaini gun dangina "



Sarki yace "Ni da kaina zan kaiku Nasrin "



Tace "Bama bukata kayi zamanka, "



Yace sa sarkin dawakai "A shirya wa su Nasrin Amalanken dawakai na alfarma a kaisu garin Samina 



Kafin kace me, an shirya komai, Ummi tayi tayi da Nasrin tazo ta canja kaya tace ba inda zata koma sai masarautar su, haka Yarima yana kuka Nasrin ta tafi ta barshi, 



Hmm lalle Nasrin akwai taurin kai



Sarki ya dafa shi yace "Ka kwantar da hankalinka magaji "


Sarauniya da Marwan kuwa idan kaga muryan su kamar an musu bushara da gidan Aljanna



Yarima yana zaune gefen Sarki sai ya tuna da Sumayya, da sauri ya tashi yayi sassan sa a zuciye...

Page 3⃣3⃣


Da yana shiga ya ganta a falon tana ta shekar kuka ya isa ya cafko wuyanta yace "saikin fada min waya miki cikin jikim ki"



Tace "Kaine abin da yasa baka sani ba saboda lokacin baka da lafiya"


Ya bata mari yace "Karya kike munafuka duk abin da ya faru ina kallon sa ne kamar a mafarki, idan ma na kusance ki zan sani "


Tayi shiru tana neman kwatar kanta dan taji wuyan rikon da ya mata,



Ya kwantar da ita ya sake danna wuyanta yace "Zaki fada ko saina kashe dake da abin dake cikin ki"



Tace "Marwan ne"



Da sauri ya saketa ta tashi ta zauna tana nadaman bawa Marwan kanta, gashi ana azabtar da ita baya gurin, ya tashi da sauri ya koma fada yana zuwa ya cafki wuyan Marwan fadawa suka mike zasu rike shi Sarki ya tsaida su yace "Lalle abu me girma ka yiwa magaji"



Yace "Haba Marwan wani irin kiyayya ne kake nuna min a matsayina na dan uwanka, bani da lafiya baka ji tausayina ba, a hakan ka samu kebewa da matata ashe kai ka mata cikin jikinta "



"Lahhhh" 
Gaba daya gurin ya dauka sai aka yi shiru ana jiran me Marwan zaice



Sarki kan shiru yayi dan ya rasa abin da zaice



Gimbiya kuwa yana mita tayi gun Sarauniya tana zuwa tace "Ina danki yake "


Sarauniya tace "Wannan wani irin rashin kunya ne"



Tace "Cikin jikina shi yamin dole in ganshi"



Sarauniya tace "ohhh shine kika zo kimin rashin kunya, to ay ke kika bashi kanki, mu kin gama mana komai ta hannunki mun samu abin da muke so, wato ke kina son mallakar Amar to bari kiji maganin da muke baki ay ba naki bane, wanda zamu sa ya haukace ne, yanzu ya samu lafiya shawara ta rage naki"



Gimbiya ta fasa kuka tace "Yanzun dama son da kike fada kike min ba so bane"



Sarauniya ta hade rai tace "Ya za'ayi ma in soki, kina matar makiyyina, in soki a yaya, wlh karya kike, nidai nayi amfani da ke ne dan na cika burina, kuma na kusa cika wa dan yau zan koma gun boka na amso sammun da zan watsawa Yarima ya sake haukacewa, ko kuma in kashe shi ma na huta, dan bayan sarauta bazai yuyu ina gani ya zauna da 'yar Sarkin Samina ba



Gimbiya tace "Ni yanzu ba damuwata bace dan kin kashe shi, damuwa ta Yaudaran da kuka min "



Sarauniya ta shege da dariyan mugunta tace "Lalle ma, sai ki nemi wani sarkin"



Marwan kuwa Ummi ce ta kwace shi daga hannun Yarima ta jashi suka tafi sassan sa daidai lokacin Gimbiya ta shigo ay ko ya fizge jikinsa yayi kanta ya kamata ya buga mata bango yace "Ki fada min me yasa kika min haka"



Tace "Wlh yaudara ta akayi "



Yace "yaudara kuma"



Bata boye masa ba ta bashi labarin komai


Ya kalleta ta yana nuna Ummi  yace "Wato kin so ki wulakanta min Mahaifiya,
Ki gode Allah ma da abin ya kare a kaina, da wani abu ya samu Ummi na da hukuncin da zan miki... "


Ya yarfe yatsa ya wuce har yakai bakin kofa ya dawo yace "Yanzun nan ki tattara ki bar min gidana Na sake ki, kije gun uban danki ki haifa masa"



Kuka ta rushe dashi, ta isa gun Ummi ta kama hannunta tace "Ummi dan Allah ki bashi hakuri"


Ummi yace "Yanzu zan miki amfani kenan "

Tana fada ta fita batare da ta jira abim da zata ce ba


Shi kuwa yana fita gun Sarki yayi ya tsuguna Yace "Ranka ya dade ka gafarce ni na saki Mata ta Sumayya, kuma yanzu na gano wadanda suka sani a halin da na shiga "



Sarki yace "Su waye "


Ya fada masa abim da Sumayya ta fada masa 



Sarki ya kalli Marwan yace "Amma kai da uwarka akwai maras sa Imani, magajin nawa kuka yiwa haka, kuma bayan batan basira harda yin zina da matarsa "



Yasunkuyar dakai, 


Sarki ya kalli kartin mutane su biyu yace "Kuje ku yi masa hukuncin daya dace"


Suka kama Marwan suka fita dashi, 


Sarki ya kalli Amar yace "Bazan kyale duk wanda yayi tozarta ka bamagaji, ita kuma Sarauniya ka kyaleta duniya zata koya mata hankali"


Yarima yace "Nagode me martaba"


Ya tashi ya tafi sassan Ummi yana zuwa ta rumgumeshi shi kuma ya kwantar da kansa a kafadarta, ta dago shi tace "Yarima nah, kayi hakuri komai na duniya me karewa ne"


Yace "Ummi Nasrin fa"


Tace "kayi hakuri ina tabbatar maka Nasrin zata dawo gare ka saboda bazata iya rayuwa da wani Namiji ba sai kai, yanzu ma abin da yasa kaga haka, taji zafin rashin adalcin da aka mata ne"



Yayi shiru Ummi ta zaunar da shi ta bashi ruwa me sanyi yasha, ta kwantar dashi a cinyar ta tana shafa gashin sa tun yaji wasai daga bisani barci ya kwashe shi. 



Su Nasrin kuwa sai magrib suka isa a kofar masarautar suka tsaya, Umma tace da masu gadin, "munzo gun Gimbiya Bilkisu ne"


Yace "Ba Gimbiya Bilkisu duk masarautar nan"



Tace "Matar sarki fa uwar gidan "


Yace "Oh kice Sarauniya, muje in kaiku"


Da amalanken dokinsu suka shiga, saida sukayi tafiya me nisa yace ku tsaya barin shiga na sanar da ita. Ya sunanki



Umma tace "Kace Hauwa ce matar Malam Iliya, da Nasrin"



Ya shiga baifi minti biyu ba saiga ta ta fito da gudu ita da wani saurayi sai karamar Yarinya a bayansu, tana zuwa ta rumgume Nasrin, tasa kuka yayin da Nasrin ma tasa kuka, shi kuma saurayin yana gefe shima kukan yake

Page 3⃣4⃣


Gimbiya Bilkisu tace da dan saurayin "Safwan je ka kira Sa'adatu kace tazo yanzun nan"



Da saurin sa ya tafi yayin da yarinyarma ta bishi da gudu, 



Suka shiga ciki Gimbiya tasa aka kawo musu abinci da kayan marmari Nasrin tace "Wanka take son yi, Gimbiya Bilkisu tasa aka hada mata ruwan wanka tana shiga, Sa'adatu ta shigo da yara biyu, a bayanta, tana zuwa tace "Sarauniya me zan gani 'yar mu ce ta dawo"


Gimbiya Bilkisu tace "Eh, elama bata sani ba ki shiru kada abin yazo mata wani iri"



Umma tace "Wa ay sanin da tayi ne yasa kika ganmu anan, kiyi hakuri na tona sirrin da kika ce baki yadda in tona ba, hakan ya faru ne dalilin abubuwa da suka faru "



Gimbiya tace "Shikenan ki bari mu tutsu sai muyi magana"



Nasrin tana fitowa Umma ta shiga, Sa'adatu ce ta bata kayan sarauta tasa, sannan ta gabatar da sallolin da ake binta,


Lokacin ma Umma ya idar da nata suka zo suka fara cin Abinci 


Sultan ne ya shigo ya  tsuguna ya gaishe su, chan yace "Umman Sultan Ina Anisa"



Tace "yanzu tabar nan," da sauri ya shiga daki"



Bayan sun gama cin abinci ne, Umma tace "To Nasrin ga mahaifiyarki nan, ita zata nuna miki mahaifinki, saidai ban sani ba ko yanzu ya sani"




Gimbiya Bilkisu tace "Ranan da akayi Yaki wanda aka kama ku a matsayin bayi ashe waziri ne ya turo su dan su kashe sarki, kuma ranan asiri ya tonu a ranan da daddare na sanar dashi komai, 



Sarki yayi bakin ciki, Sanin waziri Jakadiya da Sarkin fada 

Yace "Gimbiya Bilkisu a gaskiya kinyi kokari da har kika iya rabuwa da diyarki, yanzu gashi an kama su a matsayin bayi, kuma bamu san inda aka kaisu ba, abin da muke so dake shine muyi hakuri mu barwa Allah komai, in yaso muje, garin su Amra muyi cikiyar ta mu daukota da abin da ta haifa, 



Sannan idan mun dawo Za'a naki a matsayin Sarauniya ta,


Murna nayi tay ba kama hannun yaro Koda na fada wa Sa'adatu ma ta taya ni murna



Koda mukaje bamu sha wahala ba muka dauko su, muka samu ta haifi Namiji sun sa masa sunan Mahaifinsa, Sahabi suna kiransa da  Sultan,



Tunda Sarki yaga Sultan yasa a ransa shi zai gaji Wannan masarauta


 Amra take ce min ranan da na haihu ranan ita ma ta haihu, saboda taji labarin Matar sarkin Samina ta haihu dan ya koma, tana jin wannan labarin tasan nice, kuma itama tayi bakin ciki,



Muna dawowa kuwa Sarki yayi bikin nada ni a matsayin Sarauniya



Watan mu uku da ita tayi ciwo itama ta rasu, 


Duk da Sultan baida wayo amma kwana biyu ya rame, kullum sai yayi kuka wai akaishi gun ummansa, ta dalilin janshi da nake a jiki ne da kuma nuna masa da nake yi nice Ummansa yasa har ya saba dani in zai kirani ma yace "Umman Sultan "


Cikin haka ne na samu ciki Murna gun Sarki, Sultan da Sa'adatu ba'a magana Sultan kuwa kullun yana tare dani wai Suna zantawa da 'yar uwarsa, 


Sarki yakance Magajina wa yace maka mace zata haifa


Yace "Ni naji a jikina ay"



Koda lokacin haihuwa tayi na haifi 'ya mace, Ranan gun Sultan ake amsanta idan anzo barka. Sati na zagayowa akai suna aka sa mata ANISA Haka muka mukayi rayuwa kullum idan Anisa gun Sultan take yini, bayan shekara biyu ne Sa'adatu ta samu ciki, munyi murna sosai koda tazo haihuwa cikin sauki ta haifi 'yan biyu mata,



Tuni Sultan da Anisa suka min kaura suka tare a sassan sa'adatu, ranan suna aka bar musu sunan su wato HASSANA DA HUSSAINA


Gimbiya ta kalli 'yan biyu da suke jingine a jikim sa'adatu tace "kunga rigimammun 'yan biyun mu nan"


Sultan kuma yanzu ya shige gun Anisa a daki


Sa'adatu tace "Nikan Sarauniya kin cika su da surutu Sarki yasan da Zuwansu kuwa"



Sarauniya Bilkisu tace "Lokacin da suka iso Magrib ne Sarki ya riga ya shiga Masallaci kuma kinsan sai yayi sallan isha yake dawowa, amma yanzu nasan ya dawo, tunda ga Sultan ya shigo"


Sarauniya ta kara da cewa "Baba Hauwa yi hakuri ban tambaye ki Malam ba"


Umma tace "Ranki ya dade Malam ay tun a hanya ya rasu"



Sarauniya Bilkisu tace "Innalillahi wa inna ilaihir rajun Allah ya jikansa Allah ya masa rahma"

Tace "Muje gun sarki duka nasan yanzu haka ya dawo sassansa, tadan daga murya ta kira Sultan da Anisa suka fito yana rike da hannunta, 


Sarauniya ta kama hannun Nasrin suka tafi suna isa Sarki ya fara kallon Nasrin, 



Sa'adatu ne ta fara magana tace "Ranka ya dade kasan wannan kuwa"


Yayi murmushi yace "Taya za'ayi na santa, saidai nasan ta hada wani abu da Sarauniya tunda naga suna kama"



Sarauniya tayi murmushi tace "Wannan 'yarka ce Nasrin "


Da sauri sarki ya duro daga kan kujeransa ya rumgume ta sai hawaye Sarki ma hawaye take tana share wa Sarki nasa hawayen tana cewa "Abbana kayi shiru bana son farkon ganina dakai fuskarka ta kasance me hawaye, nafi son kayi farin ciki,"


 murmushi yayi da hawayen a fuskarsa ya sa hannunsa shima yana share mata hawayen yace "Nima nafi son farin cikin ki 'yata"



Itama murmushi tayi ya kara rumgumeta yace "Baba Hauwa ki fada min halin da 'yata ta shiga"


Umma ta basu Labarin komai, har labarin rayuwarta a garun su Sumayya da auren ta da Yarima da Irin jinyar da ta masa, da yadda ya bayyyana mata irin son da yake mata, da tauri kan da Ita Nasrin din ta musu,



Sarki ya shafa kanta yace "Alhamdulillah ban san irin Farin cikin da nake ciki ba yau," ya kalli Sultan yace "Sultan ga 'yar uwarka Nasrin"


Yace "Na ganta "


Yace "Kuje ku kwanta ku huta, gobe zan gabatar dake ga mutanen Fada "


Suka muke suka tafi, dama Sa'adatu ce me kwana gun Sarki dan haka ta Sarauniya tace "Tare zasu kwana da 'yarta"


Umma kuwa aka bata dakin da zata kwana


Koda Nasrin  tasa kayan bacci ta kwanta, Sarauniya Bilkisu tazo ta daga kanta ta sa akan cinyar tana shafa gashinta tace "'yata banji dadin abin da kika yi wa Yarima ba, A labarin da Baba Hauwa ta bani na gano tun kafin Yarima ya samu tabin hankali ya fara son ki, haka kema kin fara sonshi ne tun kafin ya samu tabin hankali saidai duk baku gane ba, saboda kuruciya, saboda haka zaki zauna nan, idan ki haihu zaki koma gidan mijinki"



Nasrin ta dago kai ta kalli Sarauniya Bilkisu a karo na farko tace "Nidai banzan koma ba, Masarautar basu da Adalci"



Sarauniya Bilkisu  tace "Rashin Adalcin me suka miki Nasrin,"


Tace "Sun min aure ba tare da na sani ba, kuma Yarima ya warke ban saniba har yamin fyade, nayi ciki," sai kuma ta fashe kuka



Gimbiya Bilkisu tayi murmushi tana share mata hawayen hakan da tayi sai Nasrin taji kamar tana lallashinta ne tace "Aure da Suka miki baki sani ba, sunyi hakane saboda su kare hakkin Addininku, sannan Yarima Yaki fada miki ya warke ne saboda yana son yasan waya sashi a cikin wannan matsala, kuma ki daina cewa Yarima ya miki fyade, ya raya Sunna da ke ne, kuma da bashi da masaniyar kuna da aure bazai miki komai ba, saboda ke kanki kinsan waye Yarima "



Nasrin tayi shiru ta fara tunanin yadda Yarima yake rokonta akan ta tsaya amma taki, itako Sarauniya ta yi zaton barci tayi yasa ta kwantar da ita itama ta kwanta barci yayi awon gaba da ita.



Nasrin kuwa ta barci ya gagareta sai tunanin Yarima take ko a wani hali yake ciki yanzu oho, tana rufe ido Yarima take gani yana rokonta 

A ranta tace "hmm jiya yar haka ina kwance a kirjin Yarimana"


Ta juya ta kalli Sarauniya yadda take baccinta sai taji dama Yarima ne a gurin mana, tayi murmushi ta juya ta rufe ido saidai har yanzu baccin yaki zuwa


Haka ta kasance da Yarima shima ya kasa bacci idan ya rufe ido saidai yaga Nasrin tana kuka gaba ki daya katifan ta zama masa kamar bayan kada. Yayi niyyar gobe zaiyi sammako ya tafi garin Samina dan ganin Nasrin dinsa....

Wannan page din naki ne Maman haneef da fatan Allah ya baki lafiya


*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*




Page 3⃣6⃣


Yarima yana kallonsa har yayi nisa Ya dubi dajin yace "Innalillahi wa inn ilaihir raju'un mai nayi wa mutanen nan ne suke niman rayuwa ta"


Durk'usawa yayi a gurin ya daga hannu yace "Ya Allah ga bawanka Amar ka taimake shi"



Nasrin kuwa sarki yasa aka gyara mata sasa guda yace "Ta zauna anan, ya zuba mata bayi, masu yawa,"



Da yamma tana kishingide ga bayi sun kewayeta ba komai take tunani ba sai Yarima, yanzu haka tasan yana hanyar zuwa gunta dan tasan bazai iya zama ba tare da ita ba.



Yarima kuwa yafiya ya ci gaba dayi ko zai samu ya fita hanya ko zai samu Allah ya hada shi da mai taimakon sa, har magrib tayi bai samu hanya ba kuma baiga Alamar da mutum a cikin dajin nan ba, 



Taimama yayi yayi Sallah bayan yayi Addu'a ya ci gaba da tafiya ga yunwa ga kishirwa tana damunsa.


Koda ya kintati isha tayi ya tsaya yayi Sallah sannan yaci ægaba da tayi har ya ji karfinsa ya kare sannan ya tsaya


A jikin bishiya wata bishiya ya jingina wanda a lokacin badan farin wata ba da ko tafin hannunsa ba zai iya gani ba.


Tun yana jingine har ya kwanta a nan barcin wahala ya kwashe shi




Bangaren Sumayya kuwa tana isa gida gun sarki Abdulbasid tayi tana zuwa ta saka kuka

Yace "Lafiya kika zo min da kuka"


Cikin kuka tace "Yarima Amar ya sake ni"



Sarki ya mike yace "Ya sake ki da ciki a jikinki, kuma Sarki Abdulhamid din yana kallo, to wlh bai isaba ni zan dau mataki akan dansa"



Sumayya tace "Wai saboda cikin jikina ba nasa bane, kuma dan uwansa shi yamin fyade "



Sarki ya kara tunzira yace "Cin zarafinki ashe suka yi, to kuwa saina kai su kara 'yar tawa guda daya"



Tace "Ranka ya dade ka kyale su, kawai idan na haihu zan kai musu dan su ni na barsu da Allah"



Sarki ya dubeta cikin tausayawa yace "Sumayya ke daya kika rage min da kuna ku biyu' yar uwarki tazo ta mutu, yanzu ke daya kika rage min dole ki bari in kwatar miki hakkinki"



Tace "Aa mai martaba ka barshi na barsu da Allah"



Sarki ya ce "To shikenan kije ba damuwa idan kin haihu ma san abin yi"


Ta tashi ta tafi sassan ta wanda mamantane a ciki yanzu tana zuwa bata boye wa maman ba ta fada mata komai



Maman tace "ay kuwa bazai yuyu ki bar masarautar ba, dole Marwan ya aure ki"



Tace "Mama ni gaskiya bana son Marwan, Yarima shi nake so"



Tace "Yaushe zaki samu yarima yanzu, ay ko baki masa komai bama sake ki zaiyi yadda yake auren 'yar gidan Sarkin Samina "



Kuka ta fasa kuwa ta rumgume tana lallashinta, can ta tallafo mata fuska tana son su hada ido da diyar tata, ita ko Sumayya tallafo mata kan da tayi yasa warin bakin maman ya doki hancin Sumayya tuni ta fizge jikinta tace "Mama bakin ki wari"



Maman ta mata mungun kallo tace "Dan uban ki ni zaki cewa ina warin baki"



Sumayya tace "yi hakuri, muyi maganar yadda zamu bullo musu"


Maman ta ce "kedai ki bari ki haihu tukun"



Nasrin kuwa har dare ba taga Yarima ba sai tace "Hala sai gobe"



Yarima kuwa sanyin asuba ne ya tada shi yana tashi ua dubi gabas yaga Alfijir ya fito ya yi taimama yayi sallah asuba, sannan ya zauna a gurin yana karatun Aqur'ani mai girma.


Saida rana ta fito ya tashi yaci gaba da tafiya ga yunwa ga kishir ruwa, 



Saida yayi tafiya me nisa daga nesa dashi ya hango tsuntsaye a sama suna shawagi, murmushi u
Yayi dan yasan za'a samu ruwa a gurin,


Ya fadi yayi a gurin yayi sujjada ya gode wa ubangijinsa ya tashi ya nufi gurin, yana zuwa gurin yaga ruwan ya sa hannu ya diba ya kuskure bakinsa, ya wanke jikinsa, yasha, har zai tafi ya ya hango kifaye a cikin ruwan,  ay kuwa ya tsaya ya kama manya ya koma gefe ya nemi kara ya tsaga cikin kifin ya cire kayan cikin ya shanya a rana, sannan ya ringa zaga gurin ko zai samu bishiya me 'ya'ya yaci 'ya'yan, saidai bai samu ba, haka ya dawo,ya kama kifin ya sake shanyawa, ya zauna, yinin ranan haka ya yini, da har yayi tunanin barin gurin sai kuma yace to idan ya bar gurin baisan inda zai dosa ba tunda bai taba zuwa Samina ba, sannan idan yabar nan ina zai samu ruwa irin haka. 



Sai bayan sallah isha ne yaje ya dauko kifin daya shanya da yake anyi rana saura kadan ya karasa bushewa, haka yaci yana tuna Nasrin da take bashi abinci da yatsun hannunta tana masa labari. Bayan yaci ya koshi , ya mayar gurin ya kwanta dan yayi wa kansa Alkawari bazai bar gurin ba, kuma bazai zauna da yunwa ba tunda Allah yasan dashi a gurin kuma ya bashi abinci, ay kuwa ya gode masa, to ma idan ya tafin ina zai dosa, idan yaga ba inda zaije ruwan nan ya bace masa fa, dole ya zauna anan hala rayuwarsa ne zata kare anan,



Yana karasa kwanciya yayi addu'an bacci ya kwanta bacci ya kwashe shi



Nasrin kuwa yauma haka ta kwanta da kewar yarima koda ta kwanta ma mafarkin sa tayi tayi yana cikin wani hali, Tana sallah asuba ko karatun da take yi bata yi ba ta fito tayi dakin Umma ta sameta akan sallaya tace "Umma Yarima"


Tace "me ya faru da Yariman"



"Umma Ina tunanin yaruma yana cikin wani hali, yau zan tafi Mula dan in gashi"



Umma tace "Wa ya fada miki"


Tace "Mafarki nayi fa"


Umma tace "kinji maganar banza kuma mafarki gaskiya ne "



Nasrin tayi shiru batace komaiba


Umma ta kara cewa "To kada in kara jin kince zaki tafi, Yarima shi zaizo kuma nasan yau ko gobe zaizo"



Nasrin ta daga kai saidai hankinta bai kwanta ba.


Wasa wasa Kwanansu biyar da zuwa amma ba Yarima, hankalin Nasrin ya tashi, gashi ko barcin rana tayi sai tayi mafarkinsa, itama Umma ta fara damuwa, dan har tayi tunanin zuwa ta duba shi



Yarima kuwa bayan ya gama karyawa da bushashen kifinsa ya kwanta gun kwanciyarsa ya daga kai sama sai ya hango katon maciji, da sauri Yarima ya mike yace "Bawan Allah kada ka cutar dani, nasan gurinku nazo na zauna, nima bani da gurin zuwa yasa nazo nan na zauna, Idan nayi niyyar kashe ka zan iya, amma bazan maka komai ba, saboda haka nima kada ka cutar da ni, amma idan ka sake ka cutar da to ni zan kashe ka"



Yana gama fada ya koma yayi kwanciyarsa.

Wannan page din naki ne Ummu siyama kanwar Yarima a gaskiya ina jin dadin comments din ki kuma kina zuciya ta💋💋

Page 3⃣7⃣


Macijin kuwa juyawa yayi yayi wani reshen yayi tafiyarsa ba tare da ya cutar da Yarima ba

BAYAN SATI BIYU

Umma kuwa shiryawa tayi tsad ta tafi garin mula dan duba Yarima dan tasan ba lafiya ba gashi a 'yan kwanakin Nasrin sai rama take kuma tasawa ranta damuwa da yawa wanda har ya shafi Sarauniya Bilkisu


Bangaren Ummi kuwa hankalinta kwance dan cewa tayi yanzu haka Yarima yana gun Nasrin yayi zaman sa, haka sarki ma

Sarauniya kuwa yanzu tana niman yadda za ayi a fito da Marwan saidai Sarki idan ya ganta ma kamar yaga banza, dan kyamarta yake.


Umma tana zuwa masarautar sassan Ummi ta shiga Ummi na ganin ta ta taso ta rumgumeta ta bata gurin zama aka cika mata gabanta da kayan marmari, bayan Ummi ta zauna sun gaisa tace "Dan wajenki kan ya samu Nasrin yaki dawowa ko"

Umma tace "Wa kenan "


Tace "Yarima mana, ba yana can ba"


Umma ta waro ido tace "wani Yariman"


Ummi tace "wani magana kike ne, Yarima Amar mana kuma Yariman gidan nan"


Umma ta kalleta tace "To ay zuwan da nayi nazo in duba shi ko lafiya baizo ya ga Nasrin ba, gata can tasa ranta a damuwa saboda bata ganshi ba"


Ummi ta tashi tsaye ta kalleta tace "Tun washe garin tafiyar ku Yarima yabar garin nan dan zuwa gun Nasrin "


Umma ma ta mike tace "To wlh bamu ganshi ba"


Ummi tace "Innalillahi wa Inna'ilaihir raju"un, to ina Yarima ya shiga"


Kwalla ne ya fara zubo mata Umma ta zaunar da ita tace "Zauna"

Ummi ta kara mikewa ta fita Umma ma ta bita a baya, Fada tayi tana zuwa ta durk'usa tace "Ranka ya dad'e Yarima ya bata"


Sarki ya tashi tsaye yace "Aisha kinsan me kike fada kuwa"


Tace "yanzu Baba Hauwa tazo take sanar min wai bai isa Samina ba"


Zaiyi magana Umma ta shigo itama ta durkusa tana zubda Kwalla


Yace "Me nake ji ne wai "


Baba hauwa ta kasa magana sai kuka


Sarki yace "Aje a shirya amalanke yanzu zan tafi garin Samina"


Sarauniya kuwa kamar ta zuba ruwa a k'asa tasha saboda dad'i


Kafin tace me an shirya komai Sarki da Ummi suka shiga, yayin da Umma ma aka sama mata wani, Sai magrib suka isa Nasrin na ganin Ummi da Sarki tasan ba lafiya ay saita fashe da kuka, Ummi ta karaso ta rungumeta Nasrin tace "Ummi Kar kice min Yarima ya rasu"


Ummi tace "yana raye saidai bamu san inda yake ba"


Da sauri Nasrin ta fizge jikin ta tace "Yariman"


Sarauniya Bilkisu ce ta zo ta isar dasu ta musu hidima saidai sun kasa cin komai dan haka Sarauniya ta kaisu Fada 


Sarki Abdulhamid yana zuwa ya durkusa gaban Sarki Abdurrahman, Ummi ma tayi yadda mijinta yayi yace "Ranka ya dade ka yafe wa Amar kada ka azabtar dashi ba laifin sa bane"


Da sauri Nasrin tace "Abbana dama yana tare dakai" 


Sarki yace "Ay bansan wa kuke nufi ba"


Sarauniya yace "Yarima Amar fa bai iso nan ba, ya zamuyi mu azabtar dashi"


Sarki yace "Yariman ne ba a ganshi ba"


Yaje ya dafa kafadar Sarki Abdulhamid yace "Ka tashi ka zauna akan kujera muyi magana "


Ya tashi Sarauniya tazo ta mikar da Ummi ta zaunar da ita tace "Ki kwantar da hankalinki "


Sarki Abdurrahman yayi  gyaran murya yace "shi matukin Amalanken Yarima ya dawo"


Sarki Abdulhamid yace "Ya dawo yace mana ya kawoshi har kofar gida, to shine da na ji bai iso ba shine nace ko kana masa hukunci ne, akan abin da mukayi wa Gimbiya Nasrin a bisa rashin sani ayi hakuri dai Ranka ya dad'e "


Sarki Abdurrahman yace "Bai kawo shi ba kan, yanzu yana ina"


Yace "dashi shima muka zo shi ya kawo mu"


Sarki Abdurrahman yace "A shigo dashi"


A kaje aka shigo da Bello ya durk'usa gaban sarki, sarki yace " Ranan da ka dauki Yarima Amar Ina ka kaishi"

In ina ya fara yana hanya-hanya 


Nasrin ta daka masa tsawa "baza ka fada ba"


Cikin sa ne ya bada kuuuuu da sauri yace "Kofar masarautar nan"


Nasrin tace "Karya kake Idan kaki fada min inda Yarima yake saina sa an yi maka Azaba"


Ya tsorata sosai sai zufa yake da ka ganshi kasan bashi da gaskiya 


Sarauniya Bilkisu yace "Dan saurayi baza ka fada ba"


Nasrin tace "Ranka ya dad'e ka bani shi in masa hukunci"


Sarki Abdurrahman yace "Ka fada ko in bada kai"


Cikin in ina yace "A cikin daji na ajiye shi"


Da sauri Nasrin tace "Akan me to"


Cikin tsoro ya fada musu komai


Ran Nasrin ya baci tace "Abbana kaban Izini zan hukunta mutane hudu, Na farko Gimbiya Sumayya data kashe min kawata, Aminyata Basma, na biyu Marwan da yaso yamin fyade Allah bai bashi sa'a ba, na uku Wannan Bellon da ya Salwantar min da Yarima, na hudu, Sarauniya Yamuna, da take son ganin bayan mijina"



Taje ta durk'usa gaban Sarki Abdulhamid tace "Ranka ya dad'e kayi hakuri zan hukunta Sarauniya Yamuna akan ta taba min mijina"


Yace "idan kin gama hukunta na taba miki miji da kika yi, to nima zan hukuntata na Salwantar min da magaji da tayi



Ta mike ta kalli fadawa tace "Gobe da asuba ina son kuje garin mula a taho min da Sarauniyar su"


A tare suka ce "An gama raki ya dad'e "


Ta nuna Bello tace "wannan kuma aje a kulle min shi"



Tana gama fada aka dauke shi aka fita dashi


Sarki yace "To shikenan ku kwantar da hankalin ku gobe da safe zamu je dajin mu duba shi"



Ummi tasa kuka tace "Ni na san baza a same shi ba, dajin namun daji suka yi yawa, dajin da ba Abinci"


Nasrin tace "Ummi wannan wani irin daji ne"


Ummi tace "Nasrin mu fidda rai kan Yarima kawai, mu raini cikin dake jikin ki"


Nasrin ta durk'usa tace "Ummi idan Yarima ya mutu nima mutuwa zanyi, Ummi bazan iya rayuwa ba Yarima ba"


Sarauniya ce ta isa ta ce "Nasrin ki dawo hayyacinki mana"

Kai ta karyar a jikin Sarauniya ta suma, Sultan da Anisa ne suka fasa ihu, Sultan cewa yake wlh suka kashe min 'yar uwa saina kashe duk dangin Sarauniya Yamuna dana Bello.

Story 
        &

UMMU MUFEED
      (Mrs Aliyu)


Wannan page din naki ne Maman Haneefa da Fatan Allah ya baki lafiya



*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*




Page 3⃣8⃣

Sauran 'yan wajen salati suke tayi tuni akayi da Nasrin sassanta, Ummi ta sa mata ruwa a fuska ta farfado tana kallonsu sai kuma tasa kuka Umma tace "Menene kuma, me yasa baza ki yadda da kaddara mummuna ko kyakykyawa ba"


Sarauniya Bilkisu tace "Wa yace muku Yarima ya mutu yana raye kuma za a ganshi"


Umma ce ta ringa musu wa'azi a cikin *HIKIMA* har kowa ya kwantar da hankalin sa


Koda suka zo kwanciya Ummi a dakin Umma ta kwana Sarki kuma ya kwana a dakin na musamman da Sarki ya bashi, Washe gari da sassafe Sarki da Ummi suka nemi hanyan mula, yayin da 'yan aiken Nasrin sunyi nisa dan tun asuba suka kama hanya suna zuwa masarautar suka yi bayanin kansu, sannan suka tambayi gun Sarauniya, aka nuna musu. A kishingide suka sameta suka kamata suka daure mata hannu suka sata cikin amalenken da suka zo da ita suka tafi abin, 


Nasrin kuwa bayan tayi wanka ta shirya ta kashigida tana jiran a kawa mata Sarauniya Yamuna dan yau saita nuna mata shageranci, yayinda Sarki da mutanensa tare da Bello kuma suka nufi daji dan neman Yarima


Yana kashingide wani bafade yazo ya durk'usa yace "Ranki ya dad'e an kawo Sarauniya Yamuna "


Kamar baza tayi magana ba sai can kuma tace "A tsare ta kuma a bata Abinci da ruwa"


Yace "An gama ranki ya dad'e" 



Ba a jima ba ta tashi da kuyangunta a baya sukayi inda aka tsare Sarauniya Yamuna


 Suka samu ta gama cin abinci ita kuwa Sarauniya tana kallonta tace "Nasrin! Dama kece kika sa aka kawo ni nan, me na miki"



Nasrin tace "Ina kika kaimin mijina"


Tace "Mijinki kuma "


"Eh Yarima Amar ina kika kaishi"


Tace "Nifa ban san inda yake ba"


Nasrin ta kalli Sarkin duka dake bayanta tace "Ka tambayeta"


Ya fara dukan ta da bulalan dukam dawaki, jikinta ya farfashe har sai da Nasrin ta fara jin tausayinta tace "a kyaleta"


Ta matso tace "kinsan Bello yana tsare a gidan nan kuma shi ya fada mana kece wacce kika shi ya Salwantar da Yarima "


Sarauniya tace "Kiyi hakuri wlh ban san inda yake ba"


Nasrin tace "za a zaunar dake anan ayi ta baki hawala idan ba a ga Yarima na ba"



Tana gama fada ta fita 



Yarima kuwa bai matsa inda yake ba yana bakin ruwa abincin sa kuma bushashshen kifi ne, idan ka ganshi baza ka ce wannan shine magajin Mula ba, saboda yadda yayi baki ga munguwa rama da yayi duk da baya sa damuwa aran sa, sannan kullum yakan yi karatun Alkur'ani mai girma



Ha sarki suka zo suka karashi duba Yarima ammabasu banshi bakarshe dai haka suka hakura suka koma,



Sarki Abdulhamid  kuwa suna  komawa ya sanar wa 'ya fadan sa komai, kowa yayi Allah wadai da halin Sarauniya 



Yarima yahau kan bishiya yana dube dube sai ya hango mafarauci yana zuwa a doki, koda mafaraucin ya kusa iso wa sai yaga halamar me rai akan bishiya sai ya nad'a kwari da bakansa zaiyi harbi koda Yarima yaga haka sai yayi saurin durowa k'asa


Yace "mutum ne ko Aljan "



Yarima yace "Mutum ne"


Mafarauci ya karewa Yarima kallo yana karanto Addua a bakinsa can yace "Me ya kawoka dajin nan "


Yarima ya fada masa komai 


Mafaraucin Yace "Yanzu ina kake son zuwa Samina ko garinku Mula"


Yarima yace "Samina nake son zuwa "


Yace "To zan baka dokina ka tafi dashi idan na gama kwanaki na a dajin nan zanzo garinku na amsa"


Yarima yace "Kai yaya zakayi ka dawo"


Yace "kada ka damu ni ina waya a dajin nan ma ay amma in ina dadewa ban zo ba, ka tafi kawai idan na yashi zanzo"


Yarima yace "Da nasan hanya da ko kwana nawa zanyi zanyi yafiya a kafa in tafi garin mu"


Yace "muje in nuna muku hanya "


Suka tafi ya nuna masa hanya sannan yace da dokin "Ka kula da Yarima kada ka masa taurin kai kaji nawan"



Dokin yayi haniniya Madu yace "Yarima a sauka lafiya Allah ya tsare"


Yace "Amin " Suka fara tafiya



Nasrin kuwa sake komawa tayi gum Sarauniya tausayinta ne ya kamata sai kuma ta tuna Ko a wani hali Yarima yake ciki oho


Tace "Fada min Yarima yana ina, Sarki sun dawo sunce basu ganshi a dajin nan ba"


Sarauniya tace "to nima ban sani ba"


Nasrin tace "Zaki zauna anan a tsare bazan sake kiba sai ranan da Yarima ya dawo, idan ko ya mutu kina zaune anan har ranan mutuwarki"


Sarauniya ta kama kafarta tace "kiyi hakuri dan Allah, tausayinta ya kamata, amma da ta tuna Yarima sai ta fizge jikin ta ta fita. Tana fita sai ta fara kwalla.



Koda Yarima ya isa Samina da tambaya ya isa Fada, yana isa Fadawa suka tare shi suka ce "Dan samari ina zaka je"


Yace "Nazo gun Nasrin ce"


Suka ce wacece me wannan suna


Yace "'yar Sarki wanda aka kawota kwanan nan"



Yace "ohhh Gimbiya Nasrin, to kai wacece"


Yace "Ni mijinta ne,"


Masu gadin suka kalli juna suka sake duban Yarima da kayan sa, daya yace "Kai yaro kada ka raina mana hankali, Gimbiyar da 'yar sarkin Mula ke aurenta zaka ce kaine mijinta, "


Yarima ya dubi jikinsa yace "Nine Yarima Amar dan Sarkin Mula "


Suka ce "karya kake, kabar nan gurin ko mu kira Dogarai su zaneka"


Yarima yace "Dan Allah ku kira min Nasrin idan ta nuna bata sanni bq komai kumin"


Daya daga cikinsu yazo yace "Da ka ce kana niman taimako ne, zamu barka kaganta amma tunda kace haka to ba wanda zai bar ka, saboda ansan kai mungu ne, shawaran da zan baka ka tafi kawai, idan ba haka ba dogarawa idan suka zo a cikin bayi zasu saka"


Yace "Bazan je ko inaba sai naga Nasrin "


Yace "To shikenan "



Yarima ya sauko Yace "Baba nine Yarima Amar, Dan sarkin Mula zan baka labarin abin da ya faru har ka ganni a..."


Maganar ta katse sakamakon jin da yayi an kama shi ana jan shi, yana dubawa yaga dogarawa ne 


Yace "ku tsaya in muku bayani"


Amma ko kulashi basuyi ba suka shiga dashi ciki saida suka sashi cikin bayi sannan suka rufe kofar suka fita abin su


Yarima yace "Ohh Allah ka taimake ni daga wannan sai wannan, na gode wa Allah da ya kawoni nan ma dan da dajin nan gwara nan"
