      (Mrs Aliyu)


Wannan page din naka ne masoyina Uban 'yayana da bazarka nake rawa, kuma kaine me kara min karfin gwiwa dole in fada wa duniya kana ji damu, kuma muma muna ji da kai,💓💓💘 I LOVE YOU Nurul qalby, Ina sonka mijina, Aliyu Muhammad (Babaye) Allah ya karo mana Arziki ta hanyar Alheri💔💘❣❤👏🏻


*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 3⃣9⃣


Bangaren Ummi kuwa tunda suka dawo take kwance ba lafiya dan sunji labarin ba a same shi ba, Dan ma Sarki yana kula da ita nesa ba kusa ba, shi din ma daurewa yake dan a yanzu tausayinta yake ji, kuma yana jinta a ransa dan mace ce me hakuri 


Shida kansa yake bata abinci saboda kulan da yake bata ne yasa har ta fara samun sauki, saidai ba walwala



Haka Nasrin ma duk da kulan da ake bata amma abu yaci tura dan yanzu ko fitowa batayi cikinta kuwa ya fara fitowa. Kuma kullun cikin Addua take tana kuka, Abin har ya shafi Sultan da Anisa, su suke tayata zama, idan ta yashi aikin Adduan nata da kuka suma su zauna suna taya ta.



Yarima kuwa duk wani aikin gidan dashi ake kuma bai damu ba, dan cewa yayi shima dole yayi tunda Nasrin ma tayi aikin bauta a gidan su.



Kwanansa uku a gidan saidai yana ta raba ido ko zaiga Nasrin saidai har yau bai ganta ba, Saidai dan saurayi da yake gani da Alama shine Yariman gidan 



Rayuwar Sumayya a gidan Rayuwa ne na 'yan awaki dan kullun sai tayi fada da Mamanta ita kuma tayita lallashi 


Ammi (maman basma) ta sake haihuwa 'yan biyu mace da namiji ba ruwanta dasu sai harkikinta da take yi, haka sauran matan sarkin ma basa ce mata komai 



Tana kwace taji wani irin ciwon mara, ay kuwa ta zuba ihu Mama tace "kee me haka zaki damen ina barci"


Tace "mara ta Mama"


Maman ta tashi ta kalli yadda take murkususu tana ihu tace "
"Haihuwa zakiyi barin samo miki magani"



Ta fita itako Gimbiya Sumayya sai ihu take bakin nan yayi dogo cewa take "Wayyyo Marwan ka cuce ni, Allah ya tsine maka marwan Sarauniya Yamuna Allah ya isa ban yafeba"


Har mama ta dawo tana ihu, ta bata maganin tasha ay kuwa ciwo ya karu sai ihu take tana karawa tana birgima a cikin dakin, koda haihuwar tazo saida Mama ta kira bayi suka riketa dan matse kafar tayi. 


Cikin ikon Allah ta haifi danta Namiji me kama da Sarauniya Yamuna.


Koda aka gyarata tuni tahau barcin wahala


Kwanansa biyar baiga Nasrin ba zuwa yanzu ya fara damuwa da rashin ganin nata, dan har ya fara tambayan bayi saidai duk wanda ya tambaya sai yace "tana nan"
"

Sultan ne ya shiga cikin bayi ya zabo mutane uku ciki harda Yarima ya kaisu dakin sa yace "Ku gyara min"

Yana fada ya koma ya zauna 

Suna cikin gyarawa Anisa ta shigo da gudu tace "Yaya kazo Anty Nasrin bata da lafiya zata mutu sai kiran Yarimanta take yi"


Yace "ohh Wannan Yarima ina ka shiga ne"



Zai tashi kenan yaga Yarima a gabansa yana hawaye yace "Baka da lafiya ne"


Yarima ya ce "Ka taimaken ka hadani da Nasrin nine Yariman da take nima"


Sultan yayi masa mungun kallo yace "Ta ina zaka zama Yariman 'yar uwata"


Yarima zai kara magana Sultan ya daga masa hannu ya kama hannun Anisa  suka tafi da gudu, shiko ya bisu har sassan Yarima ya bisu yana zuwa bakin kofar dakin Nasrin 
Sultan juyo suna yana ganin Yarima ya daka masa tsawa 

Yarima kuwa daga ciki yaji muryan Umma yana cewa "Nasrin kada ki mutu ki barmu, ki ci gaba da yiwa mijinki Addu'a zai dawo"


Sarauniya Bilkisu tace "Nasrin ki sa hakuri a zuciyar"


Sa'adatu tace "Yaufa ta suma kusan sau biyar ga ciki a jikinta kuma, kada yasamu matsala ma"


Yarima yace "Ka matsamin inga matata"


Sultan yace "Kai baka da hankaline"


Yarima yayi ta maza ya jawo Sultan ya kaishi gefe yashiga dakin da gudun sa. Yana shiga yaga Nasrin kwance kan gado ga dan tudun cikin ta, sai wani mungun rama da tayi, baiyi wata wata ba ya karasa ya rungumota sai yasa kuka me tsuma zuciya, 


daidai lokacin kuma Sultan ya karasa shigowa da gudu yaga Yarima tsakiyan gado rungume da gimbiya Nasrin.


Umma ce ta fara gane shi tace "Yarima ma!"


A hankali Nasrin ta dago fuska ta kalleshi sai ta mayar tace "Umma kin gani ko har yanzu Yarima yana min gizo"


Umma tace "Nasrin bude idonki Yarima ne"


Ta kuma bude ido ta kalle shi yana kuka ita ma kukanta fasa wanda ya zama mata sabo 



Kara matse ta yayi a jikin sa yana jijjigata kamar jaririya


Ita tuwa kara gyara zama tayi ta zauna kan cinyarsa ta kwantar da kai a k'irjinsa tace "Yarima ina ka shiga zaka sa zuciyata ta fashe in mutu"



Sarauniya Bilkisu da Sa'adatu barin gurin sukayi saboda abin kunya ya basu, sai Sultan, Anisa da Umma da suke tsaye


Kara matseta yayi yace "Labarin da dan tsawo ki daina kuka tukun kukanki yana tada min hankali"



Tace "Kaima ka daina kuka"


Sun dade a haka kafin nan kuma ya kalleta yace " Zaki iya tashi ne muje gun sarki, ina gaishe shi, na gaida mutanen gidan sai na yi wanka"


Umma tace "Ay ko tafiya bata iya yi "


Da sauri tace "Zan iya amma ka fara yin wanka tukunna"



Ta yunkura ta tashi daga jikinsa tayi luuuuu zata fadi ya riko ta yace "zauna tukun"


Umma tace "Kwanan ta biyar ko Abinci bata ci sai ruwa"



Yace "Umma kawo mana Abinci "


Tace "To ta fita can sai gata da Abinci da madara me zafi"


Madaran ya fara bata tasha sannan ya bata Abinci ta danci ba laifi, Umma ce ta hada masa ruwan wanka a bayi ya shiga, kafin ya fito Sultan ya kawo Sabbin kaya yace "Gashi inji Sarki yasa kafin a kaishi garin su"


Ta amsa ta feshe su da furare ta ajiye gefe ta tashi taje ta nimo Masa kayan marmari da mutuniyarsa madara, 


Yana firowa ya karyar da kai yace "Nasrin ki shafa min mai "


Kunya ce ta kamata ta rufe idon ta tana murmushi


Yace "kunyata wai kike ji, gaskiya bana so, kimin yadda kike mun da"


Shiru tayi idon ta a rufe yace "Haba tawan kin manta Yarimanki ne, kin manta ke kike min wanka, ki bani abinci, kimin wasa kiyi wanka a gabana, kai kin manta har tsarki... " kokarin guduwa tayi cikin zafin nama ya rikota ya hadata da kirjinsa yace "Kina gudu, kin manta ba ke daya bace,"


Hannu yakai ta cikin doguwar rigarta yana shafa cikin, ay kuwa cikin ya fara motsawa Yarima yayi murmushi yace "Ashe kasan ina nan, To ya kake, ya zaman cikin Umminka da dadi ko, kai ay komai na Umminka mai dadi ne, ka kwanta lafiya kaji"



Sarauniya Bilkisu ce ta shigo a tunaninta har yanzu Nasrin tana kwance ne dan tazo ta daga ta daga jikinsa y samu yayi wanka, sai gashi ta same su a wani irin yanayi wanda ko sugowanta masu sani ba, da sauri ta fita tana mamaki yanzu yanzu Nasrin take kwance amma gashi ta tashi, ohh dan yau ba kunya duba yadda ya kanainayeta kuma tana gidan su


Yarima kuwa da yaga bata da niyyar shafa masa mai din. Da samasa kayan yasa yasa abinsa, sannan ya zauna yasha madara wanda yayi kewarta.

Wannan page din naki ne Zee shareef kiyi yadda kike so dashi



*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 4⃣0⃣


Bayan ya gama ne ya kalleta yace " ki kaini gun Sarki idan zaki iya tawan"

Ta kauda kai cikin murmushi tace "zan iya"


Sarki kuwa yana jin labarin bayyanar Yarima ya aikawa Sarki Abdulhamid 


A yare suka fito ya ruke hannunta suka yi Fada Kowa da kowa na fada amma Yarima yaki y sake mata hannu ita ko sai murna take, yayin da Gimbiya Bilkisu take farin ciki a ranta 'yarta ta samu mijin da yake sonta.


Tsugunawa sukayi Yarima ya gaida Sarki bayan sun gaisa ne Sarki yace "Dana ina ka shigane munje nimanka a dajin da akace an ajiyeka amma bamu ganka ba"


Yarima ya basu labarin komai,  kowa na fadan ya tausaya masa, daga bisani Sultan ya matso kusa dashi yace  "Dan uwa ka yafe min rashin sanine"


Yarima yayi murmushi yace "Kada ka damu kaji"


Nasrin tace "Muje kaga Sarauniya Yamuna da Bello suna tsare"


Ya mike suka fara zuwa gun Sarauniya tana ganin su ta mike Nasrin tace "Ga Yarima Allah yayi ya dawo, saboda ransa ba a hannki yake ba"


Yarima ya matsa gabanta yace "Umma me ne miki ne, kike niman rayuwata, saboda sarata ko, Idan da kinsa hakuri ma kamar shine me sarautar, kin rabani da Dan uwa, kin kasa bashi tarbiyyan data dace, kinsa mahaifiya ta s gaba, ita kuma ta rike ki da Alheri,  yanzu gashi ana miki hukunci." ya kalli Nasrin yace "Ki mata afuwa ta koma gida"


Tace "yanzu kan sai abin da kace ay"


Yayi murmushi ya shafa fuskarta  yace "Nagode  masoyiyata "


Ita ma murmushi tayi. yakalli Sarauniya yace "To saiki tafi mula yanzu"


Ta mike tsaye da sauri tayi waje tana d'ingisawa dan sun ji mata ciwo a kafa wajen dukanta da suka yi


Tana fita suka fito sukayi gun Bello yana ganin Yarima da Nasrin suka ya mike yana tsoro ganin haka yasa Yarima ya kalli Nasrin yace "Kiyi masa afuwa shima ya tafi"


Tace "Ba komai"


Bello fashewa yayi da kuka ya fadi yana godiya


Da daddare saiga Ummi da Sarki Abdulhamid, dan ana fada musu suka ce sai sunzo, lokacin har su Yarima zasu kwanta dan yace bazai taba barin ta ba ita daya ba, sai suka ji labarin zuwan su, Da sairi Yarima ya fita yana zuwa ya rumgume Ummi sai ta saki kuka me ban tausayi, lallashinta ya shiga yi saida tayi shiru yace "Ummi kin rame da yawa me yasa haka "


Tace "ba dole in rame ba Yarima ba a san inda kake ba"


Sarki yace "Ni ka manta dani ko Magaji "


Yayi saurin zuwa ya gaishe shi yace "Haba ranka ya dade yaya za ayi in mantaka"


Bayan aci ansha ne aka zauna ana ta hira Nasrin ta fara gyangyadi Yarima ya jawota ya kwantar da ita a cinyarsa,  duk 'yan wajen saida abin ya basu kunya, yayin da ita kuma ta janye jikinta ya kara jawota jikinsa, Ummi kuwa ta watsa masa harara, kai ya sunkuyar yaki hada ido da ita, Umma da ta fahimce su sai tace "Nasrin kije ki kwanta ko "


Da sauri Yarima yace "eh muje dama nima barci nake ji"

Ummi tace "ka kyaleta kada kaje ka takura mata"

Yace "kai Ummi wani irin takura kuma, kin jima baki ganniba kuma yanzu kin ganni baza ki barni nayi abinda nake so ba"


Nasrin tayi gaba Yarima ya bita, Ummi tace "Malam ina zuwa"

Nasrin tace "Ummi raka ni zaiyi fa, hanyar da duhu"

Ummi tace "Ohhh rakaki zaiyi ko, "


Sarauniya Bilkisu tayi murmushi tace "Allah ya kara me ya kaiki shiga harkan yara"


Sarki Abdurrahman yace "Kuje abin ku Allah ya tashe mu lafiya "

Bayan sun tafi Sarki yace "Nifa sun burge ni, ina son Namiji irin haka, kada yana jin kunyar kowa akan matarsa, hakan yana da kyau "


Ya kalli Sarki Abdulhamid yace "Kayi farin ciki Yarima ya dace ya gaji sarauta"


Sarki yace "Ay ina son daga ya koma gida zanyi murabus, dan ina son in huta yanzu ma, dan matsalolin da ya faru ne badan haka ba da yanzu shine sarkin mula"


Sarki Abdurrahman yace "Masha Allah Nima ina jiran Sultan yayi aure ne in bashi nima in huta, kaga sa fara tun suna yaran su zayi musu sauki nan gaba"


Sarauniya tanisa tace "Me zai hana a bari Nasrin ta haihu sai a hada da bikin suna dana Nadin sarauta"



Sarki Abdurrahman yace "Aa ki barsu suyi abin da sukayi niyya mu namu a gayyace mu muje"


Nasrin kuwa tana shiga bayi ta fada ganin zai biyota yasa ta kulle saida tayi wankan ta, a ciki tasa kayan dan bata san me yasa ba, yau wani kunyarsa take ji, 


Shi kuwa yana ganinta haka yace "Ohh shine kika sa kaya a ciki saboda kada na ganki ko"


Kauda kai yayi shiko bai kara magana ba sai shiga bayi dan yin wanka, yana shiga tasa kayan barcinta ta dauki bargo ta shimfida a kasa ta kwanta. daga shi sai gajeren wando ya fito. Ya kalleta yace "Me haka kuma "


Tace "Name"

Yace "Malama idan so kike ki hana ni gadonki ki fada min,"


Tace "Ba ga gadon ba"

Ya harareta yace "Idan kina son bani ay akai zan same ki nima sai in kwanta ay, amma kin kwanta a kasa nima kasan zan zo ay"


Tayi shiru cikin kunya yace "To kodai baki son kwanciya dani ne saboda kina sama dani"


Da saurin ta rufe bakinta da tafin hannunta tace "Ni nace maka haka"

Ya tako ya dagota Yace "muje tawa , kada sanyi ya shafi dan Abba"


Ta taso badan ranta ya taso ba ta sauna a bakin gadon, tana ganinsa ya dauko bargon yazo ya kwanta, ta bayanta, itako ko kallonsa ta kasa yi saboda kunya, ji tayi ya jawota jikinsa ya kwantar da ita ya rufe musu jiki yace "Wai bakya kewar Yarimanki ne"

Bata ce komaiba, yace "kodai sarautar ce ta motsa"


Taki yin magana, sai jinshi tayi yasa mata hannu cikin riga yana wasa da na shanunta da suka ciko sukayi fam-fam saboda cikin dake jikinta

Da sauri ta kama hannunsa yace "Nasrin ki kyale ni mana"

Tadan sake hannun kadan yana wasa dasu ita kuwa tana rike da hannun, daga bisani kuma taji ya bakinsu waje daya yana tsotsa cikin salo, haka yayi ta wasa da ita har ya raba ta da kayan jikin ta, gani tayi yana niman wuce gona da iri da sauri ta tashi tace "Dan Allah kayi hakuri "


Ya kalleta cikin alamar tambaya 

Tace "Wlh baka san wahalar da nasha ba da kamin na farkon nan ba"

Ya tuna tun lokacin basu kara kasancewa ba yayi murmushi ya janyota jikinsa yace "Wancan na farko ne, shi yasa ya miki zafi, wannan bazai miki ba"

Ta girgiza kai tace "Aa gaskiya Ina yina tsoro kuma gashi anan muke Umman Sultan zata gane"


Yace "baza ta ganeba, dan Allah ki tausaya min"

Kuka tasa masa da sauri yace "ya isa in baki so nima na daina bazan muki ba"

Rungumeta yayi suka kwanta, saidai shi kuma bazai iya hakuri da itaba yadda yake jin kansa to amma yaya zaiyi tunda bata so, dabara ceta fado masa, dan yasanta da tausayi, suna a kwance ta jishi ya rike maransa yana "wash zan mutu "daga bisani kuma ya fara murkususu kamar zai fadi akan gadon, da sauri ta riko shi tace "Na shiga Aljanna, Yarima meke faruwa"


Yace "Nasrin marana"

Tace "Barin je gun Umma ta bani magani in kawo maka kasan wancan ita ta bamu"

Yace "Aa ki barshi, so kike asirinmu ya tonu, kuma ay idan kika je kowa sai yaji kuma cewa zasuyi, kin hana,kinga kuma bana son su ganki da laifi"

Tace "To me abin yi "

Yace "Ki kwanta kawai ki kyale ni haka zan samu sauki"

Ta kwanta tana tunani anya idan tabar Yarima haka ta masa Adalci gashi saboda kada a dora mata laifi ya kwammace ya zauna da ciwo gashi har yanzu ciwon nadamun sa

A fili tace "bazai yuyuba"

Saida ya cije yatsa yace "Magana kike"

Ba karamin jarumta tayi ba wajen kawar da kunyar ta matso ta manna kirjinta da nasa ta sa hannunta biyu ta dafa kuncin sa, ta hada bakinsu guri daya, cikin dabara ta tura harsheta ciki tana wasa da nashi harshen, cikin ransa kuwa yace "Me tausayi tabada kai kenan"

Ita da kanta take kirkiri wasanni take masa, shi kuwa mamaki ne yake, amma da ya tuna basiranta, yasan komai zata iya yin kimai ba tare da an koya mata ba, koda yazo shiganta bata hana shi ba, saima bashi goyon baya da tayi 


Ya mata zafi amma ba sosai ba, sai taji ma idan ya sake dawo mata zata amince saboda farincikin sa,

Rumgumeta yayi yana sa mata Albarka ita kuwa ta lumshe ido kalamansa suna mata dadi

Daukata yayi suka shiga bayi, saida tayi ta maza ta kuri kunyarta sannan ta yadda sukayi wanka tare, ya daukota yasata a saman gado tana kokarin janyo kayanta yace "me zakiyi"


Tace "Kaya zan saka "

Yace "Bana son kina sa kaya a duk lokacin da zamu kwanta tare, nafi son ganin ki haka,"

Tana gama fada yahau kan gadon ya janyota jikisa ya rumgumeta suka kwanta ya rufe musu jikinsu da bargo,

Tuni barci ya tafi dasu

Asuba ta gari masoya

Washe gari da sassafe Nasrin da Yarima basu tashi ba, Su Sarki Abdulhamid suka tafi, akan cewa Idan yarima ya tashi kwana biyu zaiyi ya koma gida mula,


Nasrin ce ta fara bude ido taga hasken rana tace "Innalillahi."

Yarima kuwa yajita ya tashi da yaga haske yace "mun makara"


A gurguje suka fada bayi suka alola suka fito sukayi Sallah Nasrin ce ya fara fadawa bayi tayi wanka ta fito shima ya shiga kafin ya fito ta shirya, shima a gurguje ya shirya suka fito tare, sai yanzu Yarima yake jin kunyar 'yan gidan, wai shine me wannan karfin halin, ya shiga da 'yar mutane daki kuma tsabar karfin hali harda makara, gun Sarauniya suka fara zuwa suka kwashi gaisuwa. Kan Yarima a sunkuye tace "Dana ka saki jikinka kaji, nan gidan ku ne"

Cike da kunya yace "To"

Suka yi gun Umma bayan sun gaisa tace "Sau nawa ina ta zuwa baku tashi ba"

Duka shiru sukayi saboda kunya musamman ma Yarima 

Itako data fahimci haka yasa tace "Kuje ku gaida mai martaba "


Suka mike suka nufi gun Sa'adatu nanma suka gaisheta sannan sukayi gun Sarki a fada, Kasa suka durkusa suka kwashi gaisuwa "bayan sun gaisa ne"

Yace "Allah ya muku Albarka, babanka sun jira har sun gaji baku tashi ba, sun tafi yace kwana biyu zaka kara sannan ka koma gida "

Kai ya sunkuyar da kai cikin kunya, saidai kuma da a sonsa ne da an barshi yadan more da Matarsa.


Bangaren Sarki Abdulhamid kuwa suna zuwa sukaga Sarauniya Yamuna, Sarki ya wuce Ummi kuwa tana tsaye tana mata mungun kallo,

Itako Sarauniya ganin sarki ya wuce yasa tace "Kada kiga kamar nayi nadama to ba nadama nayi ba, ni da hannuna saina kashe Danki Amar, dan bazai yuyu dana yana tsare amma danki ya gaji sarautar garin mula ba"


Ay kuwa sai a kunnen Sarki ashe dama ba nisa yayi ba, ya juyo ya kalleta...

        &
          Written  by
UMMU MUFEED
      (Mrs Aliyu)


Wannan page din Naki ne Maman Haidar kiyi yadda kike so dashi ina kaunarki💘💘


*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 4⃣1⃣



Yace "ehhh lallai kinyi magana to kije na sake ki saki daya kuma ki barmin masarautata"


Ihu Sarauniya Yamuna ta saka tana bawa sarki hakuri shi kuwa barin wajen yayi, Ummi kuwa duk da rashin mutumcin da aka mata saida taji badadi, bin Sarki tayi tana bashi hakuri amma ko ya kula ta, data dame shi ma daidai lokacin zai shiga daki ya juyo yace "Aisha ki tafi ki bani guri in kuwa kika biyo ni zan bata miki rai"


Shiru tayi ta koma sassan ta tana tuna wannan al'amari 


 Tun haihuwar Sumayya Sarki Abdulbasid ya aika wa Sarki Abdulhamid cewa, ta haihu, sarki Abdulhamid kuwa yana sane yaki zuwa kuma yaki aike, abin y batawa sarki Abdulbasid rai matuka, ya za'ayi su cuce 'yar tasa sannan kuma ta haihun ma ba zuwa ba aike, 

Har sati yazagayo ba aike ba zuwan mutum daya daga cikin masarautar Sarki Abdulhamid, 
Shikuwa sarki Abdulbasid  ya saka masa suna Tanimu, sannan yayi shirinsa ya tafi mula dan ganin ganin me yasa ya masa haka


Yana Sauka da fadawansa sarki ya masa tarban mutumci yasa aka kawo masa kayan marmari saidai ko kallo baiyi bama, Sarki Abdulhamid yana zuwa ya mika masa hannu dan suyi musabaha amma Sarki Abdulbasid yaki mika masa hannu sai cewa yayi "Danka shi yaga 'yata yace yana so, ba ni na mika masa nace na bashi sadaka ba, To me yasa bayan ya aureta, da yaji baya sonta bai sakar min 'yata ba, ya bari wani ya mata fyade. Sannan ya sakar min ita, kuma saboda rashin mutumci 'yata ta haihu na aika muku kunki zuwa kunki aike, an fada muku akuya ce, to wlh zan kai karan su daga wanda yayi sakin har wanda yayi Fyaden"


Sarki Abdulhamid yace "kayi hakuri, amma Sumayya ta fada maka abin da tayi kuwa, Wato taje tace maka an mata fyade ko, to ni ban san da wannan magana ba, ciki dai na dana Marwan ne amma ba fyade ya mata ba da yardarta sai ka tsaya ka saurareni kaji komai"


Sarki Abdulhamid ya fada masa komai tun lokacin da Yarima ya samu tabin hankali da kin kula dashi da tayi, sai baiwa ce ta kula dashi da kuma makircin da sukayi tayi da Sarauniya Yamuna da kuma Rashin mutuncin da tayi tayi musu,


Sarki Abdulbasid yayi shiru duk kunya ta ishe shi lalle shi ya dace ya bada hakuri amma ya kasa saboda kunya,

Sarki Abdulhamid yace "Kayi hakuri rashin zuwan da banyi ba Saboda batan Yarima me hankali ba a kwance yake ba"

Saida Sarki Abdulbasid ya tattaro jarumtarsa wajen kawar da kunya  sannan yace "Kayi hakuri, ni ya dace in baku hakuri, bansan irin rashin mutumcin da Sumayya ta muku ba kenan, da zuwan da zanyi na bada hakuri ne, ba na fushiba"

Sarki Abdulhamid yace "Kada ka damu komai ya wuce itama Allah ya bata miji tayi aure "

Sarki Abdulbasid baice komaiba saboda ransa ya baci Allah Allah yake ya jishi a garin gauda.

Sarki Abdulhamid yace "Bismillah mana kaci 'ya'yan itace, saidai kayi hakuri bamusan da juwanka ba da mun maka hidima"

Sarki Abdulbasid ya dauki Ayaba daya ya bare ya fara ci, saidai Ayabar daci take masa saboda irin bakin cikin da yake ciki

Yana karasa cinyewa ya mike yace "Zamu tafi Nagode a kara hakuri dan Allah "


Sarki yace "Kada ka damu fa, saidai baka fada min sunan jikan nawa ba"

Sarki Abdulbasid yayi murmushin karfin hali yace "Tanimu sunansa"

Yace "a gaida min shi kuma insha Allahu ina nan zuwa nan bada jimawa ba"

Sarki yace "Nagode "

Yana fita jama'arsa suka mike kowa yahau dokinsa suka suka tafi



Bagaren Yarima kuwa yanzu ya kuwa yanzu Nasrin ta saba dashi, duk da cikin da take dashi baisa ta nuna damuwarta ba, kuma zuwa yanzu ita dinma ta saba da jarabarsa. Sannan ya koya mata rashin kunyarsa dan yanzu ko a gaban sarkine, idan taso kwanciya take a jikinsa, Sarauniya taso tayi mata magana amma mai martaba yace "Ta kyale su"


Sarki kuwa yana sane da bukatar Sarki Abdulhamid na cewa Yarima yayi kwana biyu, amma yanzu gashi har satinsa daya bai koma ba, to shima yafi son ya huta ne dan yasa zaifi hutawa anan din saboda matarsa amma acan bazai wani huta ba, kuma gashi cikin sati dayan Yarima ya kara ja, ya ajiye dan tumbi da ka ganshi kasan lafiya tana aiki a jikinsa. 

Yau kuwa da yamma da Yarima da Nasrin suka je fada Sarki ya kalli Yarima yace "Dana Yakamata gobe ka koma mula kada mu batawa Babanka rai"

Yarima ya sunkuyar dakai yace "Ranka ya d'ade d'ade ay idan naje ba abin da zanyi a can "


Sarki yace "Eh na sani shi yasa na barka ka dan jima anan amma ay munyi da Babanka kwana biyu zaka yi anan, kaga duk da haka bamu masa Adalci ba"


Nasrin tace "To nidai gaskiya zan bishi"

Sarki ya dan harareta cikin wasa yace "to ke kuma wa yasa bakinki"

Ta turo baki tana bata rai, Yarima ya jawota jikinsa ya rumgumeta yana jijjigata, kamar jaririya yace "Kinga hanyar ba kyau bai kamata mubi wajen dake ba kina da tsohon cikin nan kinga zaki sha wahala, nima bazan dade ba zan dawo


Ta sake turo baki tace "Wata shida ne fa"

Yace "Wata shida ya tsufa mana "




Bangaren Sarki Abdulbasid kuwa yana zuwa dakinsa ya shiga ya aika a kira masa Sumayya, kusan tare suka zo da dan aiken tana zuwa ya bata wani gigitaccen mari. Saida ya fadi, tana dagowa ya sake bata wani, kuka ta fashe dashi cikin kuka tace "Me na maka "


Ya sake buge bakin yace "Irin rashin kunyar nan na turaki garin Mula"


Gabanta ya fadi yanzu kan tasan asirinta ya tonu mikewa tayi ya jawota yace "zauna nan, "


Ta zauna dabas sai ta kuma tashi kafin ya ankara ta gudu, shiru, bulala ya dauko ya bita har dakinta ya ringa zuba mata tana ihu,

Mamanta ne ta shigo da sauri tace "Kashe min 'yar zakayi sai wani dukan ta kake kamar ka samu jaka"


Tarewa tayi tana cewa wlh baka isa ba, 

tsayawa yayi yana maka kallon mamaki, wai kamar ita a matsayin 'kwa'kwara zata tsaya tana fada masa magana, saboda ta haihu dashi, Lallai rayuwa da 'kwar'kwara batayi ba, fita yayi ya barsu

Mama tace "Me kikayi yake dukanki da danyen jego"

Ta fada mata komai, Mama tace "Nifa sai nayi yadda nayi kin koma gun Yarima "


Sumayya ta turo baki tace "Ni da Yarima yaushe zai kalleni, shi da yake auren 'yar Sarkin Samina"


Mama tace "Ko yar sarkin ina yake aure sai kin koma, ke yadda muke din nan ni ina matsayin 'kwa'kwara idan muka zauna a haka an fada miki muna da matsayi a gidan nan ne, to kinga idan kin koma masa ko ba komai ke sirkar sarkine kuma kishiyar 'yar Sarkin Samina, kuma tanan ma zamu mallake 'yar sarkin tayi ta zuba mana dukiya"


Sumayya tayi murmushi tace "Shi yasa nake sonki mama"

Tace "Dalla can keda idan kin tashi rashin kunyarki kike karewa a kaina"

Tace "to bake ce kike tabo ni ba, kuma kinsan ni bani da hakuri "



Washe gari da sassafe Yarima ya shirya ya dauki Dokin Madu wanda yanzu sun saba dashi sosai Nasrin tana kuka haka ya tafi 


Da yake Jarumin saurayi mai jini a jika ne ga lafiya ga samartaka tuni ya isa garin mula saboda yasha gudu a dokin sosai,


Ummi tana ganin sa ta rumgumeshi, Fada ya fara zuwa Inada 'yan Fada suka ringa gaishe shi, yana zuwa gaban Sarki ya dur'kusa durkusa zai gaishe shi Sarki yace "Nayi fushi da kai Yarima "


Yarima yayi murmushi ya kwantar da kai jikin kujeran da Sarki yake yace "Ay nasan bazaka yi fushi dani ba "


Zama yayi a fadan sai da aka kira azhar ya tashi, bayan yayi sallah ne ya shiga gun Umminsa tana ganinsa tace "Yarima me Nasrin take baka ne haka, kasan yadda kayi kyau kuwa"


Yace "Ummi danki yayi Sa'ar mace. Ba abin dana rasa gunta"


Tace "Sai ka riketa tsakanin ka da Allah ban yadda ko sau daya ka bata mata raiba"


Yayi murmushi yace "Ummi kada ki damu da wannan "


Bayan sun zauna ne take bashi labarin abin daya faru akan Sarauniya Yamuna,

Yarima yace "Amma Ummi banso aka yanke mata wannan hukuncin ba, zan roke Sarki ya maida ita shima kuma Yaya Marwan zan sa a fito dashi haka ay yanzu yayi nadama"


Tace "Eh idan kayi haka kayi Adalci Allah ya maka Albarka"


Saida aka isha ya samu Sarki bayan ya gaishe shi yace "Ranka ya dad'e Ummi take fada min hukuncin da ka yanke akan Sarauniya, akan furucin da tayi a kaina, to ina neman Alfarma Ka maida ta kuma dan uwana, ina son ka bani izini na fito dashi"

Ayi hakuri da wannan ba yawa ✍🏻✍🏻 Nima ana sanyi😂😂


*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 4⃣2⃣

Sarki yace "Magaji, da bakinka kake cewa haka, mutanen da suke son halaka ka"


Yarima yace "Ranka ya dad'e komai ya wuce, kayi hakuri "


Sarki ya bata rai yace "to bazan dawo da ita ba, dan uwanka kuma kaje ka fiddo shi amma ka gada masa idan ya kuma yin wani gigin rashin tarbiyya bazan kyale shi ba"


Yarima yace "Nagode ranka ya dad'e, amma dayar bukatar tawa a duba a gani"

Sarki ya bata rai yace "Magaji idan kazo tayani hira ne muyi hira idan kuma kazo ne ka bata min rai to ka tashi ka tafin gun uwarka"


Ummi da yanzu ta shigo tana bakin kofa tace "Toooo abin kuma harda su zagi, "

Yarima yace "Ay na bar maganar ma "

Sarki ya kalleta yace "Danki baida zuciya "


Ta kalleshi, ta shafa kansa shi kuma kamar dama haka yake so ya kwanto kan kafadarta tace "Dana yana da Zuciya, Saidai yana Koyi ne da Annabi Muhammadu Sallallahu alaihi wa sallam"

Suma suka ka karasa salatin,


Sarki yace "Wai me Nasrin take bashi ne, kinga kibar da yayi kuma ya kara kyau da wani ja dashi"


Ummi tace "Nima na tambaya yace ba abin da ya rasa abunta"


Yarima yace "Hakane Abba komai yimin ake"


Sarki yace "Anan to me ka rasa"


Yarima ya mike yace "Ita mana "


Sarki ya dunkule hannu ya watsa yace "hungo naka, wato acan mu baka rasa mu ba"

Ya dan sosa keya yace "Na rasa mu amma, ita din ay kasan... "


Sai yayi shiru 

Sarrki yace "Nasan me? Karasa mana "


Yarima ya mike yace "Ummi, Ranka ya dad'e ni zan kwana "

Sarki yace "Ja'iri marar kunya"


Baiyi magana ba sai fita yayi da dan gudun sa.


Kwanciya yayi a dagon sai Nasrin ta fado da dan tulun cikinta ta fado  masa, juyi ya ringa yi, da wata munguwar sha'awarta, dan a wannan lokacin ta sabar masa da jikinta 



Haka ta kasance da da Nasrin ma tun da ya tafi ta kasa sukuni, taki fitowa koda Umma taga haka ita ta je sassanta ta zauna gunta tana taya ta hira amma ita Nasrin ma saurarota ma yake ba, ita dai kawai tunanin Yariman tane a ranta, haka da daddare ma ta kasa barci sai juyi take akan gado dan Yarima ya sabar mata da kwanciya a jikinsa. 
Karshe dai ta jawo taje a dauro alola ta gabayar da nafila sannan ta dauki Alku'ani tana karantawa daga bisani ta kwanta akan sallayar barci yayi awon gaba da ita.


Washe gari tunda Sassafe Yarima ya tafin gidan yari, wanda tun zuwan marwan gidan ba wanda yazo duba shi, mullun sai an masa Azaba, kuma ko waje rana da irin azabar da ake masa, gashi wadanda yasa aka kulle bada laifiba, yanzu su suke cin kaniyarsa bayan azabar da ake masa kullum idan ya dawo kuma suma sai sun gana masa, ga sashi aiki da suke, shike share musu daki ya debo musu ruwa, idan ya nuna alamar ya gaji kuwa mari ne hukuncin. Ya dade da yin nadama, shi yasan mahaifiyarsa ce bata bashi tarbiyya ba, da ta bashi da ya samu tarbiya irin wanda dan uwansa ya samu,


Shigar Yarima keda wuya ya samu ana ta jibgar masu laifi, ciki harda Marwan, dama farine jikinsa yayi jajir wani gurin ma jini ke zuba, Yarima ya dakayar dasu ya taka ya isa gun marwan da idonsa ke rufe tsabar wahala, yace "Dan uwana"


Kamar a mafarki Marwan yaji muryan Yarima da sauri ya bude ido yayi ido hudu da Yarima,


Yarima kuwa ya kunce masa daurin da suka masa, Marwan yana ganin an kunce shi ya rumgume Yarima yasa kuka yana cewa "Kanina bansan da wani kalma zan rokeka ka yafe min ba, amma gani gaban ka dan Allah ka yafe min, sannan ka rokar min bayi ma su yafe min, sannan, Sarki ma yana fushi dani dan Allah ka rokar min shi, sannan Umminka tun ina yaro nake daukan alhakinya saboda na samu daurin gindi daga Mahaifiyata..."


Yarima yace "Ya isa to ka bari idan munje ka roke su da kanka, Nidai kada ka damu na yafe maka"


Marwan yace "Ina zamu je"

Yarima yace "Ay nazo ne dama na fito da kai"


Marwan yace "Yarima kaida kanka zaka fito dani "

Wanda tunda yake bai taba kiransa Yarima ba sai yau


Yarima yace "To sai nabarka kayi ta shan wahala"


Kuka ya sake fashewa dashi Yarima ya rumgume shi yana jijjgashi,


Kafin su fito saida Yarima ya bincika wadanda basuda laifi da wadanda dama laifinsu kadan ne kuma da alama sunyi nadama ya fito dasu,

Sassan Marwan suka wuce wanda Yarima yasa bayi suka gyara, Yarima ya hada masa ruwa mai zafi yayi wanka, yana fitowa yasa kayan da Yarima ya fito masa dasu na alfarma, Yarima ya dafa masa madara ya kawo masa ya sha, bayan ya nutsa ne yace "Dan uwa muje ka gaida Sarki kuma ka roke shi ya yafe maka. Saidai kayi hakuri Umma Sarauniya aurenta ya mutu 


Marwan yace "Yarima kaine a gabana, kaine fa wanda na dad'e ina cuta "

Yace "komai ya wuce yanzu tashi muje"

Wannan page din naki ne Ummu Nawwara kiyi yadda kike so dashi ina yinki over💖💖💖


*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 4⃣3⃣

Tare suka fito Yarima na rike da hannunsa suna isa fada kowa yayi tsuru-tsuru ganin Marwan amma shi kuwa sai murmushi yake musu sabanin da da yake daure fuska, gaban Sarki suka tsuguna Yarima yace "Ranka ya dad'e ga dan uwana yazo rokan ka ka yafe masa



Sarki yace "Waya gaya maka ya shiryu "


Yarima ya kalli Marwan shikuwa ya matsa gaban Sarki ya rike kafarsa yasa kuka cikin kuka yace "Abba nasan bani da tarbiya, kuma da sanyaka bakin ciki kala daban daban na cuci talakawanka, kai harma iyalinka. Abba yanzu na shiryu na gane kurena dan Allah ka yafe min "


Yarima ma ya rarrafo yace "Abba kayi hakuri ka yafe masa ya tuba"

Sarki yayi gyaran murya yace "Ni na yafe maka amma talakawana wanda ka cuta fa, dole sai ka roke su sun yafe maka fa"


Yarima yace "Mun gode, suma zamu bisu daya bayan daya indai suna raye"


Sarki yace "To shikenan Allah ya muku Albarka amma fa ku kula fa"


Marwan yace "Mun gode Abba "


Sannan ya juya ya kalli 'yan fada yace "Dan Allah ku yafe min nasan na cutar daku lokacin da me martaba bayanan"

Kowa cewa yake ya yafe, 

Yace "Nagode kuma daga yanzu zan zamo na kwarai "


Ya kalli Yarima yace "Yarima muje gun Ummi"


Yarima yayi murmushi ya mike suka jera sai sassan Ummi, 


Ummi tasan da za'a fito da Marwan saidai bata san zata gansu tare da Yarima ba kwatsan sai ta gansu tare, suna isa gabanta Marwan ya durk'usa kasa Yace "Ummi tun ina karami nake cutar dake ba irin rashin da'an da ban miki ba amma baki taba mayar min ba, Ummi ki yafe min dan Allah badan ni ba wlh nayi nadama"


Ummi ta shafa kan sa tace "Ba komai kada ka damu na yafe maka, komai ya wuce"


Hira suka dan taba wanda Yarima da Ummi ne masu maganan Marwan kuwa yayi shiru yana tunanin irin kyan hali irin na Yarima da Umminsa, 


Haka rayuwa ta kasance, Inda tsakanin Marwan da Yarima ko wanne so yake ya kyautatawa dan uwan sa, Saida aka kwana biyu Yarima ya ba Marwan shawara yace "Yakamata suje su duba Ummansa tunda ya fito yau kwanansa hudu da fitowa kuma bai je ba "


Tare suka tafi har gidan su Umman, a wani daki kamar akurkin kaji suka sameta, kuma ga tagar a wangale ba komai a dakin sai wata tsohuwar tabarma, bangon dakin ma dun ya farfashe ta gefe daya ma kamar zai fadi, suna shiga Ta taso ta rumgume Marwan tana murnan ganin sa, tana ganin Yarima ta daure fuska tace "Kai kuma me idon mayu me kazo min nan"


Marwan ya sassauta murmushinsa yace "Kai Umma dan uwan nawa"


Tace "ohhh wato kaima sun asirce ka kamar yadda suka asirce babanka"


Yarima juyawa yayi ya fita, Yayinda Marwan ma ya fizge hannunsa da ta rike yabi bayan Yarima suna fita waje Marwan ya dafa kafadarsa yace "Kanina kayi hakuri "


Yarima yayi murmushi yace "kada ka damu fa kaje ku gana ina jiranka anan, "


Marwan yace "Aa muje gida kawai"


Yarima yace "Kada ka manta mahaifiyace kaje ku gana a hankali zata dawo hanya mu ci gaba da mata Addua,"


Marwan zaiyi magana, Yarima ya katseshi yace "Kaje kawai"


Ya koma badan ransa yaso ba, saidon farin cikin Yarima yana shiga yace "Haba Umma yakamata ace mun ajiye makaman mu tunda wadanda muke dasu ma, su da Alheri suke bin mu"


Ta kalleshi tace "sannu shanyeyye, to bari kaji ni bazan taba yada makamina ba sai ka hau kujeran mahaifinka,"


Marwan yace "Umma ki daina maganan kujera tsakanina da Amar duk wanda aka ba shi duk daya ne "


Ta harare shi tace "Dalla ja can wawan banza da wofi wanda baisan ciwon kansa ba, sarautar taka zaka ba wani to Wlh baka isa ba dole ka gaji mahaifin ka"


Marwan yace "Umma ki daina maganan nan mana, "


Tace" Bazan daina ba kuma daga yanzu kada ka sake zuwa min da wannan bushashen kinfin "


Baice komai ba ya fita tana kiransa ko ya kulata. A waje ya sami Yarima suka tafi, suna zuwa gida suka samu Madu yazo Yarima yayi murna da ganin sa, Yarima yana zuwa ya rumgume shi yace "Shine sai yau kazo ko"


Madu yace "Ayi hakuri ranka ya dad'e muna daji ne shi yasa"


Tare suka shiga dashi ya gaida Sarki sannan Yarima ya hada masa sha tara ta arziki sannan ya tafi da niyyar zai sake kawo masa ziyara


Yarima kuwa tunda yaga inda Sarauniya Yamuna take zaune ya tausaya mata, matar da take gidan sarauta amma yau sai ga inda take zaune, dan haka ne ya samu Sarki ya sanar dashi komai sannan yace "Ranka ya dad'e ina son kaji tausayinta kodan saboda danta a dawo da ita cikin masarautar nan koda baza ka mayar da auren ku ba"



Sarki yace "To Yarima zan dawo da ita gidan nan amma aurena da ita ya kare, saboda haka kuje ku dawo da ita kaida dan uwanka"


Koda Yarima ya samu Marwan ya fada masa yadda suka yi da sarki sannan yace "Kai zaka je ka dawo da ita"


Marwan yaji dadin hakan har yake tunanin idan ya fada mata Yarima ne sillar komai zata yada makamanta.

Wannan page din nakine  Aunty, kin kasance masoyiyata kuma masoyiyar Izzar mulki, dan haka kiyi yadda kike so dashi, Love you lodi-lodi💋💋💋💋

Page 4⃣4⃣

Koda yaje ya fada mata komai rufeshi tayi da fada tace "eh mana saboda shi za'a ba sarauta shi yasa zai ce haka to ni ban yadda ba, muje gidan wlh sai saisun gane kurensu daga har uwarsa kuma wlh sai sarki ya maida aurena dashi"


Marwan yace "Umma indai zaki tada hankali ne, gaskiya gwara ki zauna nan din"


Ta kalleshi cikin ranta tace "lalle Aisha ta sanyeka to kuwa dani take zanjen "


A fili kuwa cewa tayi "muje to zanyi yadda ka ce "


Yayi murmushi yace "yawwa Umma na ko kefa, har naji dadi kuwa"


Haka rayuwa ta kasance Ko ina zasu tare suke zo koda masallaci, yanzu kwana ma tare suke yi a sassan Marwan kafin kace me Marwan yayi kyau, kyansa ya fito har idan ya kalli kansa a madubi yace "Ashe idan kai ba mutumin kirki bane kana yin muni, ni yanzu kalli yadda na zama"


Yarima yace "Haka nemana dn uwa"


Ummi da Sarki kuwa sunfi kowa farin ciki ganin kan 'ya'yan nasu a hade, Yamuna kuwa tana can bakin ciki ya isheta, tana niman yadda zatayi Sarki ya maida ita kafin tasan abin yi"


Nasrin kusa yakan je wajen ta yayi kwana daya ya koma, saidai bai taba zuwa da Marwan ba shima kuma bai taba bata labarin Marwan ba


Satin sa biyu bai je ba, saboda Sarki yana koya masa yadda zai tafiyar da mulki cikin sauki, da sassafe yana tashi ya fara shiri Marwan ya mike yace "Dan uwa ina zaka je ne"


Yace "yau dai kaima ka shirya kazo muje Samina zanje, gun Nasrin "


Marwan yayi murmushi yace "To muje"


Nasrin kuwa Dama yau tun da ta tashi take nakuda a tsatstsaye bata fita ba tayi zamanta a sassanta, Umma kuwa taga shirun yayi yawa dan haka ta shiga duba ta, ta ganta a falo kwance inda tayi sallah kan sallaya tana cije lebe"


Umma tace "Nasrin Lafiya"

Tace "yau runda na tashi naji marata tana min ciwo "


Umma tace "To ko haihuwarce tazo"

Nasrin zatayi magana sai ta kasa sakamakon wani ciwo da taji, Da sauri ta kama hannun Umma, Umma kuwa da taga haka cewa tayi "Haihuwa ne tsaya in kawo miki maganin nakuda"


Da sauri ta fita bata dade basai gashi tazo mata da magani, ta bata tasha, tana sha taji kamar an hautsina mata cikin ta sai amai, 


Yarima kuwa tun a hanya jikinsa ya bashi Nasrin tana wani hali koda suka  shigo masarautar basu wuce ko inaba sai sassanta, suna zuwa suka ga Nasrin tana ta kwarara Amai, 

Da sauri Yarima yaje ya kamata yana cewa "Umma lafiya"


Ita kuwa Nasrin tana ganin Marwan tsoro ya kamata sai ta yi zubur ta mike tana nuna shi da yatsa yayin da wani sabon nakudar ta taso, Ta durkushe tace cikin iya karfin ta tare da nishi "Yarima Marwan ya biyo ka"

Shi kuwa Marwan yana ganin tana nakuda ya juya ya koma waje


Umma tace "kiyi Salati"

Ta sake bude baki tare da rike hannun Yarima da hannunta bibbiyu yayi Salati hade da wani babban nishi saiga yaro Namiji ya fito, Umma ce   taa amshe shi, shiko Yarima ya rumgume ta sai sannun yake mata, Umma ce ta ta gyara jaririn ta sa masa kaya sannan mika wa Yarima tace "Yarima gashi, sai kudan fita waje in gyarata"


Yace "Umma kije dashi zan gyarata ni"


Umma ta kalleshi tace "Yarima bafa gyaran da kake nufi bane bazaka iya ba"


Ya kalli fuskar Nasrin yace "Umma Nasrin tana bukatata kusa da ita kuma kinga tasha wahala ki tafi dashi kawai zan gyarata "


Umma ta bata rai tace "Yarima ina wasa da kaine, dan kaga na bari ta haihu a gabanka ma, "

Da sauri ya amsa yace "Yi hakuri Umma"


Fitowa yayi da jaririn a hannusa ya mikawa Marwan Ya amsa yana murmushi yace "Kaga muma mun samu Magajin Mula"


Yarima zaiyi magana kenan sai ya jiyo muryar wata baiwa tana cewa "Ahhh Yarima Gimbiya Nasrin ce ta haihu"


Yace "Eh"


Tace "barin je in sanar da Sarki ko zan samu 'yanci"


Da gudunta ta ruga yayin da Marwan kuma ya shagala da kallon ta, har ta bace sannan ya dawo da kallon sa kan fuskar jaririn, 

Yarima yace "Yayana yaya dai, kodai kana son ta zama yayata ce"


Murmushi Marwan yayi yace "Kusan haka"


Yarima yace "Da nafi kowa farin ciki, ga samun da, ga kuma Yayana ya samu mata"



Ito tana zuwa fada tayi sa'a ba wanda ya riga ta ta durk'usa taba maida numfashi tace "Ranka ya dad'e sarki Gimbiya Nasrin ta sauka lafiya"


Ay kuwa sarki yace "Me kike son in baki na wannan Albishir da kika min "


Sunkuyar da kai tayi batace komai ba, 

Sarki yace "To kije na 'yanta ki"

Ta dago tace "Ka 'yanta ni, " ay kuwa tamike ta fita da gudu ta koma gun Yarima tace "Yarima kaga Sarki ya 'yanta ni"


Marwan yace "Masha Allah mun gode Allah nima zanso ki nuna min inda kike in kawo miki ziyara"


Ta bude bakinta sannan ta toshe da tafin hannunta tace "Na isa kai ba Yayan Yarima bane, rufa min asiri kada kasa Sarki yace y fasa 'yantani"


Tana gama fada ta gudu, Marwan yace "Ta tafi fa"


Yarima yace "kada ka damu nasan inda take da iyayenta"


Kafin kace me duk masarautar sunji haihuwa Gimbiya Nasrin Sarauniya Bilkisu kuwa tana zuwa da taga su Yarima cewa tayi "dama kasan da haihuwarne kazo"


Yace "Umman Sultan nima kawai nazo ne na tarar da hakan "(da yake dama Haka yaji Nasrin da Anisa suke kiranta harma da Sultan din)


Ummi kuwa tana gama gyarataa bayan tayi mata wanka Amshe ta mika mata ta bashi nono sannan ta gyara falon bayan ya koshi Umma ta amsa ta sake mikawa Su Yarima, kowa ya shigo ana sam barka da jariri me kama da Yarima, 


Sarki kuwa kyauta yayi tayi da 'yanta bayi, haka ya aika garin mula sannan yayi ta aikawa garuruwa ana fada ya samu jika.


Yarima kuwa koda suka mika wa Sarauniya Jaririn tafiya yayi gun Nasrin dan an kaita sasan Sarauniya dan nan din anyi yawa, kuma baza a barta ta huta ba


Suna shiga tare Nasrin ta mike Yarima kuwa ya fahimci abin da yasa yayi murmushi ya kama hannunta yace "Saboda kinga Yayana ko, kada ki damu yanzu mun zama daya dashi"


Murmushi tayi itama ta koma ta zauna shima zama yayi tare da rumgume ta ya sata ta jikinsa


Shima Marwan murmushi yayi ya zauna suka gaisa, bayan dan minti kadan ne Marwan yace "Ina zuwa dan Uwa"


Ya fita yana fita kuwa ya ga Wannan baiwar nan yayi sauri ya bita amma ita ma da taji ana binta a kara da gudu abinta,


Yarima kuwa yana ganin Marwan ya ya kalli  Nasrin yace " kina mamakim ganin Dan uwa ko"


Tace "Eh wlh "


Ay kuwa ya bata labarin komai sannan ya fada mata yadda sukayi da yaga baiwar nan, koda Yarima yayi wa Nasrin kwatanceta tace ta gane ta, bata da matsala, 


Can tace "Amma ya taba zuwa ya duba Sumayya da danta kuwa"


Yarima yace Aa"


Ta mike tace "Ay kuwa ya kamata aje a duba ta kada Allah ya tambayeku ranar gobe kiyama"


Yarima yace "Kinga ban taba wannan tunanin ba, kuma shima tunda aka fada masa bai sake maganan ba"


Tace "Kai yakamata ka masa maganan yaje yaga dansa,"


Yarima yace "bari a gama hidimar suna sai muje in raka shi"


Nasrin ta mike daga jikinsa tace "Kuma wlh idan kunje kada ka bari ku hadu da ita"


Murmushi yayi yace "Kina kishin mijinki ne"


Tace "Ina son abuna dole nayi kishinsa "


Bayan mutane sun ta ganin sa, aka maida jaririn gun Uwarsa lokacin Ummi da Sarki Abdulhamid sunzo, 


Da yamma kuwa Yarima da Marwan suka nufi gun baiwa, suna zuwa Mahaifinta suka samu, Yarima bai boye masa ba ya fada masa bukatar, shikuwa da yaji dan uwan Yarima ne kuma yasan mutumin kirki ne yace ya bashi Yusra, kuma zai turo musu ita


Marwan kuwa yanajin an ambaci sunanta ya lumshe ido yace "suna me dadi"

Maman Najib na baki page din nan kyauta kiyi yadda kike so dashi, ina kaunarki over



*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 4⃣5⃣

Suna zaune ta fito tana hada ido dasu ta rufe fuskarta Yarima yace "Yusra ba gaisuwa "


Ta durk'usa cikin jin kinya ta gaishe su, Yarima ya tashi yace "Yayana, Idan ka gama ina sassan Umman Sultan "

Yana zuwa ya samu Nasrin tayi barci da yaronta a gaban ta, daukan sa yayi ya ringa jero masa Addu'o'i daga bisani ya ajiye shi ya kwanta a gefen su, har barci ya fara diban sa yaji muryan Ummi tana cewa "Yarima me yasa kai a rayuwarka baka da kunya ne ko kadan, nan fa gidan sirkanka ne amma sam baka gudun abin magana"


Ya tashi ya zauna yace "Ummi me abin kunya naga matata ce"


Hararansa tayi tace "tashi ka fita kada na sake ganin ka shigo har cikin daki, iyakacin ka falo"


Bada son ransa ba ya tashi ya fita, ya samu Marwan yana jiransa, Murmushi yayi yace "Ya kuka kare da Yayata"

Marwan yace "Ay ta amince yanzu jira nake a gama hidiman suna sai in tada maganan agun Sarki "


Yarima ya fada masa yadda sukayi da Nasrin akan zuwa ganin Tanimu yaron gimbiya


Marwan yace "Wlh ni idan na tuna abin kunya yake bani"


Yarima yace "Yaya za'a yi to haka Allah ya rubuta mu dauke shi a matsayi kaddara"


Shiru Marwan yayi yana dana sani da nadamar abin da yayi a baya, ga kunyar Yarima da yake addabarsa. 


Washe gari Ummi, Sarki, da Marwan suka koma shi kuwa Yarima kin komawa yayi yace sai anyi suna, tunda Sarki dama yace a bari tayi arba'in sannan ta koma dakin ta, da Yarima yaso yayi magana amma Ummi ta nuna masa hakan ba daidai bane kasancewar ma basu rayu da ita ba sai wannan lokacin, yakamata ya kyale su, ko ba komai Nasrin zata shaku da danginta, haka ya hakura badan ransa yaso ba,


Nasrin kuwa taga kauna, dangi Sarauniya Bilkisu ma sunzo, kullum a cikin baki suke, Yarima kuwa suna ne yana zaune a masarautar amma a rana baifi yaganta sau daya dan kullun gidan yana cike, ba daman yaje inda take, haka ya hakura, Ranan suna kuwa, yaro yaci Sunan Sahabi (Mahaifin Sultan) dan Yarima da kansa ya kaiwa Sarki Abdurrahman yace "Ranka ya dad'e ka sa masa suna, "

Sarki yace "To ka fadi wani suna za'a sa masa"


Yarima yace "duk sunan da kasa masa ina maraba dashi"


Sarki kuwa ya masa huduba da sunan dan uwansa Sahabi, sannan yace "sai kuna kiransa da Mufid,, wato Mai Amfani kenan, dan so muke ya amfani Al'umma,"



shanu aka yanka sai raguna, ranan duk bawan dake gidan sunzo suna, Sarakuna da masu milki, harda masu hannu da shuni na kasan nan, wasu ma daga wani kasa suka zo,


Da yamma aka yi wasan doki, Marwan, Amar da Sultan duk sun hau doki, ranan Yusra ma tayi kyau har ta gaji, dan tun ranan da Nasrin ta samu labarin Marwan yana sonta take janta a jiki dan su saba, kuma take fada mata yadda gidan sarauta yake saboda ta sani kafin ta shiga ciki, 


Su Maman Sayyid, Mahmah Zee, Queen Bilkisu, Aunty, Maman Ruky, Maman Aj, Anty Salma, sunyi group suna cin abinci kowa cikin farin ciki take


Can gefe kuma kidan kwarya ake Nan kuma, Fadimatu, Maryam din Ameer,Raumanatu, Rim, Tababa, Tanzy baby, Maman haidar, Sadidin Baba, Ummu Nawwar da dai sauran su suna ta dikan rawa,


Ina tafe kuwa na hango Mrs Basakwkwace Kwaila tana gefe tana dan takawa a hankali wai ita tana rawa😛


Haka taron sunan ya watse cikin farin ciki da annashuwa,

Washe gari su Ummi suka yi shirin komawa dama Sarki tun a jiya ya koma, Yarima kuwa zaida Ummi tayi da gaske ya yadda ya bisu da niyyar yana nan dawowa gani iyalinsa, 

Kafin su tafi saida  Marwan yaje yaga Yusra wanda cikin kankanin lokaci Soyayya ta shiga tsakanin su  har yake jin bazai iya rabuwa da ita ba.


Suna isa gida Marwar gun Ummansa yayi yana zuwa ya durk'usa yace "Umma sannunki da gida"


Ta kalleshi ta watsar tace "To shanyeyye wato ni ban isa dakaiba ko, nayi maka maganar kada kaje musu suna Amma saboda baka da zuciya saida kaje, ay naji wata labari wai ka samu mata a can, to wlh ban yadda da auren nan ba, Kuma sai kahau kujeran mahaifinka kuma ka auri 'yar Sarauta kamar yadda wancan matsiyacin ya aura"

Marwan ya ce "Haba Umma ki daina jifan Yarima da mungayen maganganu, shida Ummi wlh so suke ki dawo kan hanya, kuma kina dawowa wlh Mai martaba zai mayar da auren ku dashi"


Ta kalleshi shekeke tace "Ohhh so kake In bar musu sarautar to wlh basu isa ba"


Yace "Ina dan ni kike wannan abin, To ni nace bana son sarautar kuma nabarwa dan uwana"

Tace "Kai kasan wannan amma tabbas sai na dawo dakai kan hanya kuma sai ka karbi sarautar mahafinka"


Mikewa yayi cikin ransa yace "Umma ta kasa ganewa, Allah ya shiyeki"


Ya tafi gun Ummi nan sukayi ta yin hiransu daga bisani kuma suka tafi gun kwanciyar su.


Saida akayi sati da suna Suka shirya tafiya gauda dan gani Tanimu da uwarsa, Yarima kuwa ya kafin lokacin yaje Samina sau uku kenan amma baya kwana a ranan yake dawowa


Ana azhar suka isa saida sukayi Sallah sannan suka isa masarautar, saida suka gaishe da Sarki sannan Yarima yayi masa bayanin abin da ya kawosu,

Sarki yace "Waye Uban Tanima "


Marwan ya sunkuyar da kai


Sarki ya sake daka musu tsawa "Nace waye Uban Yarima"


Marwan ya dago yace "Nine"

Wannan page din naki ne Maryam Gani, kiyi yadda kike so dashi mallakin kine love you

Page 4⃣6⃣

Sarki ya kalle shi yace "Wato kai kayiwa 'yata ciki ko, kuma saboda mugunta sai yanzu ka kwaso kafa kamin me "



Yarima yace "Ranka ya dad'e kayi hakuri shima baya nan ne "

Sarki ya nuna Yarima da yatsa yace "yimin shiru kaima zanzo ta kanka ay"


Ya juya ya kalli Marwan yace "zan baka danka yanzu ku tafi dashi dan 'yata bazata ci gaba sa shayar da shege ba"


Duk kansu zunji zafin abin amma suka daure Yarima yace "Kayi hakuri Taci gaba da shayar dashi zamu na zuwa muna duba su "

Ya kalleshi yace "Duk kai ka jawo, da kayi ciwon hauka, me yasa baka sakar min 'yata ta dawoba, kasa dan uwanka ya lalata min ita"


Yarima yace "Abbana saida ya tambaye ta ko a raba auren mu amma taki tace tana son ta zauna a haka, kuma ay ita ta sani a halin da na shiga, kuma maganan cikin da dan uwana ya mata ay ba laifinsa bane shi daya, harda laifinta dan da bata bashi hadin kaiba da bazai yi mata komaiba"


Sarki yace "Koma menene Yau zaku tafi da danku"


Yarima yace "Shikenan A bamu zamu tafi dashi din"


Sarki ya mike ya fita can saiga shi ya dauko Tanimu da hannu daya yana kuka, Sumayya ta biyo shi tana kuka itama, yana isowa Yarima yayi saurin tashi ya amshe shi, Sumayya tace "Ina zaku kaimin yarona ku barmin shi ina sonsa"


Sarki yace "Bazaki ci gaba da shayar da shege ba"


Yarima yace "Ranka ya dad'e, To kayi mana Alfarma Dan uwana ya auri Sumayya saisu ci gaba da rainon dansu"


 Sarki Yayi shiru can ya kalli Sumayya yace "Ya kika ce"


Yarima ta kalla tace "Ni bazan iya rayuwa da wani da namiji ba in ba kaiba"


Yarima ya mata kallon rainin wayo,

Sarki yace "auren Marwan shine zai baki daman rike danki"

Tace "Ni dan uwansa nake so, me zanyi da Marwan wanda. Bazai samu sarautar mahaifinsa ba, Yarima kuwa shine da sarauta saboda haka Abba ka sashi ya maida auren mu"


Sarki kuwa kunyar maganarta yaji, wato ma ita me sarauta take so, kasa magana yayi yayin da Yarima yace "Ashe nima badan Allah kike sona ba, saboda haka zamu dauki Tanimu mu tafi dashi, zamu na bashi madara ke kuma muna muki fatan Alheri in kin samu miji ki aure"


Sumayya ta saka ihu sarki kuwa kallonsu kawai yake yi, Yarima ne yace "Ranka ya dad'e mun tafi"


Yace "To ku gaida babanku"


Yarima ma ne ya rumgume tanimu Suka tafi, sai magrib suka isa, Gun Ummi suka ajiye Tanimu suka nufi masallaci, Ummi kuwa Da taga alamun yana jin yunwa ta bashi madara koda yasha ta sashi a baya tana jijjiga shi barci ya kwashi shi


Bayan sun dawo ne tace "Ya na ganku da yaron kuma "


Yarima ne ya fada mata komai Ummi tace "To Tanimu Allah ya raya mana kai amma abin da nake so dakai marwan ka dauke shi ka kaishi a Umman ka ka fada mata komai, kada muzo muyi laifi ko"


Marwan yace "Ay baza ta amshe shi ba"


Ummi yace "idan taki amsa ka dawo min dashi nan"


Yace "To"


Ya rumgume shi ya tafi ya sameta ya fada mata komai yana yin shiru tace "To kuma yanzu ka kawo min shi in masa me"


Ya ce "Na kawo shi ne ki rike shi"


Tace "Wlh bazan rike dan shege ba, ka dauke shi ka kaiwa Aisha tunda yanzu itace uwarka"

Yace "Umma dan Allah kiyi hakuri ki rike min shi"


Tace "Ka fita min dashi anan idan ba haka na duk abin dana samu zan doka masa akai"


Yasan halinta sarai ba imani gareta ba dan zata iya doka masa din, ya mike ya koma gun Ummi dashi ya fada mata komai 

Ummi tace "Kaga yanzu mun fita, ay baza ta ce ba a kai mata ba"


Yace "hakane, Ummi Nagode Allah ya saka da Alheri Allah ya biya ki da gidan Aljannah"


Tace "Amin, amma kuma ba Godiya a tsakanin mu da kai, tunda kai dana ne komai na maka, ban fadi ba kuma ba Godiya a tsanina nakai"


Kukan da yake boyewane ya fito, ya fashe da kuka cikin kuka yace "Ummi ki yafe min dan Allah na cutar daku "


Ummi ta bata rai tace "ba mun wuce wannan babin ba"

Yarima yazo ya daga shi a gurin suka fita


Haka rayuwa ta kasance Ummi ke kula da Tanimu ita kuwa Yamuna bata kara tambayan ina yaron yake ba, shidai Marwan yana zuwa yana gaishe ta 


Nasrin kuwa tana nan tana ci gaba da shayar da Mufid, yayin da Baban sa kullum Yarima saiya ziyarce su, 


Cikin haka ne Sarki Abdulhamid yasa ranan Nadin sarautar Yarima Amar lokacin nadin ya kama kwanan Nasrin talatin da biyar da haihuwa dan haka tana kwana talatin Sarki yace "Ta shirya ta koma ayi komai tana can"

  🌐 IZZAR MULKI
           💓💓💓💓

Story 
        &
          Written  by
UMMU MUFEED
      (Mrs Aliyu)


Wannan page din nakine Maman Aj Kiyi yadda kike so dashi


*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 4⃣7⃣

Amar ya gyara musu sassan su aka sake yanayin gurin ya zama sabo 


Ranan kuwa da zata dawo da 'yan rakiyanta, Sa'adatu da 'yan biyunta sai Umma, Sultan da Anisa, 


Suna isa masarautar Nasrin ta ringa kallon gidan yadda ya janja mata, da ta shigo a matsayin baiwa, yanzu ko gata a matsayin matar Yarima,


Suna kaita daki basu wani jima ba suka koma a ranan suka ce zasu dawo ranan nadin sarauta.


Ummi ta musu tsaraba suka tafi suna farin ciki.

Bayan sun tafine Yarima da Marwan suka shigo Marwan ya dauki Mufid da yake gefe yana masa wasa, Yarima kuwa gefen Nasrin yaje ya zauna ya sakayo hannunsa a kugunta yace a hankali "Nayi kewarki fa"


Marwan ya kalleshi ya harare shi yace "Kaifa baka kunya ko kadan a gabana kuma ina yayanka"

Yarima yayi murmushi yace "Kaima ban hana ka ba idan Yaya Yusra tazo"


Nasrin kuwa kunya ce ta kamata, shiko Marwan da yaga alamar haka ya sake rumgume Mufid yace "Yarona mu tafi mubar wannan marar kunyan baban naka"


Suka fita dashi shiko Yarima kamar abin da yake jira kenan ya fara wasanni da ita, tuni ta biye masa, daga bisani kuwa ya dauketa cak yayi dakin kwanansu da ita, a gado ya dire ta, yaci gaba da sarrafata, yayin da itama tabiye masa wannan shi zai gwada maka lalle ba karamin kewar junansu sukayi ba.


Marwan kuwa gun Ummi yayi da Mufid, ya samu Tana bawa Tanimu Madara, yace "Ga dan uwanka na kawo maka"


Dariya Tanimu yayi Ummi tace "Ni ka kyale min yaro yana cin abinci zaka dame shi"


Ya koma gefe rumgume da Mufid yace "An barku Ummi"


Tana gama bashi ta shafa masa baya yayi gyatsa sannan ta goye shi tuni barci ya kwashe shi. 



Saida suka sami nutsuwa suka yi wanka a tare bayan sun fito ne, Nasrin tayi gun Ummi ta samu lokacin ta sauke Tanimu tana rike da Mufid,

Ta tsuguna ta gaishe ta ta amsa sannan tace "Dama yanzu nake shirin ince yakai miki shi kada a kure shi yayi kuka, tunda yayi hakuri ay"


Nasrin yayi murmushi tasa hannu ta dauke shi a gefen Marwan ta kalli Tanimu tace "Ummi ina kika samo mana yaro "


Ummi tace "Wlh yaron da Sumayya ta haifa ne, wai sun je su ganshi baban ya ce bazai 'yarsa baza ta rike ba"


Kunya ce ta kama Marwan ya mike yace "Ummi barin je fada, Maman Mufid idan Yarima ya fito kice ya same ni a fada"


Tace "To",


Bayan tafiyansa ne Ummi ta fadawa Nasrin komai, 


Nasrin tace "Ay ban manta da kashe Basma da tayi ba, Ummi wlh saina mata hukunci"


Ummi tace "Wacece Basma"


Nasrin ta fada mata komai sannan tace "Ummi Basma ita ta fara nuna min so, har yanzu ina jin ciwon mutuwar Basma, bazan taba bari in rasata a banza ba, Zan bari a gama wannan bikin tukun sannan in fara zartar da nawa hukuncin"


Ummi tace "To Allah dai ya kyauta"


Ranan Nadin sarautar kuwa tun asuba da Yarima ya dawo masallaci yayi wanka ya fita, yayin da Nasrin ma ta gyara kanta da yaronta, da 'yan biyun Sa'adatu da tun jiya suka zo, Sultan da Anisa ma anan suka kwana, suma tun da wuri sukayi kwalliyarsu, gwanin ban sha'awa. 


Kafin kace me, Masarautar Mula ta cika da sarakuna daga manyan garuruwa kai harma da kananan, 


Yarima kuwa ya boye a daki Marwan sai kuka yake, shi kuwa Marwan yana ta neman sa dan baisan inda yayi ba, Ya shiga cikin dakinsa dan yayi wanka sai ganin Yarima yayi yana hawaye


Da sauri Marwan yaje ya dafa shi yace "Kanina lafiya kuwa"


Yarima ya dago da jajayen idonsa yace "Bana son sarautar nan, Sarki ya matsa sai na amsa, ina tsoron ranan da Allah zai tambaye ni, yaua nayi da talakawa ba"


Marwan yayi murmushi yace "wannan ya nuna dan uwana zaiyi Adalci, ka kwantar da hankalin ka, ni ina ji a jikina ta sanadin wannan sarauta zaka shiga Aljanna"


Shiru Yarima yayi yana sauraronsa 


Marwan ya kara da cewa "ka tashi kaje ka gaida mutanen Samina tun dazu suka zo suna ta tambayarka nima barin yi wanka in fito"


Ya tashi ya wanke fuskarsa ya sake gyarawa sannan ya fita, sassansu ya shiga inda yayi ta gaisawa da mutane ana kara masa murna


Masarautar Mula ba masaka tsinke a ciki, a wannan lokacin ne Yarima ke tsugune a tsakiyar fada ana kokarin nada shi, yayin da masu kalangu suna yi masu algeta ma nayi, Maroka kuwa suma sai yin nasu suke yi,


Cikin dubban mutane aka nada Yarima Amar A matsayin Sarkin mula yayin da fadawa suka daga babban rigarsu suka tare shi ya zauna a kan kujeran sarauta


Tsohon Sarki Abdulhamid shine yazo ya fara gaisuwa sannan Marwan ya zo shima yayi nasa



Yamuna kuwa tana daki batasan wainar da ake toyawa ba, sai ji tayi kamar ana algaita kuma san kira na magana, 


Ta tashi ta fito bakin kofa sai taga gidan a cike yake, bata ankara ba sai taji sankira yana cewa "Allah ya kare Matashin sarki, Maigidan Gimbiyar Samina, Uban Sahabi yau ka tashi a Yarima ka zama sarki"



Fitowa tayi da sauri ta samu wata baiwa tace "Ke me ake anan haka "


Da yake sabuwar zuwa ce bata san Yamuna ba, sai tayi murmushi tace "An nada Yarima A matsayin sarki ne"


Mari ta bata tayi cikin mutane da gudu tana ihu, cikin maza ta wuce tana cewa "Wlh karya ne, dana ne sarki ba Amar ba "


Marwan ne ya hangota, yaje ya kamota tana ihun tana wasu magangan "Wallahi ya sauka akan kujeran ya baka ay kujeran ka ne"


Sai kuma ta fashe da dariya ta cire dan kwalinta tana kokarin yin tsirara..

  🌐 IZZAR MULKI

Story 
        &
          Written  by
UMMU MUFEED

Wannan Page din naki ne Mrs Usman kiyi yadda kike so dashi ina yinki over💗💗💗


*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 4⃣8⃣

Dakyar Marwan ya iya janta saida Ummi da Sarauniya Bilkisu suka zo suka taya shi suka kaita dakinta sai buge buge take tana cewa "Wlh sai ka bashi kujeran sa ay ba naka bane"


Marwan durk'usa wa yayi yana kuka yana cewa "Umma me yasa kika tsani dan uwana, Umma kinga abin da nake fada miki ko, baki mana adalci ba"


Ita kuwa yaga kayan jikinta take, Ummi da sarauniya da suka ga baza su iya ba, suka sake ta, ta fara wurgi da kayan dakin tana jifan mutanen da suke kanta, 


Marwan yace "Ummi ku fita kawai kada ta ji muku ciwo "


Ummi tace "Kai fa Marwan"


Yace "Kada ki damu dani ummi ku ku fita kawai"


Suna fita ya kamata ya hadata da jikin gado ya daure ta, ya juya zai fita sai yaji tausayin ta ya kama shi, sai ya dawo ya kunce ta, ay ko yana kunceta ta ci gaba da wurgi da kayan dakin, fito wa yayi ya kulle dakin zuciya tana masa zogi


Yarima kuwa hawaye yake yayin da ake ta zuwa gaishe shi, bayan an nutsu ne, ana jiran Sabon Sarki yayi magana, 

Saida yayi gyaran murya yace murya na rawa "Innalillahi wa inna ilaihir rajun, bai kamata kuna min murna ba, saboda *Mulki* abune mai girma wanda idan nayi wasa a ciki zan iya fadawa halaka ranan gobe kiyama, kamata yayi kumin Addu'a Allah yasa na iya yin Adalci, " haka yaci gada da jawabi yana hawaye karshe yace " a yanzu kuma ina son in nada Sarauniyata wacce zata tayani gudanar da Adalci a cikin Mulkina, Gimbiya Nasrin Bismillah"


Nasrin ta yako a hankali tazo tsakiyar fada, maroka nayi nasu, Masu algaita nayi Mata kuma na guda. Durk'usawa tayi masu mata soka zo da Alkebba suka sa mata, idaga ido tayi ta hango fuskar mai gidanta, Sabon sarki dake zubar da hawaye itama ji tayi hawayen na zuba, tasowa yayi ya iso gunta yasa hannu yana share mata hawayen, daga bisani ya kamo hannunta ta mike ya zaunar da ita a kujeran gefen sa, nan guri ya hargitse da guda, karshe dai itama jawabin tayi daga karshe tace " Ina son aje a dauko min Gimbiyar gauda 'yar Sarki Abdulbasid, ina son zan mata hukuncin kashe min kawata, masoyiyata Basma "


Sarki Abdulbasid yaji gaban sa ya fadi, yasan dai Basman da Sarauniya Nasrin take nufi amma kuma baisan da maganan Sumayya ne ta kashe taba,


Nasrin kuwa koda taga Alamunsa tace "Ina mai bawa Sarki Abdulbasid hakuri akan hukuncin da zan yanke, nasan baka san cewa Sumayya itace ta kashe min Basma ba"


Kowa shiru yayi ya zubo wa Nasrin ido, Harda sabon Sarki dan shima baisan da wannan zancen ba, itako da taga haka, ta fara bada labari a fada, kowa sai salati yake, Sarki Abdulbasid yace "Ranki ya dad'e bansan da wannan zancen ba da na sani da na yanke mata hukuncin kisa, saboda nima mutuwar Basma ta taba ni a zuciya, amma duk da haka, idan ba hukuncin kisa kika dibar mata ba, idan kin gama mata hukuncin ko min dad'ewa  zanyi nawa nima dan jinin 'yata baza ta tafi a banza ba"



Tace "Ba hukuncin kisa na daukar mata ba, kuma kaima ina rokonka kada ka kasheta, ina rokonka ka bari taci gaba da rainon yaronta a hannunta, bayan na mata hukunci zan turota ta dawo gida, kada ka mata komai,"


Yace "Shikenan Ranki ya dad'e "


Haka taro ya watse kowa ya koma inda yake, Bakin Samina mata kuwa sassan Ummi sukayi dan su kwana, dan dan Sarauniya cewa tayi bazata kwana sassan Nasrin ba taje taga abin da yafi karfin ta. 


Andan jima da yin isha'i amma ba aga Umma ba, Ummi ta ce "Bari ta duba gun Nasrin, Tana zuwa tago Nasrin Na bawa Mufid Nono shi kuma yana sunsunar wuyanta "


Ummi tace "Yo naga Ikon Allah, ko Arba'in fa batayi ba"


Ya dosa kai Yace "Ummi nono fa kawai yake bashi"


Tace "Eh gaskiya kan nono take bashi, to kai kuma me ya kawo ka"


Shiru yayi ta kalli Nasrin tace "Ohon ki idan kika bari ya kara miki wani cikin danki bai girma ba mu ba ruwanmu"


Sabon Sarki Amar ya tsuke fuska yace "Haba Ummi dan ta kara yin wani ciki kuma shine me"

Ta kama baki tace "ehhh lalle Yarima yanzu rashin kunya zaka min"


Ya sunkuyar da kai yace "Yi hakuri Ummi"


Tace "Ina Umma Hauwa take"

Yace "nikan fa banma ganta ba"

Nasrin tace '' da yamma fa na ganta da Malam (wanda Yayi wa Yarima magani)

Ummi tace "Nima na gansu "


Sarki Amar yace "Kamar fa Malam da Umma sun kamu ne"


Nasrin ta harare shitace "Uwar tawa"


Ummi bata ce komai ba ta fita,tana zuwa kofar shiga sassanta taga Malam ya rako Umma zata shiga ciki...

          Written  by
UMMU MUFEED
      (Mrs Aliyu)

Wannan page din naki ne Matar Auwalu dan sanda, Kiyi yadda kike so shi, Allah ya kare miki Dan sandanki a duk inda yake, ina yinki irin totally din nan 💋💋💋💋



Sadidin Baba Allah ya bayar muku da Alheri ya tsayar haka



*Bismillahir Rahmanir Rahim*



Page 49

Tace "Muna ta nimanku ina kika shiga ne"


Umma tayi murmushi kawai ta shiga ciki, Ummi ma murmushi tayi dan ta gane komai,


Washe gari sukayi shirin tafiya, Kafin a kira Sallah kowa ya watse sai 'yan gida aka bari. 


Lokacin ne aka kawo Sumayya koda aka fada wa Nasrin isowan Sumayya tace a tsare mata ita, saida ta gama abin da take yi sannan ta nufi inda Sumayya take, Sumayya kuwa ta kara baki, duk ta rame ta fita hayyacinta kamar ba ita bace wannan gimbiya Sumayya me ji da kanta ba.


Nasrin ta dubi Sumayya tace "Gimbiya Sumayya kinsan abin da yasa nace a kawo ki nan"


Ta girgiza kai. 


Tace "Kin tuna Basma da kika kashe"


Ji tayi gaban ta ya fadi, tunawa tayi da lokacin da take fada wa Nasrin itace ta kashe basma, lalle da bata fada nata ba da asirin ta ya rufu, to waima me yasa tun tuni bata kashe ta ba ta huta, yanzu gashi zata hukunta ta,


Nasrin tace "Tunanin me kuke yi? Kina tunanin zan kashe ki ko, eh tabbas hukuncin kisa ya kamata a miki, amma ni bazan kashe ki ba, Saboda yaronki Tanimu "


Sumayya ta hadiye wani miyaw me kauri


Nasrin ta fita, bata dade da fita ba Sarki Amar ya shigo, tana ganin sa ya kara mata wani kwarjini da haiba, tana ganinsa tayi murmushi ta taso kamar zata shige jikinsa ya daga hannu tare da dakatar da ita, yace "Dakata malama "


Tace "Ina maka murnan kasancewanka Sarki "


Yace "nazo ne in duba matata ta fita ne"


Tace "Haba Amar bai kamata kana min haka ba, nifa matarka ce dan Allah kayi a komai ya wuce ina son in koma dakina zanyi hakuri da duk abin da zan gani"


Yace "Kinyi hauka wlh, ko mata sun kare a duniya yadda kike din nan kina tunanin zan iya zama dake ne, balle kuma ina tare da Nasrin, kina tunanin Nasrin matar da za'a hada ta da wata ne, to bari kiji a yanzu haka, ko baiwar Nasrin ta fiki daraja "


Ta kara matsowa ta durkusa kasa tace "Amar dan Allah kayi hakuri ka maidani wallahi ina son ka, Ina son... "

Shigowan Nasrin ne yasa tayi shiru da maganan, Nasrin kuwa ta jita, tuni ranta ya baci ta riko wuyan ta tace "Me kika ce, Mijina kike so, wato har yanzu baki saduda ba, ina jin haushin kashe min aminiya da kika yi yanzu so kike ki auri mijina"


Tace "shifa bai ce yana sona ba"


Tace "Dama ya za'ayi yace yana sonki, kuskurenki da kika ce kina son sa"


Saboda wuyan da yaji ne numfashinta na niman daukewa yasa tayi yunkurin mikewa tsaye, kasancewan tafi Nasrin karfe yasa ba tare da saninta ba ta tureta ta ta fada jikin Sarki Amar shi kuma ya rike ta, tashin da Nasrin zatayi ta shararawa Sumayya mari, ta kuma sharara mata, daga bisani ta ringa dukanta da hannu, Sarki Amar ya rike ta ya jawota yace "wannan ay sai ki gaji"

Ta juya tace da Wanda ta taho dashi, ka tafi da ita ku hadu da Sarkin Gishiri kuyi mata hukunci, idan nazo naga ba yadda nake so ba kasan da cewa kai zansa a maka"


Yace "An gama ranki ya dade"


Haka suka tafi da ita, sukaita dukanta da bulalan doki, saida suka farfasa mata jiki sannan Sarkin gishiri yazo ya zuba gishiri, ba abin da kake ji sai ihun ta,


Saida suka kai magrib suna dukanta da zuba mata gishiri, haka sukai tayi idan ta suma sai su antaya mata ruwa ta farfado,


Haka suka je suka sake kulleta sannan suka jefa mata abinci 


Sarki Amar kuwa baisan abin dake faruwa da Yamuna ba, shidai bai ganin dan uwansa sai da akayi sallahn isha ya ganshi ya fito daga masallaci da sauri ya tare shi yace "Lafiya kuwa dan uwa"


Marwan yace "Baka san abin dake faruwa da Umma ba"


Yace "ban sani ba Menene "


Yace "Umma fa ta haukace"


Sarki ya waro ido yace "Wlh ban sani ba muje in ganta"

Suka tafi, Yamuna na ganinsu kuwa ta hau ihu ita tana wurge-wurge tana cewa "Amar Saina kashe ka kaida Aisha, kuba dana sarautarshi"


Abin ya bawa Amar tsoro, tsayawa yayi kawai yana kallon ikon Allah,  ita kuwa sai sage wurgi take da kayan dake kusa da ita, ganin zata iya jiwa Sarki Amar ciwo yasa Marwan ya janyeshi suka fita,
Ya kulle mata dakin,


Washe gari da sassafe Marwan yaje duba ta saboda da tunaninta ya kwanta duk da mahaifiyarsa taki nadama, haka yake son kayarsa kuma yana tausayinta,


Yana bude kofar yaganta kwance tayi tsirara harshenta ya fito waje, yayi da ta taune da hakorinta, Idanuwanta kuwa sun gwalo waje, koda ya tabata yaji jikinta a sankare, yasa kunnensa a kirjinta yaji zuciyarta baya bugawa, yace "Innalillahi wa Inna'ilaihir raju'un, Umma banso kika mutu a wannan halinba naso ki shiryu, me zaki ce wa Ubangijinki idan ya tambaye ki"


Kuka ya fashe dashi ya tashi ya fita Sassan Sarki Amar yayi yayi ta bugawa da karfi, Amar kuwa lokacin ya gama shiryawa kenan Nasrin ta masa rawani zai fita fada yaji buga kofar, koda yaji yasan bana lafiya ba, tunda a kaida bafaden sa ne zai sanar dashi, da sauri ya je ya bude yaga Dan uwansa yana kuka, rumgume shi yayi yace "Menene" 


Cikin kuka marwan yace "Ummana ta rasu"

Sarki Amar yace "Innalillahi wa Inna ilaihir raju'un"

Wucewa yayi gaba Marwan ya bishi a baya yana kuka,


Kafin kace me, Masarautar Mula tasan da rasuwar Tsohuwar Sarauniya, gaba daya masarautar ba wanda ya furta kalmar arziki akanta, wasuma cewa suke, baza su yafe abin da ta musu ba. (Innalillahi wa inna ilaihir raju'un, kuji fa 'yan uwa, lallai ya kamata mu kula mu zauna da mutane lafiya, yanzu gashi ta mutu ba a hanya me kyauba, kuma mutane suna binta da mungun kalamai, Allah ya shiryar da zukatanmu, Amin ) 


Nan da nan Aka mata sutura aka kaita gidanta na gaskiya,


Nasrin na zaune wani imani ne ya shigeta, lallai dan Adam ba a bakin komai yake ba, dubi yadda Sarauniya take ji da kanta, amma tashi daya an maida ita wani iri kuma tazo ta mutu ba tare da tayi nadama ba,

Hawayene ya zubo mata ta tashi ta nufi inda aka tsare Sumayya ta sameta tana kwance duk jikinta ciwo 

Cikin sanyin jiki tace "Gimbiya Sarauniya Yamuna ta mutu "

Tace "To"

Nasrin tace "Yanzu fada miki da nayi baki ji wani abu ba"

Tace "Ni ba abinda naji, nidai dan Allah kiyi hakuri ki kyaleni in tafi garin mu"

Nasrin tace "Gimbiya Sumayya zan sake ki, kuma zan sa a baki danki ki tafi dashi, babansa zaina zuwa yana duba ku, idan ya girma sai a karbe shi, Dan Allah idan kinje garinku ki zauna da kowa lafiya, ki tuba Allah zai amshi ruban ki, ki kusanci Ubangijinki da Ibada zaiji tausayinki, ki kula da wannan yaro, Dan Allah kinji"


Ta daga kai alamun eh


Nasrin ta fita ba jimawa ta dawo da Tanimu a hannun ta, da yayi kiba dawowansa Mula. ta mika mata tace "Gashi, kici baga da bashi nono duk da yanzu nasan ba ruwa a nonon saboda an dade ba asha ba, amma idan kika bashi zaizo, ta amshe shi, Nasrin tasa aka mata amalanken doki tasa mata driver tace ya kaita har mula, Marwan yazo yayi ban kwana da Tanimu duk yaji ba dadi, baya son rabuwa dashi, haka Ummi ma bata so rabuwa dashi ba saboda sabo, 


Saida suka je daji, Sumayya ta kalli Tanimu tace "Saboda kakarka ta mutu har wancan shegiya Nasrin din tana min wani wa'azinta na banza, to ni an fada mata yanzu zan mutu ne, bazan mutu ba saina haifawa Sarki Amar yara, ita kuma da danta saina kashe su, kai kuma wato saboda kai da Babanka, Sarki Amar yaki maidani, To yau zan kasheka saboda in samu daman auren Amar,


Da sauri Idi me lura dokunan ya tsaya yace "Dan Allah kada ki masa komai ni ki bani shi, tunda bana haihuwa" 


Tace "Idan na baka, zai kasance jinin marwan yana nan gwara na kashe shi"


Shiko Tanimu ganin suna surutu yasa sai dariya yake dan a tunanin sa wasa suke masa, 

Idi ya sauko yazo ya durkusa yana rokonta tace "Shikenan zan baka Tanimu, amma saika kunce min doki daya ka bani in tafi dashi, kai kuma ka koma da daya,"


Murya na rawa yace "Wallahi na yadda"

Shi duk yayi hanne sabida kada ta cutar da yaron, in yaso idan yakai musu ya rike su su bashi, yana kunce dokin ya dawo yace "Na kunce"

Tace "Ban yadda ba saina hau dokin "


Yana rumgume da ita ta hau dokin saidai tana hawa tayi wurgi da Tanimu ya bugu da bishiya kansa ya fashe, yayi da ita kuma ta sakarwa dokin linzami ya arce da gudu,


Baibi ta kanta ba yayi gun Tanimu yaga ay ko motsi bayayi ya riga ya mutu, kuka ya fashe dashi, ya dauke shi ya hau dayan dokin ya juya mula dashi,


Yana zuwa fada ya sauka, Marwan na kusa da Sarki Amar yana bashi baki akan har yanzu yaki ya daina kuka, sai ya hango idi da Tanimu jikinsa duk jini, 


Da sauri ya tashi ya tarosu yace "Idi 'yan fashine suka tare ku"


Cikin kuka idi ya fada musu komai, 

Tsohon sarki ne ya amshi gawar Tanimun yana hawaye da tausayin sa


Ummi kuwa tanaji mutuwar tanimu kuka ta saka, dan tana jin shi sosai cikin zuciyarta



Ba a dau lokaci ba akayi jana'izarsa aka kaishi makomarsa,


Bayan sun dawo Marwan ya ringa kuka, Sarki Amar ya kalle shi yaji tausayin sa, dole yayi kuka ga mutuwar uwa gata Da, ga mutuwar Uwar ma yadda tazo masa...

Page 5⃣0⃣


Yace "Yayana Kayi hakuri, ka cire damuwa a ranka tunda Allah ya riga ya rubuta haka zai faru, nasan dole zaka ji zafi a zuciyarka to Yaya zamuyi dole hakuri zamuyi mu ci gaba da yi musu Addu'a"

Marwan yayi ajiyar zuciya yace "ba komai insha Allah zanyi yadda kace"

Nasrin kuwa da cewa tayi "ba komai ni bazan kara binta ba, Allah shi zai mata hukuncin abin da tayi,"


Saidai kuma Sarki Amar ya rubuta wa Sarkin Mula wasika abin da 'yarsa tayi.



Sumayya kuwa tana zuwa a mahaifiyarta kawai ta fadi abin da tayi, 


Cewa tayi kin min daidai, yanzu zan samu in je in sa boka ya ja hankalin Sabon sarki ya juyo gare ki, mu asirce matarsa ma ta dawo baiwarki kamar da"

Tace "Yawwa Mamata"


Lokacin da wasikar ta samu Sarki yana fada koda ya aka bashi sai ya ajiyeta, dan an fada masa wasikar cikin gidane,  ya karanta wasikar, ya aika a kira masa Sumayya tana zuwa yace "Sumayya yaushe zaki tuba kibi Allah ne, yanzu dan naki ma kashe shi kikayi saboda son duniya, me zaki samu a duniyar nan wanda kike cikin sa, kin kmulaqbasma wacce bata jiba bata gani yanzu dan cikin ki ma baki kyale ba, saboda kina son sarauta, me na raga miki a gidan nan Sumayya, saboda kada mutane su gane ki kice ke 'yar kwarkwara ce yasa na miki sasa a gidan daban, na fifitaki akan kowa, amma kikayi wannan aiki, Sumayya nayi nadamar haihuwarki, kin cuce ni, kuma kije zaki gani ba duniya bace kije indai duniya ce gatanan"


Shiru tayi tana sauraron sa, Cikin ranta kuwa tana kunkuni, yana yin shiru ta tashi zata fita kenan sai taci karo da mutum, Ammy ce a gaban ta tana mata mungun kallo, ta matsa ta gefen ta zata wuce Ammy ta sharara mata mari tace "Munafuka ina zaki je, dama ke kika kashe basma, me basma ta miki"

Ta juya ta kalli sarki tace "Ranka ya dad'e dama kasan wacce ta kashe min 'yata amma kaki fada min "


Yace "Nima sai a wajen nadin sarauta nake ji, Sarauniya Nasrin tana fada"


Ta juya ta kama Sumayya da duka har ta fama mata tabon da aka mata a mula, saida ta gaji ta koma gefe tana ajiyar zuciya' yan biyunta suka zo suka rumgume ta ta rumgume su, tace "ta Allah ba taki ba, yanzu gashi na samu wasu, "


Ta daga hannu sama tace "Ya Allah ka saka min abin da wannan baiwar taka tamin, ya Allah ka sakawa Baiwarka Basma"

Ta mike ta taja hannun 'yan biyunta batare da ta kara cewa komaiba, Sarki ma tashi yayi ya tafi fada abin sa, Suna tafiya ta mike da kyar tace "Wlh saina muku hukunci idan na sake auren Sabon Sarki"


Tafiya take a hankali saboda ciwon da aka ji mata ga gefen idonta da jini ya taru mata har bata kallo sosai, sai dishi-dishi ta isa baki kofar su kenan sai tayi gocewa matakala daya ta fadi, kasa wani ice ya cakke mata ido ayko idon ya ringa tsiyayan ruwa, ihu ta zuba Mama ta fito ta kamata suka shiga ciki har lokacin ruwa ne ke tsiyaya a idon.


Anyi bakwai din Yamuna washe gari akayi na Tanimu, A ranan kuma aka daura auren Umma da Malam, ranan kuma ta tare, sai aka fara maganan auren Marwan da Yusra, ba a dau lokaci me tsawo ba suma aka daura auren su, ba a wani yi wani biki ba, saboda shi Marwan kanma yanzu duniyar tsoro take bashi, ranan ua shiga dakin Amarya Yusra tana lullube cikin mayafi, ya hau kan gadon ya daga lullubin ya kalli kyakyawan fuskarta yace "Barka da zuwa Amarya"


Ta kauda kai cikin kunya

Yace "ya kamata muyi sallah dan mu godewa ubangijinmu"


Tace "to"


Tayo Alola shima yayi yazo suka gabatar da sallah raka'a biyu, sannan ya dauko musu madara me zafi da naman zabo, suka ci, 

Sannan ya taja suka tafi kan gado, inda yayi ta nuna mata irin kaunar da yake mata har burinsa ya cika, saidai me, bai samu Yusra cikekkiyar budurwa ba, koda ya tashi ya koma bakin gadon ya zauna yana tunanin matan daya amshi budurcinsu ta hanyar lalata, wasu fyade ya musu wasu kuma da hadin kansu, yau gashi wani ya lalata masa matar daya aura, yanzu suma mazan da suka auri wadancan haka suka ji, kwata-kwata baiji haushin Yusra ba.

Ita kuwa ganin haka yasa taje ta tsuguna tace "Kayi hakuri nasan damuwarka saboda baka same budurwa bace, amma kuma zan maka biyayya"


Yace "Fyade aka miki ko da son ranki ne. Ki fada min gaskiya


Ta sunkuyar dakai tace "Gaskiya ba kyade mane, shedan ne yaja ra'ayina nabawa wani waurayina kaina daga baya yace bazai aure ni ba Amma tunda ranan ba sake bawa wani ba"


Yace "Amma su Umman ku sun sani"

Tace "Aa"

Yace "Nima nayi miki alkawari ba wanda zai sani "

Kallon sa tayi cikin mamaki yace "Eh mu kwanta ki kara bani "

Bata kwanta ba sai sake kallon sa yake yi tana mamakin yaya akayi ya sauko cikin sauri da a tunanin ta zata sha wahala kafin ya sauko ila ma ya saketa, kamota Yayi ya hauda ita gadon ya rumgume ta suka fada duniyar ma'aurata.


Washe gari aka buga kofa yaje ya bude, wata mata ne ta gani, tace "ina amaryar"

yace  "tana kwance ba Lafiya"


Tace " daman zam dauki zanin gadonku ne za'a kai fada, dan gudanar da bikin budurci"


Yace "Ummi ce ta turo ki kowa"


Tace "Kanwar Ummankane"


Yace "Bana so to, wannan ay kuna son ku tona mana asiri ne nida matata, ni daga yauma zansa Sarki ya soke wannan Al'adar, Al'adar banza, ki fada mata na riga na wanke zanin gadon tun asuba, kuma ni yanzu ina kula da matana ne bata da lafiya"


Yana gama fada ya koma, Wunin ranan yaki fitowa kuma yaki bari a ganta dan kada a gane su.




Sumayya kuwa Idon ta daya ya makance a ciwukan jikinta sunki warkewa wani gun ma tsutsa ake samu a ciki, idan kaje gunta kuwa wari ne ke tashi, mamanta ne kawai ke zuwa gunta, saidai ita dinma abu kaman yayi mata maman zata fara mata masifa, sai Ammy da take dan lekata, amma duk da haka, ba alamar nadama a ranta, ita burinta ta warke ta nemi soyayyar Sabon Sarki,


Cikin dare ciwo yayi ta mata zogi, ga tsutsotsi da suke fitowa, Mama tana karkade mata da tsintsiyan zabori, gurin yayi ja, itako sai ihu take zuwa yanzu ba ciwon yake mata ciwo ba, ko ina a cikinta kamar ana fede mata fatan jikin ta, Da mama taga baza ta iya ba, sai kawai ta fita ta bugawa Sarki kofa tace yazo jikin Sumayya ba dadi, 

Da yaki zuwa sai Ammy ta rokeshi dan haka ya tashi, suka je tare, koda suka ganta Ammy ta matsa kusa da ita ta toshe hancin ta tace "Sumayya ki daina ihu kiyi salati"

Ay kuwa kamar ance tayi ihune sai kurma ihu take tana daguwa sama tana direwa kan gado, Ammy sai cewa takeyi "kiyi Salati", amma sai ihu take, cankuma idon ta ya gwalo waje tayi wani ihu hade da shakuwa me karfi. Sai rai yayi halinsa


Sarkine yace "Innalillahi wa inna ilaihir raju'un, ta rasu "


Ammy ta rufeta ta fito

Da safe aka mata sutura aka kaita makabarta


Sarki Amar da Sarauniya Nasrin kuwa Soyayyace suke ci ba kama hannun yaro, 


Yayin da Yusra da Marwan ma suke taka rawan gani wajen gudanar da tasu Soyayyar


Shekaran Mufid biyu ta sake haihuwa aka sa mata sunan Basma, 

Batayi Arba'inba Yusra ma ta haifi mace, aka sa sunan Nasrin,


Haka suka kasance cikin kwanciyar hankali da kaunar juna.



Alhamdulillah, Ayau Allah yayi na kawo karshen littafina me suna *Izzar Mulki* sai mun hadu a sabon littafi kuma
